================================================================================
FILE: scripts\dev\export_code.py
================================================================================

from pathlib import Path

# 配置导出选项
OUTPUT_FILE = "artifacts/dev/project_source_code.txt"
ROOT_DIR = "."

# 需要导出的文件后缀
INCLUDE_EXTENSIONS = {".py", ".toml", ".yaml", ".yml", ".md", ".json"}

# 需要忽略的目录（避免导出依赖库、缓存和隐藏文件夹）
EXCLUDE_DIRS = {
    ".venv",
    "venv",
    ".git",
    "__pycache__",
    ".pytest_cache",
    ".ruff_cache",
    ".vscode",
    ".idea",
    "build",
    "dist",
    "data",
    "docs",
    "项目规划",
    "README",
    "pyproject",
}


def is_valid_file(file_path: Path) -> bool:
    # 检查后缀名
    if file_path.suffix not in INCLUDE_EXTENSIONS:
        return False

    # 检查路径中是否包含被忽略的文件夹
    for parent in file_path.parents:
        if parent.name in EXCLUDE_DIRS:
            return False

    return True


def generate_export():
    root = Path(ROOT_DIR)

    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as outfile:
        # 遍历当前目录及所有子目录
        for file_path in root.rglob("*"):
            if file_path.is_file() and is_valid_file(file_path):
                try:
                    # 读取文件内容
                    content = file_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")

                    # 写入显眼的文件分割线和相对路径
                    relative_path = file_path.relative_to(root)
                    outfile.write(f"{'=' * 80}\n")
                    outfile.write(f"FILE: {relative_path}\n")
                    outfile.write(f"{'=' * 80}\n\n")

                    # 写入代码
                    outfile.write(content)
                    outfile.write("\n\n")

                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"Skipping {file_path}: {e}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("开始导出项目代码...")
    generate_export()
    print(f"导出完成！代码已保存至当前目录下的 {OUTPUT_FILE}")


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\dev\export_tree.py
================================================================================

from pathlib import Path

# 配置导出选项
OUTPUT_FILE = "artifacts/dev/project_directory_tree.txt"
ROOT_DIR = "."

# 需要忽略的目录（保持画面整洁）
EXCLUDE_DIRS = {
    ".venv",
    "venv",
    ".git",
    "__pycache__",
    ".pytest_cache",
    ".ruff_cache",
    ".vscode",
    ".idea",
    "build",
    "dist",
    ".mypy_cache",
}


def generate_tree(dir_path: Path, prefix: str, outfile):
    """
    递归生成树状结构
    """
    # 获取当前目录下的所有内容
    items = list(dir_path.iterdir())

    # 过滤掉黑名单中的文件/文件夹
    items = [item for item in items if item.name not in EXCLUDE_DIRS]

    # 排序：文件夹在前，文件在后，同类按名称字母排序
    items.sort(key=lambda x: (x.is_file(), x.name.lower()))

    count = len(items)
    for index, item in enumerate(items):
        is_last = index == count - 1
        # 连接符判断
        connector = "└── " if is_last else "├── "

        # 写入文件
        outfile.write(f"{prefix}{connector}{item.name}\n")

        # 如果是文件夹，继续往下一层递归
        if item.is_dir():
            extension = "    " if is_last else "│   "
            generate_tree(item, prefix + extension, outfile)


def export_tree():
    root = Path(ROOT_DIR)

    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as outfile:
        # 写入根目录名称
        outfile.write(f"{root.resolve().name}/\n")
        generate_tree(root, "", outfile)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("开始导出项目目录树...")
    export_tree()
    print(f"导出完成！目录树已保存至当前目录下的 {OUTPUT_FILE}")


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\dev\peek_corpus_chunks.py
================================================================================

import sys
from pathlib import Path

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.chunker import KnowledgeDocumentChunker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.loader import KnowledgeSourceLoader
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.normalizer import KnowledgeSourceNormalizer
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.parser import MarkdownKnowledgeParser


def peek_chunks():
    raw_root = Path("data/knowledge/raw_verified").resolve()
    if not raw_root.exists():
        print(f"找不到语料目录: {raw_root}")
        sys.exit(1)

    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(raw_root)
    normalizer = KnowledgeSourceNormalizer()
    parser = MarkdownKnowledgeParser()
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    try:
        sources = loader.load_all()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"语料加载失败: {e}")
        sys.exit(1)

    output_file = Path("corpus_chunk_dict.txt")

    with open(output_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write("=== GuanChen 语料 Chunk ID 字典 ===\n")
        f.write("使用方法：在本文档中 Ctrl+F 搜索你想作为正确答案的句子，即可找到上方的 chunk_id。\n\n")

        for raw_source in sources:
            norm_source = normalizer.normalize(raw_source)
            docs = parser.parse(norm_source)
            chunks = chunker.chunk_documents(docs)

            f.write(f"\n{'=' * 60}\n")
            f.write(f" 书名: {norm_source.source_title} ({norm_source.domain.value})\n")
            f.write(f"{'=' * 60}\n")

            for c in chunks:
                f.write(f" 章节: {c.section}\n")
                f.write(f" chunk_id: {c.chunk_id}\n")
                # 打印前 100 个字符方便辨认
                preview = c.text.replace("\n", " ")[:100]
                f.write(f" 预览: {preview}...\n")
                f.write("-" * 40 + "\n")

    print(f"透视完成！请打开项目根目录下的 {output_file.name} 挑选你的 Eval Ground Truth。")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    peek_chunks()


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\smoke\smoke_bazi_agent.py
================================================================================

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import ReadyForBazi
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime import BaziAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender


def run_smoke():
    settings = Settings()
    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(settings=settings)

    ready = ReadyForBazi(
        question="请从传统八字视角分析一下我的事业倾向。",
        profile=BirthProfile(year=2000, month=2, day=16, hour=7, minute=20, gender=Gender.MALE),
    )

    result = runtime.run(ready=ready)

    print(" Bazi Agent 业务结果")
    print("=" * 40)
    print(f"原问题: {result.question}")
    print(f"排盘底座日主: {result.chart.day_master.value}")
    print(f"\n大模型结构化命理解释\n{result.answer}")
    print("=" * 40)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_smoke()


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\smoke\smoke_dream_extractor.py
================================================================================

import asyncio
import json

from rich.console import Console

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import merge_dream_record, start_new_dream
from guanchen_v2.dream.extractor import DreamExtractor
from guanchen_v2.llm.provider import build_chat_model

console = Console()


async def run_smoke_test():
    settings = Settings()
    base_model = build_chat_model(settings=settings)
    extractor = DreamExtractor(model=base_model)

    record = start_new_dream()

    cases = [
        {"name": "Case A - 首次描述", "text": "我梦到自己回到小学，在走廊里一直找不到出口，梦里特别害怕。"},
        {"name": "Case B - 补充人物 (Delta Extraction)", "text": "后来想起来，我妈妈也在那条走廊里。"},
        {"name": "Case C - 明确 Correction", "text": "我刚才说错了，不是妈妈，是以前的班主任。"},
        {
            "name": "Case D - Reality Background",
            "text": "最近工作项目快到截止日期了，而且我确实觉得压力很大。",
        },
    ]

    for i, case in enumerate(cases):
        console.rule(f"[bold blue]执行 {case['name']}")
        console.print(f"[bold]用户输入:[/bold] {case['text']}\n")

        # 1. 抽取 Patch
        try:
            patch = await extractor.extract(user_text=case["text"], current_record=record)
        except Exception as e:
            console.print(f"[bold red]抽取失败: {e}[/bold red]")
            break

        console.print("[bold green]>>> LLM 生成的 DreamPatch:[/bold green]")
        console.print(patch.model_dump_json(indent=2, exclude_unset=True))

        # 2. 合并 State
        record = merge_dream_record(record=record, patch=patch, raw_description_fragment=case["text"])

        console.print("\n[bold yellow]>>> Merge 后的当前有效状态 (Dream Elements & Background):[/bold yellow]")
        state_dump = {
            "elements": record.elements.model_dump(exclude_defaults=True),
            "reality_background": record.reality_background.model_dump(exclude_defaults=True),
        }
        console.print(json.dumps(state_dump, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
        print("\n" + "=" * 60 + "\n")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(run_smoke_test())


================================================================================
FILE: scripts\smoke\smoke_llm.py

import logging

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.core.logging import configure_logging
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.llm.exceptions import LLMError
from guanchen_v2.prompts.smoke import TEXT_SMOKE_PROMPT, build_structured_smoke_prompt

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class StructuredSmokeResult(BaseModel):
    """
    仅用于 Stage 1 Smoke 测试的契约，不属于业务 Domain Model。
    明确禁止 LLM 生成 schema 之外的冗余字段。
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    project_name: str = Field(min_length=1)
    agent_names: list[str] = Field(min_length=4, max_length=4)


def run_smoke(client: LLMClient) -> None:
    text_result = client.text(TEXT_SMOKE_PROMPT)
    assert text_result.strip(), "文本模型返回了空字符串"
    logger.info("文本生成执行成功。")
    print(text_result)

    structured_prompt = build_structured_smoke_prompt()
    structured_result = client.structured(structured_prompt, StructuredSmokeResult)
    logger.info("结构化输出执行成功。")
    print(structured_result.model_dump_json(indent=2))


def main():
    settings = Settings()
    configure_logging(settings.log_level)
    client = LLMClient.from_settings(settings=settings)
    try:
        run_smoke(client=client)
    except LLMError:
        logger.exception("LLM 基础设施调用失败")
        raise
    except Exception:
        logger.exception("发生未知的异常")
        raise


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.rerank_adapter import BgeRerankerAdapter

pytestmark = pytest.mark.smoke


def test_real_model_smoke_adapter():
    """
    这只是个证明模型能下载、能启动、能生成分数的冒烟测试，不管排序质量好坏。
    只有当你显式执行 pytest -m smoke 时，它才会去下载上 GB 的模型。
    """
    adapter = BgeRerankerAdapter()

    try:
        # 给模型送过去两套文本对
        pairs = [
            ("八字里的月令是什么？", "月令为提纲，不可轻忽。\n\n这正是在说八字的重点"),
            ("八字里的月令是什么？", "今天去吃了红烧肉，非常美味。"),
        ]

        scores = adapter.score_pairs(pairs)

        # 证明返回结果是干净、无污染、无越界的纯浮点数
        assert len(scores) == 2
        assert isinstance(scores[0], float)
        assert isinstance(scores[1], float)

    except Exception as e:
        pytest.fail(f"真实大模型冒烟测试失败：{e}")

from dataclasses import dataclass

from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import ConversationState
from guanchen_v2.memory.store import MemoryStore
from guanchen_v2.router.core import TwoLayerIntentRouter
from guanchen_v2.router.dispatch import SingleAgentDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_dispatch import DualDomainDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesizer import AgentSynthesizer


@dataclass
class ConversationContext:
    """
    Application 执行时挂载的 Runtime Context。
    只在单次 Request 内存生命周期内存在，连接持久化状态和底层依赖容器。
    """
    state: ConversationState
    memory_store: MemoryStore
    router: TwoLayerIntentRouter
    single_dispatcher: SingleAgentDispatcher
    dual_dispatcher: DualDomainDispatcher
    synthesizer: AgentSynthesizer

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError


class ApplicationError(ProjectError):
    """Application Layer 核心异常基类"""
    pass

class SessionInitializationError(ApplicationError):
    """Session 创建或加载时发生的严重错误"""
    pass

class ConversationExecutionError(ApplicationError):
    """Orchestrator 执行生命周期（Router, Dispatcher, Synthesizer 等）发生的错误"""
    pass

class StateUpdateError(ApplicationError):
    """ConversationState 更新或合并结果时发生的错误"""
    pass



================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\application\orchestrator.py
================================================================================

import json
import uuid

from guanchen_v2.application.exceptions import (
    ConversationExecutionError,
    SessionInitializationError,
    StateUpdateError,
)
from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import ConversationState, MessageState
from guanchen_v2.memory.selector import MemorySelector
from guanchen_v2.memory.store import MemoryStore
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.core import TwoLayerIntentRouter
from guanchen_v2.router.dispatch import SingleAgentDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_dispatch import AgentExecutionResult, DualDomainDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_requirements import check_dual_requirements
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext, RequirementStatus, check_requirements
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesis import SynthesisInput, SynthesizedResponse
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesizer import AgentSynthesizer


class ConversationOrchestrator:
    def __init__(
        self,
        memory_store: MemoryStore,
        router: TwoLayerIntentRouter,
        single_dispatcher: SingleAgentDispatcher,
        dual_dispatcher: DualDomainDispatcher,
        synthesizer: AgentSynthesizer,
        memory_selector: MemorySelector | None = None,
    ):
        self._memory_store = memory_store
        self._router = router
        self._single_dispatcher = single_dispatcher
        self._dual_dispatcher = dual_dispatcher
        self._synthesizer = synthesizer
        self._memory_selector = memory_selector

    async def execute(self, session_id: str | None, user_query: str) -> SynthesizedResponse:
        # 1. Session Lifecycle Initialization
        try:
            if session_id:
                state = await self._memory_store.load(session_id)
                if not state:
                    # 不存在则默认自然创建，保证用户体验
                    state = ConversationState(session_id=session_id)
                    await self._memory_store.save(state)
            else:
                session_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
                state = ConversationState(session_id=session_id)
                await self._memory_store.save(state)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise SessionInitializationError(f"Session {session_id} 初始化失败。") from exc

        try:
            # 2. Append User Message
            state.current_query = user_query
            state.messages.append(MessageState(role="user", content=user_query))

            # 3. Routing
            routing_result = await self._router.route(user_query)
            state.routing_result = routing_result

            # 4. Requirement Check
            # Stage 8：通过 Memory Selector 按领域注入长期记忆（只注入当前领域命名空间），
            # 不把全历史无脑塞给模型。
            if self._memory_selector is not None:
                await self._memory_selector.ensure_user(session_id)
                req_context = await self._memory_selector.select_for_intent(
                    routing_result.primary_intent,
                    session_id,
                )
            else:
                req_context = RequirementContext(available_data={})

            if routing_result.secondary_intents:
                req_result = check_dual_requirements(routing_result, req_context)
            else:
                req_result = check_requirements(routing_result, req_context)
            
            state.requirement_result = req_result

            # 5. Dispatching 调度
            if req_result.status in {RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT, RequirementStatus.BYPASS}:
                results = (AgentExecutionResult(intent=routing_result.primary_intent, output=req_result),)
            else:
                if routing_result.secondary_intents:
                    primary_state = getattr(state.agent_states, routing_result.primary_intent.value, {})
                    secondary_state = getattr(state.agent_states, routing_result.secondary_intents[0].value, {})
                    results = await self._dual_dispatcher.dispatch(
                        routing_result, user_query, req_context, primary_state, secondary_state
                    )
                else:
                    agent_state = getattr(state.agent_states, routing_result.primary_intent.value, {})
                    out = await self._single_dispatcher.dispatch(
                        routing_result, req_result, user_query, req_context, agent_state
                    )
                    results = (AgentExecutionResult(intent=routing_result.primary_intent, output=out),)

        except Exception as exc:
            raise ConversationExecutionError("Orchestrator 调度执行期间发生系统错误。") from exc

        # 7. Memory Write（仅写入用户明确提供 / 确认的结构化资料）
        # 八字执行成功后，将本次使用的出生资料持久化为长期记忆，
        # 使后续轮次 / 重启后无需重复询问。
        if (
            self._memory_selector is not None
            and routing_result.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
        ):
            profile = req_context.available_data.get(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE)
            if profile is not None:
                await self._memory_selector.persist_birth_profile(session_id, profile)

        # 8. Update Agent States (在不知道领域结构的前提下防御性注入)
        try:
            self._update_agent_states(state, results)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise StateUpdateError("更新 Agent State 时发生数据错误。") from exc

        # 9. Synthesis
        try:
            synth_input = SynthesisInput(query=user_query, results=results)
            synth_response = await self._synthesizer.synthesize(synth_input)

            # 10. Append Assistant Message
            state.messages.append(MessageState(role="assistant", content=synth_response.content))
        except Exception as exc:
            raise ConversationExecutionError("Synthesizer 合并响应执行失败。") from exc

        # 11. Persistence
        try:
            await self._memory_store.update(state)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise StateUpdateError("最终持久化 ConversationState 失败。") from exc

        return synth_response

    def _update_agent_states(self, state: ConversationState, results: tuple[AgentExecutionResult, ...]) -> None:
        """安全更新 Agent State，不探查 Agent 内部字段，不违反 Runtime Object Rule"""
        for res in results:
            intent_val = res.intent.value
            agent_dict = getattr(state.agent_states, intent_val, None)
            
            if agent_dict is not None:
                try:
                    if hasattr(res.output, "model_dump_json"):
                        agent_dict["latest_output"] = json.loads(res.output.model_dump_json())
                    elif isinstance(res.output, dict):
                        agent_dict["latest_output"] = json.loads(json.dumps(res.output))
                    else:
                        agent_dict["latest_output"] = str(res.output)
                except Exception:
                    agent_dict["latest_output"] = "Unserializable output representation"



================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\bazi\adapter.py
================================================================================

from datetime import datetime
from importlib.metadata import version

from lunar_python import Solar

from guanchen_v2.bazi.exceptions import BaziAdapterError
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import (
    BaziCalculationMethod,
    BaziChart,
    BirthProfile,
    DaYunPeriod,
    DaYunResult,
    EarthlyBranch,
    GanZhi,
    Gender,
    HeavenlyStem,
    HiddenStem,
    LuckDirection,
    LuckStartOffset,
    Pillar,
    TenGod,
)


class LunarPythonBaziAdapter:
    """lunar_python 官方排盘实现与 GuanChen 领域模型的防腐层映射器。"""

    def _get_library_version(self) -> str:
        return version("lunar_python")

    def _parse_gan_zhi(self, gz_str: str) -> GanZhi:
        if not gz_str or len(gz_str) != 2:
            raise ValueError(f"非法的干支字符串: '{gz_str}'")
        return GanZhi(
            stem=HeavenlyStem(gz_str[0]),
            branch=EarthlyBranch(gz_str[1]),
        )

    def _build_pillar(
        self,
        gan: str,
        zhi: str,
        stem_ten_god_str: str | None,
        hide_gans: list[str],
        hide_ten_god: list[str],
    ) -> Pillar:
        gz = GanZhi(stem=HeavenlyStem(gan), branch=EarthlyBranch(zhi))
        stem_ten_god = TenGod(stem_ten_god_str) if stem_ten_god_str else None

        hidden_stem = []
        # 使用 strict=True 确保藏干与十神数量严格一致，防止库隐式 API 漂移
        for hg, htg in zip(hide_gans, hide_ten_god, strict=True):
            hidden_stem.append(HiddenStem(stem=HeavenlyStem(hg), ten_god=TenGod(htg)))

        return Pillar(
            gan_zhi=gz,
            stem_ten_god=stem_ten_god,
            hidden_stems=hidden_stem,
        )

    def calculate(self, profile: BirthProfile) -> BaziChart:
        try:
            solar = Solar.fromYmdHms(profile.year, profile.month, profile.day, profile.hour, profile.minute, 0)
            lunar = solar.getLunar()
            eight_char = lunar.getEightChar()

            # 锁定晚子时配置
            eight_char.setSect(2)

            year_pillar = self._build_pillar(
                eight_char.getYearGan(),
                eight_char.getYearZhi(),
                eight_char.getYearShiShenGan(),
                eight_char.getYearHideGan(),
                eight_char.getYearShiShenZhi(),
            )

            month_pillar = self._build_pillar(
                eight_char.getMonthGan(),
                eight_char.getMonthZhi(),
                eight_char.getMonthShiShenGan(),
                eight_char.getMonthHideGan(),
                eight_char.getMonthShiShenZhi(),
            )

            day_pillar = self._build_pillar(
                eight_char.getDayGan(),
                eight_char.getDayZhi(),
                None,  # 明确规定日主不暴露十神关系
                eight_char.getDayHideGan(),
                eight_char.getDayShiShenZhi(),
            )

            hour_pillar = self._build_pillar(
                eight_char.getTimeGan(),
                eight_char.getTimeZhi(),
                eight_char.getTimeShiShenGan(),
                eight_char.getTimeHideGan(),
                eight_char.getTimeShiShenZhi(),
            )

            # 锁定性别映射与起运算法配置 (sect=1)
            gender_int = 1 if profile.gender == Gender.MALE else 0
            yun = eight_char.getYun(gender=gender_int, sect=1)

            start_solar = yun.getStartSolar()
            start_at = datetime(
                start_solar.getYear(),
                start_solar.getMonth(),
                start_solar.getDay(),
                start_solar.getHour(),
                start_solar.getMinute(),
                start_solar.getSecond(),
            )

            da_yuns = yun.getDaYun()
            periods = []
            for dy in da_yuns:
                # 过滤掉库实现细节中的 index=0 预备期
                if dy.getIndex() == 0:
                    continue
                periods.append(
                    DaYunPeriod(
                        index=dy.getIndex(),
                        start_year=dy.getStartYear(),
                        start_age=dy.getStartAge(),
                        gan_zhi=self._parse_gan_zhi(dy.getGanZhi()),
                    )
                )

            da_yun_result = DaYunResult(
                direction=LuckDirection.FORWARD if yun.isForward() else LuckDirection.BACKWARD,
                start_offset=LuckStartOffset(
                    years=yun.getStartYear(),
                    months=yun.getStartMonth(),
                    days=yun.getStartDay(),
                    hours=yun.getStartHour(),
                ),
                start_at=start_at,
                periods=periods,
            )

            method = BaziCalculationMethod(
                library_version=self._get_library_version(),
                late_zi_sect=2,
                yun_sect=1,
            )

            return BaziChart(
                year_pillar=year_pillar,
                month_pillar=month_pillar,
                day_pillar=day_pillar,
                hour_pillar=hour_pillar,
                da_yun=da_yun_result,
                calculation_method=method,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise BaziAdapterError("lunar_python 排盘或字段映射失败。") from exc

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError


class BaziError(ProjectError):
    """
    GuanChen Bazi 子系统异常根类型。
    """

    pass


class BaziAdapterError(BaziError):
    """
    八字第三方排盘适配器异常。

    当 lunar_python 计算失败，
    或其返回结果无法满足 GuanChen Bazi Domain Contract
    时抛出。
    """

    pass


class BaziCalculationError(BaziError):
    """
    GuanChen 八字确定性排盘业务能力执行失败。

    由 Service 层屏蔽底层 Adapter 实现细节后抛出。
    """

    pass


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\bazi\service.py
================================================================================

from guanchen_v2.bazi.adapter import LunarPythonBaziAdapter
from guanchen_v2.bazi.exceptions import BaziAdapterError, BaziCalculationError
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BaziChart, BirthProfile


class BaziService:
    """八字排盘业务层，封装底层实现，对外提供稳定的业务能力。"""

    def __init__(self, adapter: LunarPythonBaziAdapter) -> None:
        self._adapter = adapter

    def calculate(self, profile: BirthProfile) -> BaziChart:
        """执行确定性排盘。发生异常时不进行重试，并封装为纯业务异常抛出。"""
        try:
            return self._adapter.calculate(profile=profile)
        except BaziAdapterError as exc:
            raise BaziCalculationError("八字排盘业务执行失败。") from exc

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\config\settings.py

from typing import Literal

from pydantic import Field, SecretStr
from pydantic_settings import (
    BaseSettings,
    SettingsConfigDict,
)

LogLevel = Literal[
    "DEBUG",
    "INFO",
    "WARNING",
    "ERROR",
    "CRITICAL",
]


class DatabaseSettings(BaseSettings):
    """
    GuanChen 数据库独立配置。

    该配置可以在完全没有 LLM credentials 的情况下独立创建，
    供：
    - ingestion scripts
    - retrieval scripts
    - database maintenance
    - integration tests
    使用。
    """

    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
        env_file=".env",
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
        env_prefix="GUANCHEN_",
        extra="ignore",
    )

    database_url: str = Field(
        default=("postgresql+asyncpg://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/guanchen"),
        min_length=1,
    )


class Settings(DatabaseSettings):
    """
    GuanChen 完整应用配置。

    Application Runtime 需要 LLM 配置；
    纯数据库基础设施不应依赖该类型。
    """

    app_env: str = "development"

    log_level: LogLevel = "INFO"

    deepseek_api_key: SecretStr = Field(min_length=1)

    deepseek_model: str = Field(min_length=1)

    llm_temperature: float = Field(
        default=0.2,
        ge=0.0,
        le=2.0,
    )

    llm_timeout_seconds: float = Field(
        default=30.0,
        gt=0.0,
    )

    llm_max_retries: int = Field(
        default=2,
        ge=0,
        le=5,
    )


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\core\agent_contract.py
================================================================================

from __future__ import annotations

from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import Citation


class AgentExecutionStatus(StrEnum):
    """
    Agent 执行状态。

    用于 Router / Orchestrator 判断后续行为。
    """

    SUCCESS = "success"

    NEEDS_INPUT = "needs_input"

    FAILED = "failed"


class AgentResponse(BaseModel):
    """
    GuanChen v2 统一 Agent 输出 Contract。

    所有领域 Agent 最终必须转换为该结构。

    注意：
    domain_payload 保存领域专属结果。
    Router / Application 不应该解析其中业务字段。
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(
        extra="forbid",
        arbitrary_types_allowed=True,
    )

    status: AgentExecutionStatus = Field(
        default=AgentExecutionStatus.SUCCESS
    )

    answer: str = Field(
        description="最终面向用户的文本结果"
    )

    citations: tuple[Citation, ...] = Field(
        default_factory=tuple,
        description="统一引用信息"
    )

    domain: str = Field(
        description="Agent 所属领域，例如 bazi/dream/liuyao"
    )

    domain_payload: dict[str, Any] = Field(
        default_factory=dict,
        description="领域专属结构化结果"
    )

    metadata: dict[str, Any] = Field(
        default_factory=dict,
        description="非业务控制信息，例如 trace/session 信息"
    )

================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\core\errors.py
================================================================================

class ProjectError(RuntimeError):
    """
    GuanChen 所有稳定项目级异常的共同根类型。

    该类型只提供统一异常边界，不承载：
    - error code
    - HTTP status
    - logging policy
    - retry policy
    - user-facing message mapping

    各子系统继续使用自己的异常层级表达具体语义。
    """

    pass

class SessionStateError(ProjectError):
    """
    Application Session State 异常。
    
    处理：
    - invalid state
    - serialization failure
    - validation failure
    
    不归属：
    - AgentError
    - DatabaseError
    """
    pass



================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\core\logging.py
================================================================================

import logging


def configure_logging(level: str) -> None:
    """配置全局基础日志系统。仅在应用入口点（如 CLI、脚本启动或 FastAPI 启动时）调用。"""
    numeric_level = getattr(logging, level)

    logging.basicConfig(level=numeric_level, format="%(asctime)s | %(levelname)s | %(name)s | %(message)s")

import re

from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import merge_dream_record, start_new_dream
from guanchen_v2.dream.exceptions import DreamMergeConflictError
from guanchen_v2.dream.extractor import DreamExtractor
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamContextResult,
    DreamContextStatus,
    DreamPatch,
    DreamRecord,
    DreamTurnBoundary,
)

_EXPLICIT_SAME_PATTERNS = (
    r"不是(另一个|另外一个|新的?)梦",
    r"(还是|就是|继续)刚才(那个)?梦",
    r"同一个梦",
    r"刚才(那个)?梦的补充",
)

_EXPLICIT_NEW_PATTERNS = (
    r"换一个梦",
    r"我还有(一个梦|另一个梦|另外一个梦)",
    r"这是(我(的)?)?第二个梦",
    r"再说一个(新梦|新的梦|梦)",
    r"(还|又)做了(一个梦|另一个梦|另外一个梦)",
    r"(另一个|另外一个)梦[是里中：:，,]",
)

_AMBIGUOUS_PATTERNS = (
    r"又梦到",
    r"又梦见",
    r"还梦到",
    r"还梦见",
)


def classify_dream_boundary(user_text: str, has_current_dream: bool) -> DreamTurnBoundary:
    """分层优先级的梦境边界判别，保守策略：优先识别'同一梦境'声明。"""
    if not has_current_dream:
        return DreamTurnBoundary.NEW

    text = user_text.strip()

    if any(re.search(pattern, text) for pattern in _EXPLICIT_SAME_PATTERNS):
        return DreamTurnBoundary.SAME

    if any(re.search(pattern, text) for pattern in _EXPLICIT_NEW_PATTERNS):
        return DreamTurnBoundary.NEW

    if any(re.search(pattern, text) for pattern in _AMBIGUOUS_PATTERNS):
        return DreamTurnBoundary.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION

    return DreamTurnBoundary.SAME


def patch_contains_dream_content(patch: DreamPatch) -> bool:
    """判断 Patch 中是否包含有效的梦境内元素更新。现实背景不算。"""
    adds = patch.element_additions
    if any(
        (
            adds.people,
            adds.scenes,
            adds.objects,
            adds.actions,
            adds.emotions,
            adds.anomalies,
            adds.subjective_feelings,
        )
    ):
        return True

    for correction in patch.corrections:
        if correction.field.value.startswith("elements."):
            return True

    return False


def has_dream_content(record: DreamRecord) -> bool:
    """判断一个梦境记录是否真的记录了梦境事件，而不仅仅是现实背景。"""
    e = record.elements
    return any((e.people, e.scenes, e.objects, e.actions, e.emotions, e.anomalies, e.subjective_feelings))


class DreamContextService:
    def __init__(self, extractor: DreamExtractor):
        self._extractor = extractor

    async def apply_turn(
        self,
        user_text: str,
        current_record: DreamRecord | None,
    ) -> DreamContextResult:
        boundary = classify_dream_boundary(user_text, current_record is not None)

        if boundary == DreamTurnBoundary.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION:
            return DreamContextResult(
                status=DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
                record=current_record,
                clarification_question="这是刚才那个梦的补充，还是一个新的梦？",
                started_new_dream=False,
            )

        if boundary == DreamTurnBoundary.NEW:
            base_record = start_new_dream()
            started_new_dream = True
        else:
            base_record = current_record
            started_new_dream = False

        patch = await self._extractor.extract(user_text, current_record=base_record)

        try:
            # 只有当该轮有真实的梦境更新时，才将原文记录为历史
            raw_frag = user_text if patch_contains_dream_content(patch) else None
            new_record = merge_dream_record(base_record, patch, raw_description_fragment=raw_frag)
        except DreamMergeConflictError:
            return DreamContextResult(
                status=DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
                record=base_record,
                patch=patch,
                clarification_question="你是在更正刚才梦里的哪一项信息？请说明原来的内容和正确内容。",
                started_new_dream=started_new_dream,
            )

        if not has_dream_content(new_record):
            return DreamContextResult(
                status=DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
                record=new_record,
                patch=patch,
                clarification_question="请补充一下梦里实际发生了什么，哪怕只描述一两个最鲜明的片段也可以。",
                started_new_dream=started_new_dream,
            )

        return DreamContextResult(
            status=DreamContextStatus.UPDATED,
            record=new_record,
            patch=patch,
            started_new_dream=started_new_dream,
        )


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\dream\dream_merge.py
================================================================================

from guanchen_v2.dream.exceptions import DreamMergeConflictError
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamElements,
    DreamInterpretationState,
    DreamPatch,
    DreamRecord,
    InterpretationStatus,
    RealityBackground,
)


def _normalize(text: str) -> str:
    """轻量级规范化，用于稳定的去重和冲突检测"""
    return " ".join(text.split()).casefold()


def _stable_dedupe(items: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    """在保持顺序和原始大小写不变的情况下，对列表进行去重"""
    seen = set()
    result = []
    for item in items:
        norm = _normalize(item)
        if norm not in seen:
            seen.add(norm)
            result.append(item)
    return result


def merge_dream_record(
    record: DreamRecord,
    patch: DreamPatch,
    *,
    raw_description_fragment: str | None = None,
) -> DreamRecord:

    new_elements = record.elements.model_dump()
    new_bg = record.reality_background.model_dump()
    new_raw = list(record.raw_descriptions)

    for correction in patch.corrections:
        domain_name, field_name = correction.field.value.split(".")
        target_dict = new_elements if domain_name == "elements" else new_bg

        target_list = target_dict[field_name]
        norm_old = _normalize(correction.old_value)

        found_idx = -1
        for i, item in enumerate(target_list):
            if _normalize(item) == norm_old:
                found_idx = i
                break

        if found_idx == -1:
            raise DreamMergeConflictError("当前梦境记录中不存在待修正目标")

        if correction.new_value is None:
            target_list.pop(found_idx)
        else:
            target_list[found_idx] = correction.new_value

    for field in new_elements.keys():
        adds = getattr(patch.element_additions, field)
        new_elements[field] = _stable_dedupe(new_elements[field] + adds)

    for field in new_bg.keys():
        adds = getattr(patch.background_additions, field)
        new_bg[field] = _stable_dedupe(new_bg[field] + adds)

    if raw_description_fragment:
        norm_frag = _normalize(raw_description_fragment)
        if norm_frag and not any(_normalize(existing) == norm_frag for existing in new_raw):
            new_raw.append(raw_description_fragment)

    updated_elements = DreamElements(**new_elements)
    updated_bg = RealityBackground(**new_bg)

    elements_changed = record.elements != updated_elements
    bg_changed = record.reality_background != updated_bg
    raw_changed = record.raw_descriptions != new_raw

    new_revision = record.revision
    new_status = record.interpretation_state.status

    if elements_changed or bg_changed or raw_changed:
        new_revision += 1
        if new_status == InterpretationStatus.CURRENT:
            new_status = InterpretationStatus.STALE

    return DreamRecord(
        dream_id=record.dream_id,
        raw_descriptions=new_raw,
        elements=updated_elements,
        reality_background=updated_bg,
        interpretation_state=DreamInterpretationState(
            status=new_status, last_interpreted_revision=record.interpretation_state.last_interpreted_revision
        ),
        revision=new_revision,
    )


def start_new_dream() -> DreamRecord:
    """返回一个完全新的、状态隔离的DreamRecord"""
    return DreamRecord()


def mark_interpreted(record: DreamRecord) -> DreamRecord:
    """助手将记录解释状态转换为当前"""
    new_record = record.model_copy(deep=True)
    new_record.interpretation_state.status = InterpretationStatus.CURRENT
    new_record.interpretation_state.last_interpreted_revision = new_record.revision
    return new_record

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError


class DreamError(ProjectError):
    """
    GuanChen Dream 子系统异常根类型。
    """

    pass


class DreamMergeConflictError(DreamError):
    """
    DreamRecord merge 过程中出现不可满足的状态冲突。
    """

    pass


class DreamExtractionError(DreamError):
    """
    梦境结构化增量信息抽取失败。
    """

    pass


class DreamInterpretationError(DreamError):
    """
    梦境解释生成或证据契约验证失败。
    """

    pass


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\dream\extractor.py
================================================================================

import json

from guanchen_v2.dream.exceptions import DreamExtractionError
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.llm.exceptions import LLMError
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamElements,
    DreamPatch,
    DreamRecord,
    RealityBackground,
)
from guanchen_v2.prompts.dream_agent import (
    DREAM_EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    DREAM_EXTRACTION_USER_TEMPLATE,
)


class DreamExtractor:
    """
    Dream Agent 的结构化增量信息抽取器。

    职责：
    - 只抽取用户本轮明确提供的 DreamPatch；
    - 只向 LLM 暴露当前有效 Dream 状态；
    - 不负责状态 merge；
    - 不负责梦境解释；
    - 所有 LLM 调用统一通过 LLMClient。
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        llm_client: LLMClient,
    ) -> None:
        self._llm_client = llm_client

    def _build_context(
        self,
        current_record: DreamRecord | None,
    ) -> str:
        """
        构建抽取任务所需的最小当前状态。

        不传递：
        - raw_descriptions
        - interpretation_state
        - revision
        - 其他历史运行时信息
        """
        if current_record is None:
            elements = DreamElements()
            reality_background = RealityBackground()
        else:
            elements = current_record.elements
            reality_background = current_record.reality_background

        context = {
            "elements": elements.model_dump(),
            "reality_background": (reality_background.model_dump()),
        }

        return json.dumps(
            context,
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )

    async def extract(
        self,
        user_text: str,
        current_record: DreamRecord | None = None,
    ) -> DreamPatch:
        if not user_text.strip():
            raise DreamExtractionError("梦境信息抽取输入不能为空")

        current_context = self._build_context(current_record=current_record)

        user_prompt = DREAM_EXTRACTION_USER_TEMPLATE.format(
            current_context=current_context,
            user_text=user_text,
        )

        try:
            result = await self._llm_client.generate_structured(
                system_prompt=(DREAM_EXTRACTION_SYSTEM_PROMPT),
                user_prompt=user_prompt,
                schema=DreamPatch,
            )
        except LLMError as exc:
            raise DreamExtractionError("梦境信息结构化抽取失败") from exc

        if not isinstance(
            result,
            DreamPatch,
        ):
            raise DreamExtractionError("模型未返回合法的 DreamPatch 结构化输出")

        return result


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\dream\retrieval.py
================================================================================

from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalFilter,
    RetrievalResult,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import RetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamRecord,
    DreamRetrievalAspect,
    DreamRetrievalQuery,
    NonBlankText,
)

DREAM_RETRIEVAL_FILTER = RetrievalFilter(domains=frozenset({KnowledgeDomain.DREAM}))


class DreamRetrievalEvidence(BaseModel):
    """一个 Dream retrieval aspect 对应的检索结果集合。"""

    aspect: DreamRetrievalAspect
    query: NonBlankText
    results: list[RetrievalResult] = Field(default_factory=list)


def _join_values(values: list[str]) -> str:
    return "、".join(values)


def build_dream_retrieval_queries(
    record: DreamRecord,
) -> list[DreamRetrievalQuery]:
    """
    将当前 DreamRecord 转换为确定性的 Dream retrieval queries。

    只使用已经结构化确认的梦境状态，
    不使用 raw_descriptions、revision 或模型自行推断的信息。
    """
    queries: list[DreamRetrievalQuery] = []

    elements = record.elements
    background = record.reality_background

    symbol_parts: list[str] = []

    if elements.people:
        symbol_parts.append(f"人物：{_join_values(elements.people)}")

    if elements.scenes:
        symbol_parts.append(f"场景：{_join_values(elements.scenes)}")

    if elements.objects:
        symbol_parts.append(f"对象：{_join_values(elements.objects)}")

    if elements.actions:
        symbol_parts.append(f"行为：{_join_values(elements.actions)}")

    if elements.anomalies:
        symbol_parts.append(f"异常现象：{_join_values(elements.anomalies)}")

    if symbol_parts:
        queries.append(
            DreamRetrievalQuery(
                aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.SYMBOL,
                query=(f"梦境传统象征 {'；'.join(symbol_parts)}"),
            )
        )

    emotion_parts: list[str] = []

    if elements.emotions:
        emotion_parts.append(f"梦中情绪：{_join_values(elements.emotions)}")

    if elements.subjective_feelings:
        emotion_parts.append(f"醒后感受：{_join_values(elements.subjective_feelings)}")

    if emotion_parts:
        queries.append(
            DreamRetrievalQuery(
                aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.EMOTION,
                query=(f"梦境情绪传统解释 {'；'.join(emotion_parts)}"),
            )
        )

    reality_parts: list[str] = []

    if background.recent_events:
        reality_parts.append(f"近期事件：{_join_values(background.recent_events)}")

    if background.ongoing_contexts:
        reality_parts.append(f"当前情境：{_join_values(background.ongoing_contexts)}")

    if background.stressors:
        reality_parts.append(f"压力：{_join_values(background.stressors)}")

    if background.user_associations:
        reality_parts.append(f"主观联想：{_join_values(background.user_associations)}")

    if reality_parts:
        queries.append(
            DreamRetrievalQuery(
                aspect=DreamRetrievalAspect.REALITY_CONTEXT,
                query=(f"梦境与现实情境传统解释 {'；'.join(reality_parts)}"),
            )
        )

    return queries


def dedupe_retrieval_results(
    evidence_list: list[DreamRetrievalEvidence],
) -> list[RetrievalResult]:
    """
    跨 Dream retrieval aspect 对 Chunk 做稳定去重。

    保留同一 chunk 第一次出现的位置，
    不根据 score 再次排序。
    """
    deduped: list[RetrievalResult] = []
    seen_chunk_ids: set[str] = set()

    for evidence in evidence_list:
        for result in evidence.results:
            if result.chunk_id in seen_chunk_ids:
                continue

            seen_chunk_ids.add(result.chunk_id)
            deduped.append(result)

    return deduped


class DreamRetrievalService:
    """
    Dream Agent 的领域检索适配层。

    领域边界：
    - 所有 Dream query 必须限定在 KnowledgeDomain.DREAM；
    - 调用方不能覆盖或关闭该过滤条件；
    - Shared RetrievalService 继续负责 Dense/Sparse/RRF/Reranker。
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        retrieval_service: RetrievalService,
    ) -> None:
        self._retrieval_service = retrieval_service

    async def retrieve(
        self,
        record: DreamRecord,
    ) -> list[DreamRetrievalEvidence]:
        queries = build_dream_retrieval_queries(record)

        evidence_groups: list[DreamRetrievalEvidence] = []

        for retrieval_query in queries:
            results = await self._retrieval_service.retrieve(
                query=retrieval_query.query,
                filters=DREAM_RETRIEVAL_FILTER,
            )

            evidence_groups.append(
                DreamRetrievalEvidence(
                    aspect=retrieval_query.aspect,
                    query=retrieval_query.query,
                    results=results,
                )
            )

        return evidence_groups


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\dream\runtime.py
================================================================================

import json
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any

from pydantic import (
    BaseModel,
    ConfigDict,
    Field,
    model_validator,
)

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError
from guanchen_v2.dream.context import (
    DreamContextService,
    DreamContextStatus,
)
from guanchen_v2.dream.dream_merge import mark_interpreted
from guanchen_v2.dream.exceptions import (
    DreamInterpretationError,
)
from guanchen_v2.dream.retrieval import (
    DreamRetrievalService,
    dedupe_retrieval_results,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    Citation,
    RetrievalResult,
)
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import (
    DreamRecord,
    InterpretationStatus,
    NonBlankText,
)
from guanchen_v2.prompts.dream_agent import (
    DREAM_INTERPRETATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    DREAM_INTERPRETATION_USER_TEMPLATE,
)


class DreamResponseMode(StrEnum):
    INITIAL = "initial"
    UPDATE = "update"
    CONTINUE = "continue"


class DreamInterpretationDraft(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    traditional_view: NonBlankText | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=("仅基于提供的传统知识 evidence 形成的文化象征解释；证据不足或未使用传统证据时为 null"),
    )

    psychological_context_view: NonBlankText = Field(
        description=("非诊断性的心理 / 现实情境反思，只基于用户明确提供的梦境、情绪和现实背景"),
    )

    used_chunk_ids: list[str] = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description=("traditional_view 实际使用的 TRADITIONAL EVIDENCE chunk_id，按首次使用顺序返回。"),
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_evidence_contract(
        self,
    ) -> "DreamInterpretationDraft":
        for chunk_id in self.used_chunk_ids:
            if not chunk_id.strip():
                raise ValueError("used_chunk_ids 不能包含空值。")

        if self.traditional_view is None and self.used_chunk_ids:
            raise ValueError("traditional_view 为空时，used_chunk_ids 必须为空。")

        if self.traditional_view is not None and not self.used_chunk_ids:
            raise ValueError("traditional_view 非空时，必须至少引用一个实际使用的 chunk_id。")

        return self


class DreamInterpretationResponse(BaseModel):
    traditional_view: NonBlankText
    psychological_context_view: NonBlankText


class DreamAgentStatus(StrEnum):
    NEEDS_CLARIFICATION = "needs_clarification"
    ANSWERED = "answered"


class DreamAgentResult(BaseModel):
    status: DreamAgentStatus
    record: DreamRecord
    clarification_question: str | None = None
    response: DreamInterpretationResponse | None = None
    citations: list[Citation] = Field(default_factory=list)


def select_response_mode(
    record: DreamRecord,
) -> DreamResponseMode:
    """确定性决定本轮 Dream 回复模式。"""
    if record.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.NOT_STARTED:
        return DreamResponseMode.INITIAL

    if record.interpretation_state.status == InterpretationStatus.STALE:
        return DreamResponseMode.UPDATE

    return DreamResponseMode.CONTINUE


def _format_evidence(
    results: list[RetrievalResult],
) -> str:
    """
    将 RetrievalResult 转换为 LLM 可使用的 evidence。

    只暴露 citation 所需 identity 与正文，
    不暴露 Dense/Sparse/RRF/Reranker 分数。
    """
    if not results:
        return "[]"

    formatted: list[str] = []

    for index, result in enumerate(
        results,
        start=1,
    ):
        formatted.append(
            f"[Evidence {index}]\nchunk_id: {result.chunk_id}\nsource_title: {result.source_title}\nsection: {result.section}\ntext: {result.text}\n"
        )

    return "\n".join(formatted)


def _resolve_used_evidence(
    results: list[RetrievalResult],
    used_chunk_ids: list[str],
) -> list[RetrievalResult]:
    """
    将 LLM 声明实际使用的 chunk_id 映射回本轮合法 evidence。

    - 禁止引用未检索到的 chunk；
    - 按 LLM 第一次使用顺序返回；
    - 重复 chunk_id 只保留一次。
    """
    result_map = {result.chunk_id: result for result in results}

    resolved: list[RetrievalResult] = []
    seen: set[str] = set()

    for raw_chunk_id in used_chunk_ids:
        chunk_id = raw_chunk_id.strip()

        if chunk_id in seen:
            continue

        if chunk_id not in result_map:
            raise DreamInterpretationError("模型返回了未授权的传统知识证据编号。")

        seen.add(chunk_id)
        resolved.append(result_map[chunk_id])

    return resolved


def _map_citations(
    results: list[RetrievalResult],
) -> list[Citation]:
    """只为已经确认实际使用的 evidence 生成 Citation。"""
    return [Citation.from_retrieval_result(result) for result in results]


class DreamAgentRuntime:
    def __init__(
        self,
        context_service: DreamContextService,
        retrieval_service: DreamRetrievalService,
        llm_client: LLMClient,
        record_store: Any | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._context_service = context_service
        self._retrieval_service = retrieval_service
        self._llm_client = llm_client

        self._current_record: DreamRecord | None = None
        self._record_store = record_store

    async def _restore_record(self) -> None:
        """跨进程恢复：从持久层加载当前 DreamRecord（Stage 8）。"""
        if self._current_record is None and self._record_store is not None:
            self._current_record = await self._record_store.load_latest()

    async def _persist_record(self) -> None:
        """每个 turn 结束后持久化当前 DreamRecord。"""
        if self._record_store is not None and self._current_record is not None:
            await self._record_store.save(self._current_record)

    async def handle_turn(
        self,
        user_text: str,
    ) -> DreamAgentResult:
        await self._restore_record()

        # 1. 更新 Dream 状态
        context_result = await self._context_service.apply_turn(
            user_text,
            current_record=self._current_record,
        )

        # Merge 成功后，新状态立即成为当前状态。
        self._current_record = context_result.record
        await self._persist_record()

        # 2. 需要澄清时短路
        if context_result.status == DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION:
            return DreamAgentResult(
                status=DreamAgentStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
                record=self._current_record,
                clarification_question=(context_result.clarification_question),
            )

        # 3. 回复模式由状态机决定
        mode = select_response_mode(self._current_record)

        # 4. DreamRetrievalService 内部强制 DREAM domain
        evidence_groups = await self._retrieval_service.retrieve(self._current_record)

        deduped_results = dedupe_retrieval_results(evidence_groups)

        # 5. 构建最小化解释上下文
        state_str = json.dumps(
            {
                "elements": (self._current_record.elements.model_dump()),
                "reality_background": (self._current_record.reality_background.model_dump()),
            },
            ensure_ascii=False,
        )

        if context_result.patch is None:
            delta_str = "{}"
        else:
            delta_str = context_result.patch.model_dump_json(exclude_unset=True)

        evidence_str = _format_evidence(deduped_results)

        user_prompt = DREAM_INTERPRETATION_USER_TEMPLATE.format(
            response_mode=mode.value,
            current_state=state_str,
            current_delta=delta_str,
            traditional_evidence=evidence_str,
        )

        # 6. LLM 结构化解释
        try:
            draft = await self._llm_client.generate_structured(
                system_prompt=(DREAM_INTERPRETATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT),
                user_prompt=user_prompt,
                schema=DreamInterpretationDraft,
            )
        except ProjectError as exc:
            raise DreamInterpretationError("梦境解释生成失败") from exc

        # 7. 验证 LLM 声明使用的 evidence
        used_results = _resolve_used_evidence(
            results=deduped_results,
            used_chunk_ids=draft.used_chunk_ids,
        )

        fallback_traditional_view = "当前共享知识库没有检索到足够可追溯的传统资料，因此本轮暂不作具体的传统象征判断。"

        # 8. traditional_view 必须与真实 evidence lineage 对齐
        if draft.traditional_view is None or not used_results:
            final_traditional_view = fallback_traditional_view
            citations: list[Citation] = []
        else:
            final_traditional_view = draft.traditional_view
            citations = _map_citations(used_results)

        response = DreamInterpretationResponse(
            traditional_view=final_traditional_view,
            psychological_context_view=(draft.psychological_context_view),
        )

        # 9. 解释成功后才将 interpretation 标为 CURRENT
        self._current_record = mark_interpreted(self._current_record)
        await self._persist_record()

        return DreamAgentResult(
            status=DreamAgentStatus.ANSWERED,
            record=self._current_record,
            response=response,
            citations=citations,
        )

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\knowledge\embeddings.py

from typing import Sequence

from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeEmbeddingError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeChunk


class BgeEmbeddingAdapter:
    """
    GuanChen BGE Embedding Adapter。

    职责：
    1. 使用固定 Embedding 模型生成 Dense Vector。
    2. 保证输出维度为 512。
    3. 保证向量执行 L2 normalization。
    4. 管理模型生命周期与 device / batch 策略。
    5. 向持久化与检索层暴露稳定的模型身份。
    6. 隔离 sentence-transformers / PyTorch 异常。

    不负责：
    - PostgreSQL / pgvector
    - Retrieval
    - Reranker
    - Citation
    """

    MODEL_NAME = "BAAI/bge-small-zh-v1.5"
    DIMENSION = 512

    def __init__(
        self,
        device: str = "cpu",
        batch_size: int = 32,
    ) -> None:
        self._device = device
        self._batch_size = batch_size

        try:
            self._model = SentenceTransformer(
                self.MODEL_NAME,
                device=self._device,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("Embedding 模型加载失败。") from exc

    @property
    def model_name(self) -> str:
        """
        当前 Adapter 生成向量所使用的稳定模型身份。

        Persistence 与 Dense Retrieval 必须使用该值，
        防止不同 Embedding 模型的向量被错误混用。
        """
        return self.MODEL_NAME

    def embed_documents(
        self,
        chunks: Sequence[KnowledgeChunk],
    ) -> list[list[float]]:
        if not chunks:
            return []

        texts_to_embed: list[str] = []

        for chunk in chunks:
            if not chunk.text.strip():
                raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError(f"尝试对空文本进行 Embedding。Chunk ID: {chunk.chunk_id}")

            # stored chunk text != embedding representation
            #
            # 数据库存储的 chunk.text 保持原文可读性；
            # 模型输入额外加入 source_title + section，
            # 但绝不修改 KnowledgeChunk 本身。
            embed_input = f"{chunk.source_title}\n{chunk.section}\n{chunk.text}"

            texts_to_embed.append(embed_input)

        try:
            embeddings = self._model.encode(
                texts_to_embed,
                batch_size=self._batch_size,
                show_progress_bar=False,
                normalize_embeddings=True,
                convert_to_numpy=True,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("文档批量 Embedding 推理失败。") from exc

        expected_shape = (
            len(chunks),
            self.DIMENSION,
        )

        if not hasattr(embeddings, "shape") or embeddings.shape != expected_shape:
            actual_shape = getattr(
                embeddings,
                "shape",
                "Unknown",
            )

            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError(f"模型输出的形状异常，预期为 {expected_shape}，实际为 {actual_shape}。")

        try:
            return embeddings.tolist()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("向量格式转换失败。") from exc

    def embed_query(
        self,
        query: str,
    ) -> list[float]:
        if not query.strip():
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("检索查询 (Query) 不能为空。")

        try:
            embedding = self._model.encode(
                query,
                show_progress_bar=False,
                normalize_embeddings=True,
                convert_to_numpy=True,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("查询 Embedding 推理失败。") from exc

        expected_shape = (self.DIMENSION,)

        if not hasattr(embedding, "shape") or embedding.shape != expected_shape:
            actual_shape = getattr(
                embedding,
                "shape",
                "Unknown",
            )

            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError(f"模型输出了错误的形状 {actual_shape}，预期应为 {expected_shape}。")

        try:
            return embedding.tolist()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeEmbeddingError("向量格式转换失败。") from exc

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError


class KnowledgeError(ProjectError):
    """GuanChen Knowledge 子系统异常根类型。"""

    pass


class KnowledgeEmbeddingError(KnowledgeError):
    """
    Embedding 模型加载、推理或向量契约异常。
    """

    pass


class KnowledgePersistenceError(KnowledgeError):
    """
    Knowledge persistence / database 基础异常。
    """

    pass


class KnowledgeTransactionError(KnowledgePersistenceError):
    """
    Knowledge document transaction 执行失败。
    """

    pass


class KnowledgeRetrievalError(KnowledgeError):
    """
    Knowledge retrieval 执行或输入契约异常。
    """

    pass


class KnowledgeRerankerError(KnowledgeRetrievalError):
    """
    Reranker 模型加载、推理或结果契约异常。
    """

    pass


class KnowledgeAgentError(KnowledgeError):
    """
    Knowledge Agent 业务契约异常。

    例如：
    - 非法 query
    - 非法 evidence lineage
    - 模型返回未授权 chunk_id
    """

    pass

from collections.abc import Collection, Hashable, Sequence
from typing import TypeVar

T = TypeVar("T", bound=Hashable)


def calculate_recall_at_k(
    relevant_items: Collection[T],
    ranked_items: Sequence[T],
    k: int,
) -> float:
    """
    Recall@K

        | relevant ∩ top_k | / | relevant |

    relevance unit 由调用方决定：
    - 正式 Stage 4 Eval：source_title + section
    - Diagnostic：exact chunk_id
    """
    if k <= 0:
        raise ValueError("k 必须大于 0")

    if not relevant_items:
        raise ValueError("Ground Truth 不能为空")

    top_k = ranked_items[:k]
    hits = set(top_k).intersection(relevant_items)

    return len(hits) / len(relevant_items)


def calculate_mrr_at_k(
    relevant_items: Collection[T],
    ranked_items: Sequence[T],
    k: int,
) -> float:
    """
    单个 query 的 Reciprocal Rank@K。

    整个数据集对结果取平均后得到 MRR@K。
    """
    if k <= 0:
        raise ValueError("k 必须大于 0")

    if not relevant_items:
        raise ValueError("Ground Truth 不能为空")

    for rank, item in enumerate(
        ranked_items[:k],
        start=1,
    ):
        if item in relevant_items:
            return 1.0 / rank

    return 0.0


def calculate_percentile(
    latencies_ms: Sequence[float],
    percentile: float,
) -> float:
    """使用线性插值计算百分位数。"""
    if not latencies_ms:
        raise ValueError("没有 latency observation，无法计算 percentile")

    if not 0.0 <= percentile <= 100.0:
        raise ValueError("percentile 必须位于 [0, 100]")

    sorted_latencies = sorted(latencies_ms)

    position = (len(sorted_latencies) - 1) * (percentile / 100.0)

    lower = int(position)
    upper = lower + 1

    if upper >= len(sorted_latencies):
        return sorted_latencies[lower]

    lower_weight = upper - position
    upper_weight = position - lower

    return sorted_latencies[lower] * lower_weight + sorted_latencies[upper] * upper_weight


def calculate_relative_mrr_gain(
    baseline_mrr: float,
    candidate_mrr: float,
) -> float:
    """
    Reranker 相对于 RRF baseline 的 MRR 相对增益：

        (candidate - baseline) / baseline
    """
    if baseline_mrr <= 0.0:
        raise ValueError("baseline MRR 必须大于 0，才能计算 relative MRR gain")

    return (candidate_mrr - baseline_mrr) / baseline_mrr

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import FrozenSet, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import RetrievalFilter


class RetrievalEvalCase(BaseModel):
    """
    单条 Retrieval Ground Truth。

    relevant_chunk_ids 的职责：
    1. 作为人工标注 anchor，保证标注能追溯到真实 corpus chunk；
    2. Preflight 时从数据库反查 source_title + section；
    3. source_title + section 才是 Stage 4 正式 Retrieval Gate 的 relevance unit；
    4. exact chunk_id 仅保留为诊断指标。
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    case_id: str = Field(min_length=1)
    query: str = Field(min_length=1)
    relevant_chunk_ids: FrozenSet[str] = Field(min_length=1)
    filters: Optional[RetrievalFilter] = None
    note: Optional[str] = None

    @field_validator("case_id", "query")
    @classmethod
    def validate_required_text(cls, value: str) -> str:
        normalized = value.strip()
        if not normalized:
            raise ValueError("字段不能为空或纯空格")
        return normalized

    @field_validator("relevant_chunk_ids")
    @classmethod
    def validate_chunk_ids(
        cls,
        value: FrozenSet[str],
    ) -> FrozenSet[str]:
        normalized = frozenset(chunk_id.strip() for chunk_id in value)

        if not normalized:
            raise ValueError("relevant_chunk_ids 不能为空")

        if any(not chunk_id for chunk_id in normalized):
            raise ValueError("relevant_chunk_ids 不能包含纯空格 ID")

        return normalized


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetrievalEvalMetrics:
    """单条 Retrieval Pipeline 的汇总指标。"""

    recall_at_5: float
    mrr_at_10: float
    query_count: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetrievalEvalReport:
    """
    Stage 4 Retrieval Eval 总报告。

    dense / sparse / rrf / reranked：
        正式 Stage 4 指标，relevance unit 为 source_title + section。

    *_exact_chunk_diagnostic：
        精确 chunk_id 诊断指标，不参与 Stage 4 Core Retrieval Gate。

    reranker 字段允许为 None：
        None 表示该 benchmark 没有执行 reranker，
        不能与实际测得的 0.0 混淆。
    """

    query_count: int

    dense: RetrievalEvalMetrics
    sparse: RetrievalEvalMetrics
    rrf: RetrievalEvalMetrics

    dense_exact_chunk_diagnostic: RetrievalEvalMetrics
    sparse_exact_chunk_diagnostic: RetrievalEvalMetrics
    rrf_exact_chunk_diagnostic: RetrievalEvalMetrics

    reranked: RetrievalEvalMetrics | None = None
    reranked_exact_chunk_diagnostic: RetrievalEvalMetrics | None = None

    reranker_relative_mrr_gain: float | None = None
    reranker_added_p50_ms: float | None = None
    reranker_added_p95_ms: float | None = None

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import FrozenSet, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator


class KnowledgeDomain(StrEnum):
    """共享知识库支持的业务知识领域白名单。"""

    BAZI = "bazi"
    YIJING = "yijing"
    LIUYAO = "liuyao"
    DREAM = "dream"
    TRADITIONAL_CULTURE = "traditional_culture"


class SourceType(StrEnum):
    """知识库来源类型边界。"""

    PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC = "public_domain_classic"
    PROJECT_NOTE = "project_note"


def normalize_required_text(value: str) -> str:
    """
    通用基础非空文本校验。

    仅剥离首尾空白，保留内部原有格式，
    包括换行、段落和有意义的空格。
    """
    normalized = value.strip()

    if not normalized:
        raise ValueError("字段不能为空或纯空格")

    return normalized


def canonicalize_text_for_id(value: str) -> str:
    """
    专用于确定性 ID 的文本规范化。

    将连续空白规范为单空格，
    不用于修改实际持久化的 KnowledgeDocument.text
    或 KnowledgeChunk.text。
    """
    canonicalized = " ".join(value.split())

    if not canonicalized:
        raise ValueError("文本规范化后不能为空")

    return canonicalized


def generate_deterministic_document_id(
    domain: str,
    source_title: str,
    section: str,
) -> str:
    """基于领域、书名和章节路径生成稳定逻辑文档 ID。"""
    normalized_domain = normalize_required_text(domain)
    normalized_title = normalize_required_text(source_title)
    normalized_section = normalize_required_text(section)

    content = (f"DOC||{normalized_domain}||{normalized_title}||{normalized_section}").encode("utf-8")

    return hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()[:32]


def generate_deterministic_chunk_id(
    document_id: str,
    section: str,
    text: str,
) -> str:
    """
    基于 Document identity 与规范化 Chunk 内容生成确定性 ID。

    whitespace-only 差异经过 canonicalization 后不会改变 ID；
    规范化后的实际内容变化会改变 ID。
    """
    normalized_document_id = normalize_required_text(document_id)
    normalized_section = normalize_required_text(section)
    canonicalized_text = canonicalize_text_for_id(text)

    content = (f"{normalized_document_id}||{normalized_section}||{canonicalized_text}").encode("utf-8")

    return hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()[:32]


class KnowledgeDocument(BaseModel):
    """具有稳定来源身份的逻辑知识文档单元。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    document_id: str = Field(min_length=1)
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str = Field(min_length=1)
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str = Field(min_length=1)
    text: str = Field(min_length=1)

    @field_validator(
        "document_id",
        "source_title",
        "section",
        "text",
        mode="before",
    )
    @classmethod
    def validate_required_fields(cls, value: str) -> str:
        return normalize_required_text(value)

    @classmethod
    def from_parser(
        cls,
        *,
        domain: KnowledgeDomain,
        source_title: str,
        source_type: SourceType,
        section: str,
        text: str,
    ) -> "KnowledgeDocument":
        """Parser 创建 KnowledgeDocument 的标准构造入口。"""
        document_id = generate_deterministic_document_id(
            domain=domain.value,
            source_title=source_title,
            section=section,
        )

        return cls(
            document_id=document_id,
            domain=domain,
            source_title=source_title,
            source_type=source_type,
            section=section,
            text=text,
        )


class KnowledgeChunk(BaseModel):
    """
    Embedding 与 Retrieval 使用的最小知识搜索单元。

    不持有向量、检索分数或 query-time ranking 信息。
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    chunk_id: str = Field(min_length=1)
    document_id: str = Field(min_length=1)
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str = Field(min_length=1)
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str = Field(min_length=1)
    text: str = Field(min_length=1)

    @field_validator(
        "chunk_id",
        "document_id",
        "source_title",
        "section",
        "text",
        mode="before",
    )
    @classmethod
    def validate_required_fields(cls, value: str) -> str:
        return normalize_required_text(value)

    @classmethod
    def from_document_part(
        cls,
        *,
        document_id: str,
        domain: KnowledgeDomain,
        source_title: str,
        source_type: SourceType,
        section: str,
        text: str,
    ) -> "KnowledgeChunk":
        """由 KnowledgeDocument 派生 Chunk 的标准构造入口。"""
        normalized_document_id = normalize_required_text(document_id)
        normalized_section = normalize_required_text(section)
        validated_text = normalize_required_text(text)

        chunk_id = generate_deterministic_chunk_id(
            document_id=normalized_document_id,
            section=normalized_section,
            text=validated_text,
        )

        return cls(
            chunk_id=chunk_id,
            document_id=normalized_document_id,
            domain=domain,
            source_title=source_title,
            source_type=source_type,
            section=normalized_section,
            text=validated_text,
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DenseSearchHit:
    """
    Dense Retrieval 返回的内部候选结果。

    score 越大表示相关性越强。
    Embedding 本身不进入该对象。
    """

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str
    score: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SparseSearchHit:
    """
    Sparse Retrieval 返回的内部候选结果。

    score 为 BM25 原始分值，不进行跨检索器直接比较。
    """

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str
    score: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SparseIndexEntry:
    """SparseRetriever 使用的只读知识快照。"""

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetrievalFilter:
    """
    统一检索过滤器。

    同一字段中的多个值使用 OR 语义；
    不同字段之间使用 AND 语义；
    None 表示该字段不设限制。
    """

    domains: Optional[FrozenSet[KnowledgeDomain]] = None
    source_types: Optional[FrozenSet[SourceType]] = None
    source_titles: Optional[FrozenSet[str]] = None
    sections: Optional[FrozenSet[str]] = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RrfSearchHit:
    """
    RRF 融合后的内部候选结果。

    score 为 RRF 融合分。
    """

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str
    score: float
    dense_rank: Optional[int]
    sparse_rank: Optional[int]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RerankedSearchHit:
    """
    Reranker 重排后的内部候选结果。

    score 为 reranker 原始 relevance score。
    """

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str
    score: float
    rrf_score: float
    rrf_rank: int
    dense_rank: Optional[int]
    sparse_rank: Optional[int]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class RetrievalResult:
    """RAG 对上层暴露的稳定最终检索结果。"""

    rank: int
    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    domain: KnowledgeDomain
    source_title: str
    source_type: SourceType
    section: str
    text: str
    rrf_score: float
    rrf_rank: int
    reranker_score: Optional[float]
    dense_rank: Optional[int]
    sparse_rank: Optional[int]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Citation:
    """
    对最终回答暴露的稳定知识来源凭证。

    不包含正文、检索分数或动态引用序号。
    """

    chunk_id: str
    document_id: str
    source_title: str
    section: str

    @classmethod
    def from_retrieval_result(
        cls,
        result: RetrievalResult,
    ) -> "Citation":
        return cls(
            chunk_id=result.chunk_id,
            document_id=result.document_id,
            source_title=result.source_title,
            section=result.section,
        )


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\knowledge\rerank_adapter.py
================================================================================

import math

import torch.nn as nn
from sentence_transformers import CrossEncoder

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeRerankerError


class BgeRerankerAdapter:
    """
    BAAI/bge-reranker-base 交叉编码器 (Cross-Encoder) 适配层。
    职责：
    1. 冻结模型语义契约，仅接受 SentenceTransformers 方案。
    2. 抵挡大模型环境所有的第三方报错，转译为安全的业务异常。
    3. 拦截数量不对齐、NaN、无穷大 (Inf) 等大模型幻觉/异常。
    """

    MODEL_NAME = "BAAI/bge-reranker-base"

    def __init__(self):
        self._model = None

    def _get_model(self):
        if self._model is not None:
            return self._model

        try:
            self._model = CrossEncoder(self.MODEL_NAME, default_activation_function=nn.Identity())
            return self._model
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeRerankerError(f"无法加载重排模型 {self.MODEL_NAME}。请检查网络或本地缓存。") from exc

    def score_pairs(self, pairs: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> list[float]:
        """
        向交叉编码器发送成对的 (query, passage)，获取未归一化的原生打分。
        分数越大代表相关性越高。
        """
        if not pairs:
            return []

        model = self._get_model()

        try:
            raw_scores = model.predict(pairs)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeRerankerError("重排模型推理崩溃。") from exc

        try:
            if hasattr(raw_scores, "tolist"):
                scores_list = raw_scores.tolist()
            elif isinstance(raw_scores, list):
                scores_list = raw_scores
            else:
                scores_list = [float(raw_scores)]
        except Exception as exc:
            raise KnowledgeRerankerError("无法将重排模型的输出解析为浮点数列表。") from exc

        if len(scores_list) != len(pairs):
            raise KnowledgeRerankerError("重排模型返回的评分数量与输入对数量不匹配！")

        final_scores = []
        for i, s in enumerate(scores_list):
            try:
                score = float(s)
            except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
                raise KnowledgeRerankerError(f"重排模型返回了非标量结构或无法转为浮点数：{s}") from exc

            if not math.isfinite(score):
                raise KnowledgeRerankerError(f"重排模型返回了非法的无穷数值打分 (NaN/Inf)：{score}")

            final_scores.append(score)

        return final_scores


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\casting.py
================================================================================

import secrets
from typing import Literal, Protocol, cast

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.iching_adapter import IChingPyAdapter
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    CastingMethod,
    CoinFace,
    CoinToss,
    HexagramResult,
    LineValue,
)

SixLineValues = tuple[LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue]


class ManualCastingInput(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    method: Literal[CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES] = CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES
    lines: SixLineValues


class CoinRandomSource(Protocol):
    def toss(self) -> CoinFace: ...


class SystemRandomCoinSource:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._rng = secrets.SystemRandom()

    def toss(self) -> CoinFace:
        return CoinFace.HEAD if self._rng.choice([True, False]) else CoinFace.TAIL


class HexagramCastingService:
    def __init__(self, adapter: IChingPyAdapter, random_source: CoinRandomSource) -> None:
        self._adapter = adapter
        self._random = random_source

    def cast_manual(self, input_data: ManualCastingInput) -> HexagramResult:
        facts = self._adapter.resolve_facts(input_data.lines)
        return HexagramResult(
            casting_method=CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES,
            lines=facts.lines,
            primary_hexagram=facts.primary_hexagram,
            changed_hexagram=facts.changed_hexagram,
            moving_line_positions=facts.moving_line_positions,
            coin_tosses=[],
        )

    def cast_random(self) -> HexagramResult:
        tosses = []
        line_values = []

        for pos in range(1, 7):
            coins = (self._random.toss(), self._random.toss(), self._random.toss())
            val_int = sum(3 if c == CoinFace.HEAD else 2 for c in coins)
            lv = LineValue(val_int)

            tosses.append(CoinToss(position=pos, coins=coins, line_value=lv))
            line_values.append(lv)

        canonical_lines = cast(SixLineValues, tuple(line_values))
        facts = self._adapter.resolve_facts(canonical_lines)

        return HexagramResult(
            casting_method=CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN,
            lines=facts.lines,
            primary_hexagram=facts.primary_hexagram,
            changed_hexagram=facts.changed_hexagram,
            moving_line_positions=facts.moving_line_positions,
            coin_tosses=tosses,
        )


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\checkpoint.py
================================================================================

import uuid
from typing import Any

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import LiuyaoCheckpointError


class LiuyaoCheckpointManager:
    """负责将 LangGraph 的 Thread Reference 同步到 Application Session State"""

    @staticmethod
    def get_or_create_thread(agent_state: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """从状态中获取当前 Thread ID，如果不存在则自动创建一个新的以隔离流程"""
        if not isinstance(agent_state, dict):
            raise LiuyaoCheckpointError("非法的 agent_state 容器类型。")
            
        if "thread_id" not in agent_state:
            agent_state["thread_id"] = f"liuyao_thread_{uuid.uuid4().hex}"
            agent_state.pop("status", None)
            agent_state.pop("pending_node", None)
        return agent_state["thread_id"]

    @staticmethod
    def save_checkpoint_reference(
        agent_state: dict[str, Any],
        thread_id: str,
        status: str,
        pending_node: str | None = None
    ) -> None:
        """更新工作流恢复所需的核心 metadata"""
        agent_state["thread_id"] = thread_id
        agent_state["status"] = status
        
        if pending_node:
            agent_state["pending_node"] = pending_node
        elif "pending_node" in agent_state:
            del agent_state["pending_node"]

    @staticmethod
    def get_thread_id(agent_state: dict[str, Any]) -> str | None:
        return agent_state.get("thread_id")

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError


class LiuyaoError(ProjectError):
    """
    GuanChen 六爻子系统异常根类型。
    """

    pass


class HexagramCalculationError(LiuyaoError):
    """
    六爻卦象确定性计算失败。
    当第三方排盘库崩溃或返回非法格式时抛出。
    """

    pass


class LiuyaoPolicyError(LiuyaoError):
    """六爻策略与状态机校验不一致异常"""

    pass

class LiuyaoCheckpointError(LiuyaoError):
    """
    LangGraph thread 创建、恢复或引用无效等持久化异常。
    """
    pass
