from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import CastingMethod, HexagramResult


def format_hexagram_facts_for_prompt(hexagram: HexagramResult) -> str:
    parts = []

    method_str = "手工指定" if hexagram.casting_method == CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES else "系统三枚硬币"
    parts.append(f"起卦方式：{method_str}")

    p_hex = hexagram.primary_hexagram
    p_name = f" ({p_hex.name}卦)" if p_hex.name else ""
    parts.append(f"\n本卦：上{p_hex.upper_trigram} 下{p_hex.lower_trigram}{p_name}")

    if hexagram.changed_hexagram:
        c_hex = hexagram.changed_hexagram
        c_name = f" ({c_hex.name}卦)" if c_hex.name else ""
        parts.append(f"变卦：上{c_hex.upper_trigram} 下{c_hex.lower_trigram}{c_name}")

    if hexagram.moving_line_positions:
        moves = "、".join([f"第{pos}爻" for pos in hexagram.moving_line_positions])
        parts.append(f"动爻位置：{moves}")
    else:
        parts.append("动爻位置：无动爻 (静卦)")

    parts.append("\n六爻确定性事实 (初爻 -> 上爻)：")
    for line in hexagram.lines:
        line_parts = [f"第{line.position}爻: {line.polarity.value}", "动" if line.moving else "静"]
        if line.heavenly_stem and line.earthly_branch:
            line_parts.append(f"干支={line.heavenly_stem}{line.earthly_branch}")
        if line.element:
            line_parts.append(f"五行={line.element}")
        if line.six_relative:
            line_parts.append(f"六亲={line.six_relative}")
        if line.is_shi:
            line_parts.append("[世爻]")
        if line.is_ying:
            line_parts.append("[应爻]")

        parts.append(" | ".join(line_parts))

    return "\n".join(parts)


def format_evidence_for_prompt(results: list) -> str:
    if not results:
        return "当前未检索到相关传统证据支持。"

    parts = []
    for idx, res in enumerate(results, 1):
        parts.append(f"[Evidence {idx}]\nchunk_id: {res.chunk_id}\nsource_title: {res.source_title}\ntext: {res.text}\n")
    return "\n".join(parts)


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\iching_adapter.py
================================================================================

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import HexagramCalculationError
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    HexagramIdentity,
    HexagramLine,
    LineValue,
    YinYang,
)


class VendorLineFacts(BaseModel):
    """
    从 ichingpy SixLinesDivinationEngine 标准化出的单爻事实。

    six_spirit 当前 ichingpy 0.1.10 未提供，因此保持 None。
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    heavenly_stem: str
    earthly_branch: str
    element: str
    six_relative: str

    six_spirit: str | None = None

    is_shi: bool = False
    is_ying: bool = False


class HexagramFacts(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    lines: tuple[HexagramLine, ...]
    primary_hexagram: HexagramIdentity
    changed_hexagram: HexagramIdentity | None
    moving_line_positions: list[int]


class IChingPyAdapter:
    """
    GuanChen 与 ichingpy 之间的唯一适配边界。

    项目内部只使用：
        LineValue(6/7/8/9)
        HexagramResult / HexagramLine

    ichingpy 的对象结构不会泄漏到业务层。
    """

    def _resolve_vendor_hexagram(
        self,
        line_values: tuple[LineValue, ...],
    ) -> tuple[HexagramIdentity, tuple[VendorLineFacts, ...]]:
        """
        唯一正式 vendor path：

        LineValue
        -> ichingpy.LineStatus
        -> Line / Trigram / Hexagram
        -> SixLinesDivinationEngine.execute()
        -> hexagram.interpretation.lines
        -> GuanChen normalized facts
        """
        import ichingpy
        from ichingpy.enum.role import HexagramRole

        if len(line_values) != 6:
            raise HexagramCalculationError("ichingpy 六爻构造输入必须正好包含 6 爻")

        status_map = {
            LineValue.OLD_YIN: ichingpy.LineStatus.CHANGING_YIN,
            LineValue.YOUNG_YANG: ichingpy.LineStatus.STATIC_YANG,
            LineValue.YOUNG_YIN: ichingpy.LineStatus.STATIC_YIN,
            LineValue.OLD_YANG: ichingpy.LineStatus.CHANGING_YANG,
        }

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # 1. 构造真实 ichingpy Hexagram
        # 顺序保持 bottom -> top：
        # lines[:3] = 初、二、三爻（内卦）
        # lines[3:] = 四、五、上爻（外卦）
        # -----------------------------------------------------
        vendor_lines = [ichingpy.Line(status=status_map[value]) for value in line_values]

        hexagram = ichingpy.Hexagram(
            inner=ichingpy.Trigram(lines=vendor_lines[:3]),
            outer=ichingpy.Trigram(lines=vendor_lines[3:]),
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # 2. 六爻装卦 / 纳甲 / 六亲 / 世应
        # -----------------------------------------------------
        engine = ichingpy.SixLinesDivinationEngine()
        engine.execute(hexagram)

        interpretation = hexagram.interpretation
        if interpretation is None:
            raise HexagramCalculationError("ichingpy 未生成六爻装卦结果")

        interpreted_lines = list(interpretation.lines)

        if len(interpreted_lines) != 6:
            raise HexagramCalculationError(f"ichingpy 装卦结果异常：预期 6 爻，实际返回 {len(interpreted_lines)} 爻")

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # 3. 卦象 Identity
        #
        # ichingpy Trigram 有正式 name，例如：
        # 乾 / 兑 / 离 / 震 / 巽 / 坎 / 艮 / 坤
        #
        # SixLinesDivinationEngine 本身不提供稳定的完整六十四卦名，
        # 所以 name 保持 None，不制造伪卦名。
        # -----------------------------------------------------
        identity = HexagramIdentity(
            name=None,
            upper_trigram=hexagram.outer.name,
            lower_trigram=hexagram.inner.name,
        )

        # -----------------------------------------------------
        # 4. 从 SixLineLineInterp 提取真实装卦字段
        #
        # 注意：
        # 数据在 hexagram.interpretation.lines，
        # 不在原始 hexagram.inner.lines / outer.lines 上。
        # -----------------------------------------------------
        normalized_lines: list[VendorLineFacts] = []

        for line_interp in interpreted_lines:
            stem = line_interp.stem
            branch = line_interp.branch
            relative = line_interp.relative
            role = line_interp.role

            normalized_lines.append(
                VendorLineFacts(
                    heavenly_stem=stem.label,
                    earthly_branch=branch.label,
                    element=branch.phase.label,
                    six_relative=relative.label,
                    # ichingpy 0.1.10 当前没有六神数据
                    six_spirit=None,
                    is_shi=role == HexagramRole.SUBJECT,
                    is_ying=role == HexagramRole.OBJECT,
                )
            )

        return identity, tuple(normalized_lines)

    @staticmethod
    def _to_changed_line_value(value: LineValue) -> LineValue:
        """
        动爻变换的 canonical 规则。

        老阴 6 -> 少阳 7
        老阳 9 -> 少阴 8
        静爻保持不变
        """
        if value == LineValue.OLD_YIN:
            return LineValue.YOUNG_YANG

        if value == LineValue.OLD_YANG:
            return LineValue.YOUNG_YIN

        return value

    def resolve_facts(
        self,
        line_values: tuple[LineValue, ...],
    ) -> HexagramFacts:
        try:
            if len(line_values) != 6:
                raise HexagramCalculationError("六爻输入必须正好包含 6 爻")

            # -------------------------------------------------
            # 1. 本卦 + 装卦事实
            # -------------------------------------------------
            primary_identity, primary_line_facts = self._resolve_vendor_hexagram(line_values)

            hexagram_lines: list[HexagramLine] = []
            moving_positions: list[int] = []

            for position, (value, vendor_fact) in enumerate(
                zip(
                    line_values,
                    primary_line_facts,
                    strict=True,
                ),
                start=1,
            ):
                moving = value in {
                    LineValue.OLD_YIN,
                    LineValue.OLD_YANG,
                }

                polarity = (
                    YinYang.YIN
                    if value
                    in {
                        LineValue.OLD_YIN,
                        LineValue.YOUNG_YIN,
                    }
                    else YinYang.YANG
                )

                if moving:
                    moving_positions.append(position)

                hexagram_lines.append(
                    HexagramLine(
                        position=position,
                        value=value,
                        polarity=polarity,
                        moving=moving,
                        heavenly_stem=vendor_fact.heavenly_stem,
                        earthly_branch=vendor_fact.earthly_branch,
                        element=vendor_fact.element,
                        six_relative=vendor_fact.six_relative,
                        six_spirit=vendor_fact.six_spirit,
                        is_shi=vendor_fact.is_shi,
                        is_ying=vendor_fact.is_ying,
                    )
                )

            # -------------------------------------------------
            # 2. 变卦
            #
            # 仅执行最小 canonical line transformation，
            # 然后重新交给同一个 ichingpy path 构造卦象。
            # 不维护任何六十四卦映射表。
            # -------------------------------------------------
            changed_identity: HexagramIdentity | None = None

            if moving_positions:
                changed_line_values = tuple(self._to_changed_line_value(value) for value in line_values)

                changed_identity, _ = self._resolve_vendor_hexagram(changed_line_values)

            return HexagramFacts(
                lines=tuple(hexagram_lines),
                primary_hexagram=primary_identity,
                changed_hexagram=changed_identity,
                moving_line_positions=moving_positions,
            )

        except HexagramCalculationError:
            raise

        except Exception as exc:
            raise HexagramCalculationError("六爻卦象构造与装卦推演失败") from exc


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\policy.py
================================================================================

from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, model_validator

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import LiuyaoPolicyError
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import LiuyaoState
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    DecisionQuestion,
    DecisionQuestionStatus,
    NonBlankText,
)


class ConfirmationAction(StrEnum):
    CONFIRM = "confirm"
    MODIFY = "modify"
    CANCEL = "cancel"


class ConfirmationResponse(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    action: ConfirmationAction
    modification: NonBlankText | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_action_payload(self) -> "ConfirmationResponse":
        if self.action != ConfirmationAction.MODIFY and self.modification is not None:
            raise ValueError("只有 MODIFY action 可以携带 modification")
        return self


class ClarificationAssessment(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    needs_clarification: bool
    clarified_question: NonBlankText | None = None
    reality_constraints: list[NonBlankText] = Field(default_factory=list)
    clarification_prompt: NonBlankText | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_assessment(self) -> "ClarificationAssessment":
        if self.needs_clarification:
            if self.clarification_prompt is None:
                raise ValueError("需要澄清时必须提供 clarification_prompt")
            if self.clarified_question is not None:
                raise ValueError("尚需澄清时不能提前形成 clarified_question")
        else:
            if self.clarified_question is None:
                raise ValueError("可进入确认时必须存在 clarified_question")
            if self.clarification_prompt is not None:
                raise ValueError("已形成候选问题时不能继续携带 clarification_prompt")
        return self


def rebuild_question(
    question: DecisionQuestion,
    **updates: Any,
) -> DecisionQuestion:
    """使用完整 model_validator 重新校验并构建 DecisionQuestion"""
    payload = question.model_dump()
    payload.update(updates)
    return DecisionQuestion.model_validate(payload)


def invalidate_downstream(state: LiuyaoState) -> LiuyaoState:
    """统一的下游结果失效清空 helper，修改问题会导致所有后续资产失效"""
    return {
        **state,
        "confirmed": False,
        "hexagram": None,
        "retrieved_context": [],
        "reflection": None,
        "citations": [],
        "final_response": None,
    }


def project_confirmed(question: DecisionQuestion) -> bool:
    """confirmed 状态永远由 question.status 确定性投影"""
    return question.status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED


def validate_confirmation_projection(state: LiuyaoState) -> None:
    """State 一致性 Guard：防止 status 与 confirmed 出现孤立脱节"""
    question: DecisionQuestion | None = state.get("question")
    if not question:
        raise LiuyaoPolicyError("六爻状态机中缺少 DecisionQuestion")

    expected = project_confirmed(question)
    if state.get("confirmed", False) != expected:
        raise LiuyaoPolicyError("六爻确认状态不一致")


def can_cast(state: LiuyaoState) -> bool:
    """
    确定性起卦硬门槛：
    只有 question.status == CONFIRMED 且 confirmed projection 一致时才允许起卦。
    """
    validate_confirmation_projection(state)
    question: DecisionQuestion = state["question"]
    return question.status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED


def apply_clarification_assessment(
    state: LiuyaoState,
    assessment: ClarificationAssessment,
) -> LiuyaoState:
    """根据 LLM 的 ClarificationAssessment 驱动状态机更新"""
    validate_confirmation_projection(state)
    question: DecisionQuestion = state["question"]

    # 防止非草稿/非澄清阶段直接通过评估结果重写状态，绕过显式 MODIFY 语义
    if question.status not in {
        DecisionQuestionStatus.DRAFT,
        DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
    }:
        raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"当前状态 {question.status} 不允许直接应用澄清结果")

    # 若之前已处于非 DRAFT 状态，新 proposal 产生时需要先清理下游
    cleaned_state = invalidate_downstream(state)

    if assessment.needs_clarification:
        new_q = rebuild_question(
            question,
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
            clarified_question=None,
            reality_constraints=assessment.reality_constraints,
        )
        return {
            **cleaned_state,
            "question": new_q,
            "clarification": assessment.clarification_prompt,
            "pending_user_input": None,  # 无论何种分支，澄清一旦成功应用，均视为已消费修改意见
            "confirmed": project_confirmed(new_q),
        }
    else:
        new_q = rebuild_question(
            question,
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
            clarified_question=assessment.clarified_question,
            reality_constraints=assessment.reality_constraints,
        )
        return {
            **cleaned_state,
            "question": new_q,
            "clarification": None,
            "pending_user_input": None,  # 成功形成候选问题，清空 pending 缓冲
            "confirmed": project_confirmed(new_q),
        }


def apply_confirmation_response(
    state: LiuyaoState,
    response: ConfirmationResponse,
) -> LiuyaoState:
    """根据用户的 ConfirmationResponse 驱动状态机更新（CONFIRM / MODIFY / CANCEL）"""
    validate_confirmation_projection(state)
    question: DecisionQuestion = state["question"]
    current_status = question.status

    # 1. 处理 CONFIRM
    if response.action == ConfirmationAction.CONFIRM:
        if current_status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED:
            return state
        if current_status != DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"当前状态 {current_status} 不允许直接确认")

        new_q = rebuild_question(question, status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED)
        return {
            **state,
            "question": new_q,
            "confirmed": project_confirmed(new_q),
        }

    # 2. 处理 MODIFY
    elif response.action == ConfirmationAction.MODIFY:
        if current_status not in {
            DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
            DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
        }:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"当前状态 {current_status} 不支持修改")

        cleaned_state = invalidate_downstream(state)
        new_q = rebuild_question(
            question,
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
            clarified_question=None,
        )

        modification = response.modification
        return {
            **cleaned_state,
            "question": new_q,
            "clarification": (None if modification is not None else "请问您希望如何修改或补充当前问题？"),
            "pending_user_input": modification,
            "confirmed": project_confirmed(new_q),
        }

    # 3. 处理 CANCEL
    elif response.action == ConfirmationAction.CANCEL:
        if current_status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED:
            return state
        if current_status not in {
            DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
            DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
        }:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"当前状态 {current_status} 无法取消")

        cleaned_state = invalidate_downstream(state)
        new_q = rebuild_question(question, status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED)
        return {
            **cleaned_state,
            "question": new_q,
            "confirmed": project_confirmed(new_q),
        }

    raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"未知的确认动作: {response.action}")


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\retrieval.py
================================================================================

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDomain, RetrievalFilter, RetrievalResult
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import RetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import HexagramResult

LIUYAO_RETRIEVAL_FILTER = RetrievalFilter(domains=frozenset({KnowledgeDomain.LIUYAO, KnowledgeDomain.YIJING}))


def build_liuyao_queries(question_text: str, hexagram: HexagramResult) -> list[str]:
    queries = []

    # 1. 本卦查询
    p_hex = hexagram.primary_hexagram
    q1 = f"{p_hex.upper_trigram}上{p_hex.lower_trigram}下"
    if p_hex.name:
        q1 += f" {p_hex.name}卦"
    queries.append(f"卦象含义 {q1}")

    # 2. 动爻查询
    if hexagram.moving_line_positions:
        moves = "、".join([f"第{pos}爻动" for pos in hexagram.moving_line_positions])
        c_hex = hexagram.changed_hexagram
        c_str = f"变卦{c_hex.upper_trigram}上{c_hex.lower_trigram}下" if c_hex else ""
        queries.append(f"爻辞与动爻解析 {moves} {c_str}".strip())

    # 3. 事象映射
    queries.append(f"六爻决策参考 测事 测{question_text}")

    return queries


class LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder:
    def __init__(self, retrieval_service: RetrievalService):
        self._retrieval_service = retrieval_service

    async def retrieve(self, question_text: str, hexagram: HexagramResult) -> list[RetrievalResult]:
        queries = build_liuyao_queries(question_text, hexagram)
        all_results = []
        seen = set()

        for query in queries:
            results = await self._retrieval_service.retrieve(query, filters=LIUYAO_RETRIEVAL_FILTER)
            for res in results:
                if res.chunk_id not in seen:
                    seen.add(res.chunk_id)
                    all_results.append(res)

        return all_results


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\state.py
================================================================================

from typing import TypedDict

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import Citation, RetrievalResult
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import CastingMethod, DecisionQuestion, DecisionReflection, HexagramResult, LineValue


class LiuyaoState(TypedDict):
    """
    LangGraph 工作流的运行时状态 (Orchestration State)。
    """

    # 业务侧的 Source of Truth
    question: DecisionQuestion

    # 当前等待用户回答的系统澄清提示语（不需要保留历史）
    clarification: str | None

    # Transient Field：用于搭载 MODIFY 后用户修改/补充的内容，供下轮 clarify 消费
    pending_user_input: str | None

    # Routing 投影，必须始终保持与 question.status == CONFIRMED 同步
    confirmed: bool

    # 起卦策略，连接 Task 2 的确定性执行
    casting_method: CastingMethod
    manual_lines: tuple[LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue, LineValue] | None

    # 确定性代码产生的排盘事实
    hexagram: HexagramResult | None

    # Transient: 传递给 Interpretation 节点的完整证据原文
    retrieved_context: list[RetrievalResult]

    # Transient: LLM 生成的结构化反思草稿
    reflection: DecisionReflection | None

    # 标准的引文溯源
    citations: list[Citation]

    # 最终发送给用户的决策反思文本
    final_response: str | None


def create_initial_liuyao_state(original_question: str) -> LiuyaoState:
    """
    纯函数：创建初始的六爻工作流状态。
    """
    question = DecisionQuestion(
        original_question=original_question,
    )

    return {
        "question": question,
        "clarification": None,
        "pending_user_input": None,
        "confirmed": False,
        "casting_method": CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN,
        "manual_lines": None,
        "hexagram": None,
        "retrieved_context": [],
        "reflection": None,
        "citations": [],
        "final_response": None,
    }

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\liuyao\workflow.py

import re

from langgraph.checkpoint.base import BaseCheckpointSaver
from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import InMemorySaver
from langgraph.graph import END, START, StateGraph
from langgraph.types import interrupt
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import Citation
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.casting import HexagramCastingService, ManualCastingInput
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import LiuyaoPolicyError
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.formatter import format_evidence_for_prompt, format_hexagram_facts_for_prompt
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import (
    ClarificationAssessment,
    ConfirmationResponse,
    apply_clarification_assessment,
    apply_confirmation_response,
    can_cast,
)
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.retrieval import LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import LiuyaoState
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import CastingMethod, DecisionQuestionStatus, DecisionReflection
from guanchen_v2.prompts.liuyao_agent import (
    LIUYAO_CLARIFICATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    LIUYAO_CLARIFICATION_USER_TEMPLATE,
    LIUYAO_INTERPRETATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    LIUYAO_INTERPRETATION_USER_TEMPLATE,
)

_EVIDENCE_REF_PATTERN = re.compile(r"\[Evidence\s+(\d+)\]")


def validate_evidence_references(reflection: DecisionReflection, citations: list[Citation]) -> None:
    """验证 DecisionReflection 中 [Evidence N] 的引用是否真实存在"""
    refs = {int(match) for match in _EVIDENCE_REF_PATTERN.findall(reflection.traditional_reflection)}

    max_index = len(citations)
    invalid_refs = sorted(ref for ref in refs if ref < 1 or ref > max_index)

    if invalid_refs:
        raise LiuyaoPolicyError("DecisionReflection 包含不存在的 Evidence 引用")


class LiuyaoAgentRuntime:
    def __init__(
        self,
        casting_service: HexagramCastingService,
        llm_client: LLMClient,
        retrieval_builder: LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder,
        checkpointer: BaseCheckpointSaver | None = None
    ):
        self._casting_service = casting_service
        self._llm_client = llm_client
        self._retrieval_builder = retrieval_builder
        self._checkpointer = checkpointer or InMemorySaver()

    async def clarify_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        q = state["question"]
        pending = state.get("pending_user_input", "")

        user_prompt = LIUYAO_CLARIFICATION_USER_TEMPLATE.format(
            original_question=q.original_question,
            reality_constraints="、".join(q.reality_constraints) if q.reality_constraints else "无",
            pending_user_input=pending if pending else "无",
        )

        assessment = await self._llm_client.generate_structured(
            system_prompt=LIUYAO_CLARIFICATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT, user_prompt=user_prompt, schema=ClarificationAssessment
        )

        return apply_clarification_assessment(state, assessment)

    def confirmation_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        q = state["question"]
        resume_payload = interrupt(
            {
                "type": "liuyao_confirmation",
                "question": q.clarified_question,
                "reality_constraints": q.reality_constraints,
                "allowed_actions": ["confirm", "modify", "cancel"],
            }
        )
        try:
            response = ConfirmationResponse.model_validate(resume_payload)
        except ValidationError as exc:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("无效的六爻确认响应") from exc
        return apply_confirmation_response(state, response)

    def cast_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        if not can_cast(state):
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("状态机未就绪或未通过用户确认，拒绝执行起卦操作")

        method = state.get("casting_method")
        if method == CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES:
            manual_lines = state.get("manual_lines")
            if manual_lines is None or len(manual_lines) != 6:
                raise LiuyaoPolicyError("手工起卦必须提供完整 manual_lines")
            hexagram = self._casting_service.cast_manual(ManualCastingInput(lines=manual_lines))
        elif method == CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN:
            hexagram = self._casting_service.cast_random()
        else:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"不支持的起卦方式: {method}")

        return {"hexagram": hexagram}

    async def retrieve_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        if not can_cast(state):
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("未通过确认检查，禁止检索")

        hexagram = state.get("hexagram")
        if not hexagram:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("缺少 HexagramResult，无法执行六爻知识检索")

        q = state["question"]
        if q.clarified_question is None:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("缺少已确认的 clarified_question，无法执行六爻知识检索")

        results = await self._retrieval_builder.retrieve(q.clarified_question, hexagram)
        citations = [Citation.from_retrieval_result(r) for r in results]

        return {"retrieved_context": results, "citations": citations}

    async def interpret_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        if not can_cast(state):
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("未通过确认检查，禁止生成六爻决策反思")

        q = state["question"]
        hexagram = state.get("hexagram")

        if hexagram is None:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("缺少 HexagramResult，无法生成六爻决策反思")
        if q.clarified_question is None:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("缺少已确认的 clarified_question")

        evidence_results = state.get("retrieved_context", [])

        facts_str = format_hexagram_facts_for_prompt(hexagram)
        evidence_str = format_evidence_for_prompt(evidence_results)
        constraints_str = "、".join(q.reality_constraints) if q.reality_constraints else "无"

        user_prompt = LIUYAO_INTERPRETATION_USER_TEMPLATE.format(
            question=q.clarified_question,
            reality_constraints=constraints_str,
            hexagram_facts=facts_str,
            evidence=evidence_str,
        )

        reflection = await self._llm_client.generate_structured(
            system_prompt=LIUYAO_INTERPRETATION_SYSTEM_PROMPT, user_prompt=user_prompt, schema=DecisionReflection
        )
        return {"reflection": reflection}

    def finalize_node(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> dict:
        reflection = state.get("reflection")
        if not reflection:
            raise LiuyaoPolicyError("缺失 DecisionReflection，无法渲染最终回复")

        citations = state.get("citations", [])

        # 确定性防伪造校验
        validate_evidence_references(reflection, citations)

        parts = [
            f"### 【现状评估】\n{reflection.situation_summary}\n",
            f"### 【文化推演】\n{reflection.traditional_reflection}\n",
            f"### 【现实校验】\n{reflection.reality_check}\n",
            "### 【决策参考】",
        ]

        for opt in reflection.decision_options:
            parts.append(f"- {opt}")

        parts.append(f"\n> *注：{reflection.uncertainty_note}*")

        if citations:
            parts.append("\n### 【参考来源】")
            for idx, cite in enumerate(citations, start=1):
                parts.append(f"[Evidence {idx}] {cite.source_title} - {cite.section}")

        return {"final_response": "\n".join(parts)}

    def route_after_clarify(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> str:
        status = state["question"].status
        if status == DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION:
            return END
        elif status == DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION:
            return "confirmation"

        raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"clarify 后出现非法状态 (状态机断裂): {status}")

    def route_after_confirmation(self, state: LiuyaoState) -> str:
        status = state["question"].status
        if status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED:
            return "cast"
        elif status == DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION:
            return "clarify"
        elif status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED:
            return END

        raise LiuyaoPolicyError(f"confirmation 后出现非法状态 (状态机断裂): {status}")

    def build_graph(self) -> StateGraph:
        builder = StateGraph(LiuyaoState)

        builder.add_node("clarify", self.clarify_node)
        builder.add_node("confirmation", self.confirmation_node)
        builder.add_node("cast", self.cast_node)
        builder.add_node("retrieve", self.retrieve_node)
        builder.add_node("interpret", self.interpret_node)
        builder.add_node("finalize", self.finalize_node)

        builder.add_edge(START, "clarify")

        builder.add_conditional_edges("clarify", self.route_after_clarify)
        builder.add_conditional_edges("confirmation", self.route_after_confirmation)

        builder.add_edge("cast", "retrieve")
        builder.add_edge("retrieve", "interpret")
        builder.add_edge("interpret", "finalize")
        builder.add_edge("finalize", END)

        return builder.compile(checkpointer=self._checkpointer)

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\llm\client.py

from typing import Any, TypeVar

from langchain_core.exceptions import OutputParserException
from langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel
from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage
from langchain_core.runnables import RunnableLambda
from openai import APIConnectionError, APIError, APIStatusError
from pydantic import BaseModel, ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings
from guanchen_v2.llm.exceptions import (
    LLMEmptyResponseError,
    LLMError,
    LLMProviderError,
    LLMResponseError,
    LLMRetryExhaustedError,
    LLMStructuredOutputError,
    LLMTransientProviderError,
)
from guanchen_v2.llm.provider import build_chat_model

StructuredT = TypeVar(
    "StructuredT",
    bound=BaseModel,
)


def _translate_provider_error(
    exc: Exception,
) -> LLMError:
    """
    将第三方异常转换为 GuanChen 稳定 LLM 异常。

    重要约束：
    - 不把 str(exc) 拼入稳定项目错误消息；
    - Provider 原始异常通过 exception chaining 保存在 __cause__；
    - 对外消息只表达应用需要知道的错误类别。
    """
    if isinstance(
        exc,
        (
            OutputParserException,
            ValidationError,
        ),
    ):
        return LLMStructuredOutputError("结构化输出失败")

    if isinstance(
        exc,
        APIConnectionError,
    ):
        return LLMTransientProviderError("LLM 网络连接失败")

    if isinstance(
        exc,
        APIStatusError,
    ):
        if (
            exc.status_code
            in (
                408,
                409,
                429,
            )
            or exc.status_code >= 500
        ):
            return LLMTransientProviderError("LLM 服务暂时不可用")

        return LLMProviderError("LLM 服务请求失败")

    if isinstance(
        exc,
        APIError,
    ):
        return LLMProviderError("LLM API 调用失败")

    return LLMProviderError("LLM 调用失败")


class LLMClient:
    def __init__(
        self,
        model: BaseChatModel,
        max_retries: int = 0,
        *,
        retry_wait_exponential_jitter: bool = True,
    ) -> None:
        self._model = model
        self._max_retries = max_retries
        self._retry_jitter = retry_wait_exponential_jitter

    @classmethod
    def from_settings(
        cls,
        settings: Settings,
    ) -> "LLMClient":
        model = build_chat_model(settings=settings)

        return cls(
            model=model,
            max_retries=(settings.llm_max_retries),
        )

    def _invoke_text_once(
        self,
        prompt: str,
    ) -> str:
        try:
            response = self._model.invoke(prompt)

            if not isinstance(
                response.content,
                str,
            ):
                raise LLMResponseError("模型调用返回了非文本内容")

            if not response.content.split():
                raise LLMEmptyResponseError("返回了空字符串结果")

            return response.content

        except (
            LLMResponseError,
            LLMEmptyResponseError,
        ):
            raise

        except Exception as exc:
            raise _translate_provider_error(exc) from exc

    def text(
        self,
        prompt: str,
    ) -> str:
        runnable = RunnableLambda(self._invoke_text_once).with_retry(
            retry_if_exception_type=(
                LLMTransientProviderError,
                LLMEmptyResponseError,
            ),
            wait_exponential_jitter=(self._retry_jitter),
            stop_after_attempt=(self._max_retries + 1),
        )

        try:
            return runnable.invoke(prompt)

        except LLMTransientProviderError as exc:
            raise LLMRetryExhaustedError("文本生成重试次数已耗尽") from exc

    def _invoke_structured_once(
        self,
        payload: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> StructuredT:
        prompt: str = payload["prompt"]
        schema: type[StructuredT] = payload["schema"]

        try:
            structured_llm = self._model.with_structured_output(schema)

            result = structured_llm.invoke(prompt)

            if result is None:
                raise LLMEmptyResponseError("结构化输出返回了空结果")

            if not isinstance(
                result,
                schema,
            ):
                raise LLMStructuredOutputError(f"结构化输出类型不匹配，预期 Schema: {schema.__name__}")

            return result

        except (
            LLMEmptyResponseError,
            LLMStructuredOutputError,
        ):
            raise

        except Exception as exc:
            raise _translate_provider_error(exc) from exc

    def structured(
        self,
        prompt: str,
        schema: type[StructuredT],
    ) -> StructuredT:
        runnable = RunnableLambda(self._invoke_structured_once).with_retry(
            retry_if_exception_type=(
                LLMTransientProviderError,
                LLMEmptyResponseError,
                LLMStructuredOutputError,
            ),
            wait_exponential_jitter=(self._retry_jitter),
            stop_after_attempt=(self._max_retries + 1),
        )

        try:
            return runnable.invoke(
                {
                    "prompt": prompt,
                    "schema": schema,
                }
            )

        except LLMTransientProviderError as exc:
            raise LLMRetryExhaustedError("结构化输出重试次数已耗尽") from exc

    async def _ainvoke_structured_once(
        self,
        payload: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> StructuredT:
        system_prompt: str = payload["system_prompt"]
        user_prompt: str = payload["user_prompt"]
        schema: type[StructuredT] = payload["schema"]

        messages = [
            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
            HumanMessage(content=user_prompt),
        ]

        try:
            structured_llm = self._model.with_structured_output(schema)

            result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(messages)

            if result is None:
                raise LLMEmptyResponseError("结构化输出返回了空结果")

            if not isinstance(
                result,
                schema,
            ):
                raise LLMStructuredOutputError(f"结构化输出类型不匹配，预期 Schema: {schema.__name__}")

            return result

        except (
            LLMEmptyResponseError,
            LLMStructuredOutputError,
        ):
            raise

        except Exception as exc:
            raise _translate_provider_error(exc) from exc

    async def generate_structured(
        self,
        *,
        system_prompt: str,
        user_prompt: str,
        schema: type[StructuredT],
    ) -> StructuredT:
        runnable = RunnableLambda(self._ainvoke_structured_once).with_retry(
            retry_if_exception_type=(
                LLMTransientProviderError,
                LLMEmptyResponseError,
                LLMStructuredOutputError,
            ),
            wait_exponential_jitter=(self._retry_jitter),
            stop_after_attempt=(self._max_retries + 1),
        )

        try:
            return await runnable.ainvoke(
                {
                    "system_prompt": (system_prompt),
                    "user_prompt": (user_prompt),
                    "schema": schema,
                }
            )

        except LLMTransientProviderError as exc:
            raise LLMRetryExhaustedError("结构化输出重试次数已耗尽") from exc

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\llm\exceptions.py

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError


class LLMError(ProjectError):
    """
    GuanChen LLM 基础设施异常根类型。

    所有稳定的大模型调用异常都应继承该类型。
    """

    pass


class LLMProviderError(LLMError):
    """
    LLM Provider 不可重试错误。

    例如：
    - 非瞬时 API 请求错误
    - 非法模型配置
    - Provider 明确拒绝请求
    """

    pass


class LLMTransientProviderError(LLMProviderError):
    """
    LLM Provider 瞬时错误。

    例如：
    - 网络连接失败
    - 429
    - 5xx
    - 临时服务不可用

    Retry policy 由 LLMClient 负责。
    """

    pass


class LLMResponseError(LLMError):
    """
    Provider 调用已经完成，
    但返回结果不满足 GuanChen 应用层契约。
    """

    pass


class LLMEmptyResponseError(LLMResponseError):
    """LLM 没有返回应用期望的有效内容。"""

    pass


class LLMStructuredOutputError(LLMResponseError):
    """LLM structured output 不满足目标 Schema。"""

    pass


class LLMRetryExhaustedError(LLMError):
    """可重试错误达到最大尝试次数后仍未恢复。"""

    pass


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\llm\provider.py
================================================================================

from langchain_core.language_models import BaseChatModel
from langchain_deepseek import ChatDeepSeek

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import Settings


def build_chat_model(settings: Settings) -> BaseChatModel:
    """
    基础对话模型构建工厂 (Model Construction)。

    提供给 LLMClient、Agent Runtime 共享使用的统一基座。
    注意：
    - 这里严格设置 max_retries=0，禁止底层 Provider 偷偷重试；
    - 所有的 Retry 和重试策略均由各自的应用方（如 LLMClient 或 Agent）自行管理。
    - 在 extra_body 中禁用 deepseek 的 thinking 模式。
    """
    return ChatDeepSeek(
        model=settings.deepseek_model,
        api_key=settings.deepseek_api_key.get_secret_value(),
        temperature=settings.llm_temperature,
        timeout=settings.llm_timeout_seconds,
        max_retries=0,
        extra_body={"thinking": {"type": "disabled"}},
    )


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\memory\checkpointer.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 8 LangGraph PostgreSQL Checkpointer 封装。

设计约束（冻结规划）：
- 直接使用官方 langgraph-checkpoint-postgres，不手写 checkpoint engine；
- checkpointer 只负责 LangGraph thread state，与长期用户资料（SQLAlchemy）严格分层。
"""
import re

from langgraph.checkpoint.postgres.aio import AsyncPostgresSaver
from psycopg_pool import AsyncConnectionPool

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import DatabaseSettings

_ASYNC_PREFIX = re.compile(r"^postgresql\+asyncpg://")


def to_psycopg_conninfo(database_url: str) -> str:
    """asyncpg URL 转换为 psycopg 可用的连接串（去掉 +asyncpg 方言前缀）。"""
    return _ASYNC_PREFIX.sub("postgresql://", database_url)


class PostgresCheckpointerFactory:
    """创建官方 AsyncPostgresSaver 的唯一入口。"""

    @staticmethod
    async def create(
        database_url: str | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[AsyncPostgresSaver, AsyncConnectionPool]:
        """
        创建并初始化（setup）一个连接 PostgreSQL 的 AsyncPostgresSaver。

        返回 (saver, pool)：pool 必须由调用方在进程结束时负责 close。
        """
        url = database_url or DatabaseSettings().database_url

        pool = AsyncConnectionPool(
            conninfo=to_psycopg_conninfo(url),
            kwargs={"autocommit": True},
            open=False,
        )
        await pool.open()

        saver = AsyncPostgresSaver(pool)
        await saver.setup()

        return saver, pool

    @staticmethod
    async def close(pool: AsyncConnectionPool) -> None:
        await pool.close()


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\memory\database.py
================================================================================

from collections.abc import AsyncGenerator

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import (
    AsyncEngine,
    AsyncSession,
    async_sessionmaker,
    create_async_engine,
)

from guanchen_v2.config.settings import DatabaseSettings


def create_async_engine_and_factory(
    database_url: str | None = None,
) -> tuple[AsyncEngine, async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]]:
    """
    创建独立的 AsyncEngine + sessionmaker。

    每个调用都会得到一套全新的 Engine / Factory：
    - 长时间运行的进程（scripts / FastAPI）在启动时创建一次即可；
    - 测试必须为每个测试函数创建独立实例，并在测试结束前 dispose，
      避免 asyncpg 连接池跨 event loop 复用导致
      "another operation is in progress" / "Event loop is closed" 类错误。
    """
    url = database_url or DatabaseSettings().database_url

    engine = create_async_engine(
        url,
        echo=False,
    )

    factory = async_sessionmaker(
        bind=engine,
        expire_on_commit=False,
    )

    return engine, factory


database_settings = DatabaseSettings()

# 进程级默认实例：供 scripts / 应用运行期使用。
engine, async_session_factory = create_async_engine_and_factory(
    database_settings.database_url
)


async def get_session() -> AsyncGenerator[AsyncSession, None]:
    """
    FastAPI / 业务代码依赖注入入口。

    注意：依赖本生成器的请求必须运行在同一个 event loop 内。
    """
    async with async_session_factory() as session:
        yield session

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\memory\exceptions.py

from guanchen_v2.core.errors import ProjectError


class MemoryError(ProjectError):
    """Memory Store 基础异常"""
    pass

class MemoryNotFoundError(MemoryError):
    """
    更新或读取时，Session 不存在。
    注意：load() 允许返回 None，但 update() 必须抛出此异常。
    """
    pass

class MemoryAlreadyExistsError(MemoryError):
    """
    创建（save）Session 时，指定的 Session ID 已存在。
    """
    pass

class MemoryPersistenceError(MemoryError):
    """
    未来供数据库后端使用，例如序列化失败、网络故障或数据库崩溃。
    """
    pass

from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import (
    DateTime,
    ForeignKey,
    Index,
    Integer,
    String,
    Text,
    UniqueConstraint,
    func,
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import (
    DeclarativeBase,
    Mapped,
    mapped_column,
)


class MemoryBase(DeclarativeBase):
    pass


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 会话状态（Application 层恢复容器）
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class ConversationStateRecord(MemoryBase):

    __tablename__ = "conversation_states"

    session_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(128),
        primary_key=True,
    )

    state_json: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text,
        nullable=False,
    )

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
    )

    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        onupdate=func.now(),
    )

class UserProfileRow(MemoryBase):
    """用户主体。v2.0 采用匿名 session 身份，user_id 即会话 / 匿名身份标识。"""

    __tablename__ = "user_profile"

    user_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(128),
        primary_key=True,
    )

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
    )

    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        onupdate=func.now(),
    )


class BirthProfileRow(MemoryBase):
    """
    用户明确提供的出生资料（唯一有效事实）。

    更正语义（Stage 8）：
    - 每个用户只保留一条“当前有效”出生资料；
    - 后写的明确更正覆盖当前事实（upsert），并刷新 updated_at；
    - v2.0 不做完整版本树。
    """

    __tablename__ = "birth_profile"
    __table_args__ = (
        UniqueConstraint(
            "user_id",
            name="uq_birth_profile_user_id",
        ),
    )

    user_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(128),
        ForeignKey("user_profile.user_id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
        primary_key=True,
    )

    year: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    month: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    day: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    hour: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    minute: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False)
    gender: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)
    birth_place_text: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(512),
        nullable=True,
    )

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
    )

    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        onupdate=func.now(),
    )


class ConversationRow(MemoryBase):

    )


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\memory\repositories.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 8 长期用户资料 Repository 层。

设计约束（冻结规划）：
- 只为真实持久化需求创建 repository，不做抽象接口层套娃；
- 只保存“用户明确提供 / 确认”的结构化资料；
- 后写的明确更正覆盖当前有效事实（upsert），并保留 updated_at。
"""
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import delete, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import async_sessionmaker

from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import MessageState
from guanchen_v2.memory.exceptions import MemoryPersistenceError
from guanchen_v2.memory.models import (
    BirthProfileRow,
    ConversationRow,
    DecisionRecordRow,
    DreamRecordRow,
    MessageRow,
    UserProfileRow,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import DreamRecord
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import DecisionRecord


def _now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)


class UserProfileRepository:
    """用户主体的幂等创建 / 查询。"""

    def __init__(self, session_factory: async_sessionmaker) -> None:
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def get_or_create(self, user_id: str) -> UserProfileRow:
        try:
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                result = await session.execute(
                    select(UserProfileRow).where(UserProfileRow.user_id == user_id)
                )
                row = result.scalar_one_or_none()
                if row is not None:
                    return row

                row = UserProfileRow(user_id=user_id)
                session.add(row)
                await session.commit()
                return row
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("用户档案写入失败") from exc


async def _ensure_user_async(
    session_factory: async_sessionmaker,
    user_id: str,
) -> None:
    """写入用户域数据前，确保 FK 父行 user_profile 存在（幂等）。"""
    user_repo = UserProfileRepository(session_factory)
    await user_repo.get_or_create(user_id)


class BirthProfileRepository:
    """出生资料：当前唯一有效事实 + 用户更正语义。"""

    def __init__(self, session_factory: async_sessionmaker) -> None:
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    @staticmethod
    def _row_to_domain(row: BirthProfileRow) -> BirthProfile:
        return BirthProfile(
            year=row.year,
            month=row.month,
            day=row.day,
            hour=row.hour,
            minute=row.minute,
            gender=Gender(row.gender),
            birth_place_text=row.birth_place_text,
        )

    async def get(self, user_id: str) -> BirthProfile | None:
        try:
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                result = await session.execute(
                    select(BirthProfileRow).where(BirthProfileRow.user_id == user_id)
                )
                row = result.scalar_one_or_none()
                if row is None:
                    return None
                return self._row_to_domain(row)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("出生资料读取失败") from exc

    async def upsert(self, user_id: str, profile: BirthProfile) -> None:
        """
        用户更正语义：存在则覆盖为最新事实，不存在则创建。
        单用户单行（唯一约束），刷新 updated_at。
        """
        now = _now()
        try:
            await _ensure_user_async(self._session_factory, user_id)
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                result = await session.execute(
                    select(BirthProfileRow).where(BirthProfileRow.user_id == user_id)
                )
                row = result.scalar_one_or_none()

                if row is None:
                    row = BirthProfileRow(
                        user_id=user_id,
                        year=profile.year,
                        month=profile.month,
                        day=profile.day,
                        hour=profile.hour,
                        minute=profile.minute,
                        gender=profile.gender.value,
                        birth_place_text=profile.birth_place_text,
                        created_at=now,
                        updated_at=now,
                    )
                    session.add(row)
                else:
                    row.year = profile.year
                    row.month = profile.month
                    row.day = profile.day
                    row.hour = profile.hour
                    row.minute = profile.minute
                    row.gender = profile.gender.value
                    row.birth_place_text = profile.birth_place_text
                    row.updated_at = now

                await session.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("出生资料写入失败") from exc

    async def delete(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        """幂等删除，用于测试隔离 / 用户主动清除资料。"""
        try:
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                await session.execute(
                    delete(BirthProfileRow).where(BirthProfileRow.user_id == user_id)
                )
                await session.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("出生资料删除失败") from exc


class ConversationRepository:
    """会话与消息的追加式持久化。"""

    def __init__(self, session_factory: async_sessionmaker) -> None:
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def create(self, conversation_id: str, user_id: str) -> None:
        try:
            await _ensure_user_async(self._session_factory, user_id)
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                existing = await session.execute(
                    select(ConversationRow).where(
                        ConversationRow.conversation_id == conversation_id
                    )
                )
                if existing.scalar_one_or_none() is not None:
                    return

                session.add(
                    ConversationRow(
                        conversation_id=conversation_id,
                        user_id=user_id,
                    )
                )
                await session.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("会话创建失败") from exc

    async def append_message(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        role: str,
        content: str,
    ) -> None:
        if role not in {"user", "assistant", "system"}:
            raise ValueError("Message role 必须是 user, assistant 或 system")

        try:
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                session.add(
                    MessageRow(
                        conversation_id=conversation_id,
                        role=role,
                        content=content,
                    )
                )
                await session.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("消息写入失败") from exc

    async def get_recent_messages(
        self,
        conversation_id: str,
        limit: int = 20,
    ) -> list[MessageState]:
        try:
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                result = await session.execute(
                    select(MessageRow)
                    .where(MessageRow.conversation_id == conversation_id)
                    .order_by(MessageRow.created_at.asc(), MessageRow.message_id.asc())
                    .limit(limit)
                )
                rows = result.scalars().all()

            return [
                MessageState(
                    role=row.role,
                    content=row.content,
                    timestamp=row.created_at,
                )
                for row in rows
            ]
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("会话消息读取失败") from exc


class DreamRecordRepository:
    """解梦域当前 DreamRecord 的保存 / 读取（按 user 取最新）。"""

    def __init__(self, session_factory: async_sessionmaker) -> None:
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def save(self, user_id: str, record: DreamRecord) -> None:
        try:
            now = _now()
            await _ensure_user_async(self._session_factory, user_id)
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                result = await session.execute(
                    select(DreamRecordRow).where(
                        DreamRecordRow.record_id == str(record.dream_id)
                    )
                )
                row = result.scalar_one_or_none()

                if row is None:
                    session.add(
                        DreamRecordRow(
                            record_id=str(record.dream_id),
                            user_id=user_id,
                            record_json=record.model_dump_json(),
                            created_at=now,
                            updated_at=now,
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    row.record_json = record.model_dump_json()
                    row.updated_at = now

                await session.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("梦境记录写入失败") from exc

    async def get_latest(self, user_id: str) -> DreamRecord | None:
        try:
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                result = await session.execute(
                    select(DreamRecordRow)
                    .where(DreamRecordRow.user_id == user_id)
                    .order_by(
                        DreamRecordRow.updated_at.desc(),
                        DreamRecordRow.created_at.desc(),
                    )
                    .limit(1)
                )
                row = result.scalar_one_or_none()
                if row is None:
                    return None
                return DreamRecord.model_validate_json(row.record_json)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("梦境记录读取失败") from exc


class DecisionRecordRepository:
    """六爻决策域当前 DecisionRecord 的保存 / 读取（按 user 取最新）。"""

    def __init__(self, session_factory: async_sessionmaker) -> None:
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def save(self, user_id: str, record: DecisionRecord) -> None:
        try:
            now = _now()
            await _ensure_user_async(self._session_factory, user_id)
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                result = await session.execute(
                    select(DecisionRecordRow).where(
                        DecisionRecordRow.record_id == str(record.record_id)
                    )
                )
                row = result.scalar_one_or_none()

                if row is None:
                    session.add(
                        DecisionRecordRow(
                            record_id=str(record.record_id),
                            user_id=user_id,
                            thread_id=record.thread_id,
                            record_json=record.model_dump_json(),
                            created_at=now,
                            updated_at=now,
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    row.thread_id = record.thread_id
                    row.record_json = record.model_dump_json()
                    row.updated_at = now

                await session.commit()
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("决策记录写入失败") from exc

    async def get_latest(self, user_id: str) -> DecisionRecord | None:
        try:
            async with self._session_factory() as session:
                result = await session.execute(
                    select(DecisionRecordRow)
                    .where(DecisionRecordRow.user_id == user_id)
                    .order_by(
                        DecisionRecordRow.updated_at.desc(),
                        DecisionRecordRow.created_at.desc(),
                    )
                    .limit(1)
                )
                row = result.scalar_one_or_none()
                if row is None:
                    return None
                return DecisionRecord.model_validate_json(row.record_json)
        except Exception as exc:
            raise MemoryPersistenceError("决策记录读取失败") from exc


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\memory\selector.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 8 Memory Selector：按领域选择长期记忆，实现 namespace / domain 隔离。

设计约束（冻结规划）：
- 八字只读取 BirthProfile + 相关近期对话；
- 解梦读取当前 DreamRecord（由 DreamAgentRuntime.record_store 在运行时维护）；
- 六爻读取当前 DecisionRecord（由 LiuyaoAgentAdapter.record_store 在运行时维护）；
- 建立 Memory namespace / domain 隔离，防止梦境上下文污染八字分析；
- Memory Selector 不把全历史无脑塞给模型。
"""
from typing import Protocol

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import async_sessionmaker

from guanchen_v2.memory.repositories import (
    BirthProfileRepository,
    DecisionRecordRepository,
    DreamRecordRepository,
    UserProfileRepository,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import DreamRecord
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import DecisionQuestionStatus, DecisionRecord
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext

_PENDING_QUESTION_STATUSES = {
    DecisionQuestionStatus.DRAFT,
    DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
    DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
}


class MemoryPersistence(Protocol):
    """领域记忆持久化的最小契约（供运行时代入）。"""

    async def load_latest(self) -> object | None: ...

    async def save(self, record: object) -> None: ...


class MemorySelector:
    """
    长期记忆的领域选择器。

    每个 intent 只能看到自己命名空间内的数据：
    - BAZI       -> BirthProfile（唯一长期用户事实）
    - DREAM      -> 运行时由 DreamRecordStore 维护，选择器不注入上下文
    - LIUYAO     -> 运行时由 DecisionRecordStore / checkpointer 维护
    - KNOWLEDGE  -> 无需用户记忆
    """

    def __init__(self, session_factory: async_sessionmaker) -> None:
        self._session_factory = session_factory
        self._user_repo = UserProfileRepository(session_factory)
        self._birth_repo = BirthProfileRepository(session_factory)
        self._dream_repo = DreamRecordRepository(session_factory)
        self._decision_repo = DecisionRecordRepository(session_factory)

    async def ensure_user(self, user_id: str) -> None:
        """幂等建立匿名用户主体。"""
        await self._user_repo.get_or_create(user_id)

    async def select_for_intent(
        self,
        intent: IntentType,
        user_id: str,
    ) -> RequirementContext:
        """
        按领域选择本次请求应注入的长期记忆。

        领域隔离在这里被结构性保证：每个分支只读取自己的 repository，
        梦境 / 六爻数据永远不会出现在 Bazi 的 context 中。
        """
        if intent == IntentType.BAZI:
            profile = await self._birth_repo.get(user_id)
            if profile is not None:
                return RequirementContext(
                    available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: profile}
                )
            return RequirementContext(available_data={})

        if intent == IntentType.LIUYAO:
            # 六爻读取当前 DecisionRecord：仅当存在未完成的决策（中断 / 待确认）
            # 时注入问题种子，使恢复流程无需用户重新提供问题。
            record = await self._decision_repo.get_latest(user_id)
            if record is not None and record.question.status in _PENDING_QUESTION_STATUSES:
                seed = (
                    record.question.clarified_question
                    or record.question.original_question
                )
                if seed:
                    return RequirementContext(
                        available_data={RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION: seed}
                    )
            return RequirementContext(available_data={})

        # DREAM / KNOWLEDGE / CHITCHAT / OUT_OF_SCOPE：
        # 当前轮内容来自用户本轮输入，不注入历史记忆到 requirement context。
        # （解梦的当前 DreamRecord 由 DreamAgentRuntime.record_store 在运行时维护）
        return RequirementContext(available_data={})

    async def persist_birth_profile(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        profile: BirthProfile,
    ) -> None:
        """用户明确提供 / 更正后的出生资料写入长期记忆。"""
        await self._birth_repo.upsert(user_id, profile)

    async def persist_decision_record(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        record: DecisionRecord,
    ) -> None:
        """六爻决策记录写入长期记忆（用户明确提供 / 确认后）。"""
        await self._decision_repo.save(user_id, record)

    async def load_birth_profile(self, user_id: str) -> BirthProfile | None:
        return await self._birth_repo.get(user_id)

    async def load_dream_memory(self, user_id: str) -> DreamRecord | None:
        """解梦域当前 DreamRecord（供 DreamAgentRuntime 恢复 / 测试核验）。"""
        return await self._dream_repo.get_latest(user_id)

    async def load_decision_memory(self, user_id: str) -> DecisionRecord | None:
        """六爻域当前 DecisionRecord（供恢复 / 测试核验）。"""
        return await self._decision_repo.get_latest(user_id)

from typing import Protocol

from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import ConversationState


class MemoryStore(Protocol):
    """
    Session State 的持久化抽象层。
    只负责 CRUD，不理解业务，不修改状态数据。
    """

    async def save(self, state: ConversationState) -> None:
        """
        持久化一个新的 Session State。
        如果 session_id 已存在，必须抛出 MemoryAlreadyExistsError 以防止覆盖。
        """
        ...

    async def load(self, session_id: str) -> ConversationState | None:
        """
        加载指定的 Session State。
        如果不存在，返回 None，而不抛出异常。
        """
        ...

    async def update(self, state: ConversationState) -> None:
        """
        更新已存在的 Session State。
        如果 session_id 不存在，必须抛出 MemoryNotFoundError。
        """
        ...

    async def delete(self, session_id: str) -> None:
        """
        删除指定的 Session State。
        操作应具备幂等性 (Idempotent)，如果不存在则静默成功，不报错。
        """
        ...

"""Stage 8 领域记忆 Store：绑定到具体 user 的持久化适配。

这些 Store 只做“按用户读写领域记录”，不包含业务判断。
DreamAgentRuntime / LiuyaoAgentAdapter 通过它们获得跨进程可恢复的领域记忆。
"""
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import async_sessionmaker

from guanchen_v2.memory.repositories import (
    DecisionRecordRepository,
    DreamRecordRepository,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import DreamRecord
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import DecisionRecord


class PostgresDreamRecordStore:
    """解梦域：按 user 读写当前 DreamRecord。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker,
    ) -> None:
        self._user_id = user_id
        self._repo = DreamRecordRepository(session_factory)

    async def load_latest(self) -> DreamRecord | None:
        return await self._repo.get_latest(self._user_id)

    async def save(self, record: DreamRecord) -> None:
        await self._repo.save(self._user_id, record)


class PostgresDecisionRecordStore:
    """六爻决策域：按 user 读写当前 DecisionRecord。"""

    def __init__(
        self,
        user_id: str,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker,
    ) -> None:
        self._user_id = user_id
        self._repo = DecisionRecordRepository(session_factory)

    async def load_latest(self) -> DecisionRecord | None:
        return await self._repo.get_latest(self._user_id)

    async def save(self, record: DecisionRecord) -> None:
        await self._repo.save(self._user_id, record)

from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Literal, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, computed_field, field_validator, model_validator


class Gender(StrEnum):
    MALE = "male"
    FEMALE = "female"


class HeavenlyStem(StrEnum):
    """天干"""

    JIA = "甲"
    YI = "乙"
    BING = "丙"
    DING = "丁"
    WU = "戊"
    JI = "己"
    GENG = "庚"
    XIN = "辛"
    REN = "壬"
    GUI = "癸"


class EarthlyBranch(StrEnum):
    """地支"""

    ZI = "子"
    CHOU = "丑"
    YIN = "寅"
    MAO = "卯"
    CHEN = "辰"
    SI = "巳"
    WU = "午"
    WEI = "未"
    SHEN = "申"
    YOU = "酉"
    XU = "戌"
    HAI = "亥"


class FiveElement(StrEnum):
    """五行"""

    METAL = "金"
    WOOD = "木"
    WATER = "水"
    FIRE = "火"
    EARTH = "土"


class TenGod(StrEnum):
    """十神"""

    BI_JIAN = "比肩"
    JIE_CAI = "劫财"
    SHI_SHEN = "食神"
    SHANG_GUAN = "伤官"
    PIAN_CAI = "偏财"
    ZHENG_CAI = "正财"
    QI_SHA = "七杀"
    ZHENG_GUAN = "正官"
    PIAN_YIN = "偏印"
    ZHENG_YIN = "正印"


class LuckDirection(StrEnum):
    FORWARD = "forward"
    BACKWARD = "backward"


# --- 领域五行映射（非自研算法，仅属固定映射） ---
STEM_ELEMENT_MAP: dict[HeavenlyStem, FiveElement] = {
    HeavenlyStem.JIA: FiveElement.WOOD,
    HeavenlyStem.YI: FiveElement.WOOD,
    HeavenlyStem.BING: FiveElement.FIRE,
    HeavenlyStem.DING: FiveElement.FIRE,
    HeavenlyStem.WU: FiveElement.EARTH,
    HeavenlyStem.JI: FiveElement.EARTH,
    HeavenlyStem.GENG: FiveElement.METAL,
    HeavenlyStem.XIN: FiveElement.METAL,
    HeavenlyStem.REN: FiveElement.WATER,
    HeavenlyStem.GUI: FiveElement.WATER,
}

BRANCH_ELEMENT_MAP: dict[EarthlyBranch, FiveElement] = {
    EarthlyBranch.ZI: FiveElement.WATER,
    EarthlyBranch.CHOU: FiveElement.EARTH,
    EarthlyBranch.YIN: FiveElement.WOOD,
    EarthlyBranch.MAO: FiveElement.WOOD,
    EarthlyBranch.CHEN: FiveElement.EARTH,
    EarthlyBranch.SI: FiveElement.FIRE,
    EarthlyBranch.WU: FiveElement.FIRE,
    EarthlyBranch.WEI: FiveElement.EARTH,
    EarthlyBranch.SHEN: FiveElement.METAL,
    EarthlyBranch.YOU: FiveElement.METAL,
    EarthlyBranch.XU: FiveElement.EARTH,
    EarthlyBranch.HAI: FiveElement.WATER,
}


class BirthProfile(BaseModel):
    """出生输入事实契约，仅记录客观输入，不包含计算过程参数"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    year: int = Field(ge=1, le=9999, description="公历出生年份")
    month: int = Field(ge=1, le=12, description="公历出生月份")
    day: int = Field(ge=1, le=31, description="公历出生日期")
    hour: int = Field(ge=0, le=23, description="当地民用时小时，0-23")
    minute: int = Field(ge=0, le=59, description="分钟，0-59")
    gender: Gender = Field(description="用于确定大运顺逆的性别参数")
    birth_place_text: Optional[str] = Field(default=None, description="可选出生地原始文本，v2.0 不参与排盘")

    @field_validator("birth_place_text")
    @classmethod
    def clean_birth_place(cls, v: Optional[str]) -> Optional[str]:
        if v is None:
            return None
        stripped = v.strip()
        return stripped if stripped else None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_date_time(
        self,
    ) -> "BirthProfile":
        """
        验证年月日时分组成真实存在的民用时间。

        底层 datetime 异常只作为 cause 保留，
        不进入稳定 validation message。
        """
        try:
            datetime(
                self.year,
                self.month,
                self.day,
                self.hour,
                self.minute,
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise ValueError("非法的日期时间组合，请检查年月日时分。") from exc

        return self


class GanZhi(BaseModel):
    """干支组合值对象"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    stem: HeavenlyStem
    branch: EarthlyBranch

    @computed_field
    @property
    def gan_zhi(self) -> str:
        """派生字段，自动拼接天干地支，避免重复存储与状态不一致"""
        return f"{self.stem.value}{self.branch.value}"


class HiddenStem(BaseModel):
    """藏干与地支十神绑定结构"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    stem: HeavenlyStem
    ten_god: TenGod

    @computed_field
    @property
    def element(self) -> FiveElement:
        """天干所属五行是确定性映射，由模型派生，避免状态矛盾"""
        return STEM_ELEMENT_MAP[self.stem]


class Pillar(BaseModel):
    """四柱结构，整合干支、五行、十神及藏干"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    gan_zhi: GanZhi
    # 日柱的天干自身即为日主，无相对于自身的“十神”概念，故可为 None
    stem_ten_god: Optional[TenGod]
    hidden_stems: list[HiddenStem] = Field(min_length=1)

    @computed_field
    @property
    def stem_element(self) -> FiveElement:
        return STEM_ELEMENT_MAP[self.gan_zhi.stem]

    @computed_field
    @property
    def branch_element(self) -> FiveElement:
        return BRANCH_ELEMENT_MAP[self.gan_zhi.branch]


class BaziCalculationMethod(BaseModel):
    """排盘溯源参数，描述本次排盘基于的底层库和流派规则"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    library: Literal["lunar_python"] = "lunar_python"
    library_version: str = Field(min_length=1)

    """
    四柱晚子时规则
    sect = 2 → 晚子时日柱按当天
    sect = 1 → 晚子时日柱按明天
    """
    late_zi_sect: Literal[2] = 2
    """
    起运时间计算规则
    yun sect = 1 → 按天数和时辰数换算
    yun sect = 2 → 按分钟数换算
    """
    yun_sect: Literal[1] = 1

    time_basis: Literal["local_civil_time_as_provided"] = "local_civil_time_as_provided"

    true_solar_time: Literal[False] = False
    birth_place_affects_calculation: Literal[False] = False


class LuckStartOffset(BaseModel):
    """起运偏移量"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    years: int
    months: int
    days: int
    hours: int


class DaYunPeriod(BaseModel):
    """单步大运"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    index: int = Field(ge=1)
    start_year: int
    start_age: int = Field(ge=1)
    gan_zhi: GanZhi


class DaYunResult(BaseModel):
    """大运计算结果集合"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    direction: LuckDirection
    start_offset: LuckStartOffset
    start_at: datetime
    periods: list[DaYunPeriod] = Field(min_length=1)


class BaziChart(BaseModel):
    """八字排盘顶层聚合根"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    year_pillar: Pillar
    month_pillar: Pillar
    day_pillar: Pillar
    hour_pillar: Pillar
    da_yun: DaYunResult
    calculation_method: BaziCalculationMethod

    @computed_field
    @property
    def day_master(self) -> HeavenlyStem:
        """派生字段：日主，固定从日柱天干读取，防止数据冗余和不一致"""
        return self.day_pillar.gan_zhi.stem

FILE: src\guanchen_v2\models\liuyao.py

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import IntEnum, StrEnum
from typing import Annotated, Literal
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, StringConstraints, model_validator

NonBlankText = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(strip_whitespace=True, min_length=1),
]


class DecisionQuestionStatus(StrEnum):
    DRAFT = "draft"
    NEEDS_CLARIFICATION = "needs_clarification"
    PENDING_CONFIRMATION = "pending_confirmation"
    CONFIRMED = "confirmed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"


class CastingMethod(StrEnum):
    SYSTEM_THREE_COIN = "system_three_coin"
    MANUAL_SIX_LINES = "manual_six_lines"


class LineValue(IntEnum):
    OLD_YIN = 6
    YOUNG_YANG = 7
    YOUNG_YIN = 8
    OLD_YANG = 9


class YinYang(StrEnum):
    YIN = "yin"
    YANG = "yang"


class CoinFace(StrEnum):
    HEAD = "head"
    TAIL = "tail"


class CoinToss(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    position: int
    coins: tuple[CoinFace, CoinFace, CoinFace]
    line_value: LineValue


class HexagramLine(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    position: int = Field(ge=1, le=6)
    value: LineValue

    polarity: YinYang
    moving: bool

    heavenly_stem: NonBlankText | None = None
    earthly_branch: NonBlankText | None = None
    element: NonBlankText | None = None
    six_relative: NonBlankText | None = None
    six_spirit: NonBlankText | None = None
    is_shi: bool = False
    is_ying: bool = False


class HexagramIdentity(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    name: NonBlankText | None = None
    upper_trigram: NonBlankText
    lower_trigram: NonBlankText


class HexagramResult(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    casting_method: CastingMethod
    line_order: Literal["bottom_to_top"] = "bottom_to_top"

    lines: tuple[HexagramLine, ...]

    primary_hexagram: HexagramIdentity
    changed_hexagram: HexagramIdentity | None = None
    moving_line_positions: list[int] = Field(default_factory=list)
    coin_tosses: list[CoinToss] = Field(default_factory=list)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_invariants(self) -> "HexagramResult":
        if len(self.lines) != 6:
            raise ValueError("HexagramResult 必须正好包含 6 爻")

        for i, line in enumerate(self.lines, start=1):
            if line.position != i:
                raise ValueError(f"第 {i} 爻的 position 必须为 {i}")

            is_yin = line.polarity == YinYang.YIN
            is_moving = line.moving

            if line.value == LineValue.OLD_YIN and (not is_yin or not is_moving):
                raise ValueError("老阴(6) 必须是阴且动")
            elif line.value == LineValue.YOUNG_YANG and (is_yin or is_moving):
                raise ValueError("少阳(7) 必须是阳且静")
            elif line.value == LineValue.YOUNG_YIN and (not is_yin or is_moving):
                raise ValueError("少阴(8) 必须是阴且静")
            elif line.value == LineValue.OLD_YANG and (is_yin or not is_moving):
                raise ValueError("老阳(9) 必须是阳且动")

        actual_moving = [line.position for line in self.lines if line.moving]
        if self.moving_line_positions != actual_moving:
            raise ValueError("moving_line_positions 必须与各爻的 moving 状态一致")

        if actual_moving and self.changed_hexagram is None:
            raise ValueError("存在动爻时，必须包含 changed_hexagram")
        if not actual_moving and self.changed_hexagram is not None:
            raise ValueError("无动爻时，不能包含 changed_hexagram")

        if self.casting_method == CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN:
            if len(self.coin_tosses) != 6:
                raise ValueError("使用系统三枚硬币法时，必须包含正好 6 次 coin_tosses 记录")
            for i, toss in enumerate(self.coin_tosses):
                if toss.position != i + 1:
                    raise ValueError(f"coin_tosses[{i}] 的 position 必须是 {i + 1}")
                if toss.line_value != self.lines[i].value:
                    raise ValueError(f"coin_tosses[{i}] 的 line_value 必须与 lines[{i}] 一致")

                coin_sum = sum(3 if c == CoinFace.HEAD else 2 for c in toss.coins)
                if coin_sum != toss.line_value.value:
                    raise ValueError(f"coin_tosses[{i}] 的硬币组合之和不等于 {toss.line_value.value}")
        elif self.casting_method == CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES:
            if len(self.coin_tosses) != 0:
                raise ValueError("手工起卦不能包含 coin_tosses 记录")

        return self


class DecisionQuestion(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", validate_assignment=True)

    original_question: NonBlankText = Field(description="用户最初提出的问题原文")
    clarified_question: NonBlankText | None = Field(default=None)
    reality_constraints: list[NonBlankText] = Field(default_factory=list)
    status: DecisionQuestionStatus = DecisionQuestionStatus.DRAFT

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_question_state(self) -> "DecisionQuestion":
        if (
            self.status
            in {
                DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
                DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
            }
            and self.clarified_question is None
        ):
            raise ValueError("待确认或已确认的问题必须存在 clarified_question")
        return self


class DecisionReflection(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    situation_summary: NonBlankText = Field(description="对用户当前面临决策情境和现实约束的客观总结")
    traditional_reflection: NonBlankText = Field(description="严格基于 Retrieval Evidence 与 Hexagram Facts 得出的传统文化视角反思")
    reality_check: NonBlankText = Field(
        description="将传统文化视角与用户明确提出的 reality_constraints 相结合的现实校验。当传统结论与现实冲突时，提示现实约束优先"
    )
    decision_options: list[NonBlankText] = Field(
        description="给用户提供的可进一步评估或行动的方向参考，而非必须执行的命令",
        min_length=1,
    )
    uncertainty_note: NonBlankText = Field(description="免责与边界声明，明确表示该推演仅作文化参考，不能用于确定性预测")


class DecisionRecord(BaseModel):
    """
    六爻决策域的持久化领域记录（Stage 8）。

    只保存用户明确提供 / 确认的问题与确定性 / 结构化产物，
    不保存任何自动抽取的隐式画像。
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    record_id: UUID = Field(default_factory=uuid4)

    question: DecisionQuestion = Field(description="澄清 / 确认后的决策问题")

    thread_id: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="关联的 LangGraph 持久线程 ID，用于跨进程恢复 HITL 流程",
    )

    status: DecisionQuestionStatus = DecisionQuestionStatus.DRAFT

    hexagram: HexagramResult | None = None

    reflection: DecisionReflection | None = None

    final_response: str | None = None

    created_at: datetime = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )

    updated_at: datetime = Field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc),
    )


================================================================================
FILE: src\guanchen_v2\prompts\bazi_agent.py
