# =================================================================================
# Mocks & Fixtures
# =================================================================================


@pytest.fixture
def mock_deps():
    """提供纯 Fake 的外部依赖，确保测试 100% 聚焦在流程与契约"""
    caster = MagicMock(spec=HexagramCastingService)

    # 构造合法的静卦，防止格式化器抛错
    dummy_hex = HexagramResult(
        # 【修复处】：改为 MANUAL_SIX_LINES 即可合法地跳过 coin_tosses 校验
        casting_method=CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES,
        lines=tuple(HexagramLine(position=i, value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG, polarity=YinYang.YANG, moving=False) for i in range(1, 7)),
        primary_hexagram=HexagramIdentity(name="否", upper_trigram="乾", lower_trigram="坤"),
    )
    caster.cast_random.return_value = dummy_hex

    llm = AsyncMock()
    # 第一次提问澄清，第二次形成解释草稿
    llm.generate_structured.side_effect = [
        ClarificationAssessment(needs_clarification=False, clarified_question="未来一周可否跳槽？", reality_constraints=["房贷压力"]),
        DecisionReflection(
            situation_summary="面临跳槽抉择",
            traditional_reflection="卦象提示[Evidence 1]要谨慎。",
            reality_check="由于房贷压力，不可裸辞。",
            decision_options=["先拿到书面 offer 再说"],
            uncertainty_note="玄学仅供参考",
        ),
    ]

    retrieval = AsyncMock()
    retrieval.retrieve.return_value = [
        RetrievalResult(
            rank=1,
            chunk_id="c1",
            document_id="d1",
            domain=KnowledgeDomain.LIUYAO,
            source_title="《增删卜易》",
            source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
            section="断跳槽",
            text="应爻生世，大吉。",
            rrf_score=0.9,
            rrf_rank=1,
            reranker_score=None,
            dense_rank=1,
            sparse_rank=1,
        )
    ]

    return caster, llm, retrieval


class DummyCoinSource:
    """仅用于填充真实 HexagramCastingService 的入参契约"""

    def toss(self) -> CoinFace:
        return CoinFace.HEAD


# =================================================================================
# Stage 6 Acceptance Tests
# =================================================================================


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A01_pause_before_confirm(mock_deps):
    """1. pause before confirm：第一次 run 停在 confirmation，未确认时 cast=0"""
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a01_pause"}}

    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("我想跳槽")
    await graph.ainvoke(state, config)

    paused_state = graph.get_state(config)
    assert paused_state.next == ("confirmation",)
    assert paused_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A02_confirm_resume(mock_deps):
    """2. confirm/resume：同 thread resume 后 cast=1，并完成 final response"""
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a02_confirm"}}

    # 1. 停在确认点
    await graph.ainvoke(create_initial_liuyao_state("我想跳槽"), config)

    # 2. 发起确认
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "confirm"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    final_state = graph.get_state(config)

    # 图走到了尽头
    assert len(final_state.next) == 0
    # 状态是最终态
    assert final_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    # 工具被调用刚好 1 次
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 1
    # 输出了最终组合的文本
    final_text = final_state.values["final_response"]
    assert "由于房贷压力，不可裸辞" in final_text
    assert "[Evidence 1] 《增删卜易》 - 断跳槽" in final_text


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A03_modify_invalidates_downstream_and_re_interrupts(mock_deps):
    """
    3. modify/resume 特别关注场景：
       修改内容不丢，旧 hexagram/citations/reflection/final_response 全清；
       形成新 candidate 后仍然 cast=0，直到用户再次 CONFIRM。
    """
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps

    # 准备一个“被历史调用污染过”的满载状态
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("原问题")
    state["question"] = rebuild_question(state["question"], clarified_question="被历史污染的已确认问题", status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED)
    state["confirmed"] = True
    state["hexagram"] = "old_hexagram"
    state["retrieved_context"] = ["old_context"]
    state["citations"] = ["old_citation"]
    state["reflection"] = "old_reflection"
    state["final_response"] = "old_response"

    # [核心断言 1]：Policy 层面上，MODIFY 必须瞬间清空所有下游资产
    response = ConfirmationResponse(action=ConfirmationAction.MODIFY, modification="不测事业了，测感情")
    new_state = apply_confirmation_response(state, response)

    assert new_state["confirmed"] is False
    assert new_state["hexagram"] is None
    assert new_state["retrieved_context"] == []
    assert new_state["citations"] == []
    assert new_state["reflection"] is None
    assert new_state["final_response"] is None
    assert new_state["pending_user_input"] == "不测事业了，测感情"

    # [核心断言 2]：Graph 运行时再次推入新一轮
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a03_modify"}}

    # 把清理完的状态放入 Graph 开始新一轮流转 (会触发 clarify_node)
    await graph.ainvoke(new_state, config)
    current_state = graph.get_state(config)

    # 再次挂起在 confirmation
    assert current_state.next == ("confirmation",)

    # 确认 modification 已经被 clarify 消费清空
    assert current_state.values["pending_user_input"] is None

    # 最重要：由于没走过 CONFIRM 节点，Caster 坚决不许碰
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A04_cancel_routes_to_end(mock_deps):
    """4. cancel/resume：END，cast=0，无 downstream"""
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a04_cancel"}}

    await graph.ainvoke(create_initial_liuyao_state("测运势"), config)

    # 取消动作
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "cancel"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    final_state = graph.get_state(config)
    assert len(final_state.next) == 0
    assert final_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED

    # 彻底断开，没有起卦，没有引文
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 0
    assert final_state.values["hexagram"] is None
    assert final_state.values["final_response"] is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A05_manual_fixed_fixture(mock_deps):
    """5. manual fixed fixture：固定六爻输入得到稳定本卦/变卦/装卦结构。注入真实的起卦引擎测试全连通。"""
    _, llm, retrieval = mock_deps

    # 使用真实的起卦器！
    adapter = IChingPyAdapter()
    real_caster = HexagramCastingService(adapter, DummyCoinSource())

    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(real_caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a05_manual_fixed"}}

    # 给初始状态强制插入手工指定模式 (7,7,7,7,7,7 为纯乾卦静卦)
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试手工模式流转")
    state["casting_method"] = CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES
    state["manual_lines"] = (
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
    )

    await graph.ainvoke(state, config)

    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "confirm"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    final_state = graph.get_state(config)
    hexagram = final_state.values["hexagram"]

    # 验证真实的起卦算法被触发，装卦信息正确留存
    assert hexagram.primary_hexagram.upper_trigram == "乾"
    assert hexagram.moving_line_positions == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_A06_projection_and_security_gates(mock_deps):
    """6. projection/security gate：非 confirmed、投影不一致、非法 resume 都不能穿透到 cast"""
    caster, llm, retrieval = mock_deps
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(caster, llm, retrieval)
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "a06_security"}}

    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试越权")
    await graph.ainvoke(state, config)

    # 1. 发送非法的 resume payload -> Confirmation Response Schema 会阻拦并抛出 PolicyError
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "illegal_action"})
    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="无效的六爻确认响应"):
        await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    # 2. 直接构造破损的状态投喂给 cast_node
    broken_state = create_initial_liuyao_state("恶意穿透")
    broken_state["question"] = rebuild_question(
        broken_state["question"], clarified_question="恶意穿透的假问题", status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    )
    broken_state["confirmed"] = False  # 投影不一致！

    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="确认状态不一致"):
        runtime.cast_node(broken_state)

    broken_state["question"] = rebuild_question(
        broken_state["question"], clarified_question="恶意穿透的假问题", status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION
    )
    broken_state["confirmed"] = False

    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="未就绪或未通过用户确认"):
        runtime.cast_node(broken_state)

    # 从头到尾，假工具从未被真正调用过
    assert caster.cast_random.call_count == 0
    assert caster.cast_manual.call_count == 0

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from langgraph.types import Command

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.exceptions import LiuyaoPolicyError
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import rebuild_question
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import create_initial_liuyao_state
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.workflow import LiuyaoAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    CastingMethod,
    CoinToss,
    DecisionQuestionStatus,
    DecisionReflection,
    HexagramIdentity,
    HexagramLine,
    HexagramResult,
    LineValue,
    YinYang,
)


def _fake_hexagram() -> HexagramResult:
    """构造与 line_value 严格一致的合法 HexagramResult。"""
    lines = tuple(
        HexagramLine(
            position=i,
            value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
            polarity=YinYang.YANG,
            moving=False,
            heavenly_stem="甲",
            earthly_branch="子",
            element="水",
            six_relative="父母",
        )
        for i in range(1, 7)
    )
    return HexagramResult(
        casting_method=CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN,
        lines=lines,
        primary_hexagram=HexagramIdentity(name="乾", upper_trigram="乾", lower_trigram="乾"),
        changed_hexagram=None,
        moving_line_positions=[],
        coin_tosses=[
            CoinToss(
                position=i,
                coins=("head", "tail", "tail"),
                line_value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
            )
            for i in range(1, 7)
        ],
    )


@pytest.fixture
def mock_caster():
    caster = MagicMock()
    caster.cast_random.return_value = _fake_hexagram()
    caster.cast_manual.return_value = _fake_hexagram()
    return caster


@pytest.fixture
def mock_llm():
    llm = MagicMock()
    llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock(
        return_value=DecisionReflection(
            situation_summary="现状总结",
            traditional_reflection="传统推演",
            reality_check="现实校验",
            decision_options=["参考选项"],
            uncertainty_note="仅供参考",
        )
    )
    return llm


@pytest.fixture
def mock_retrieval():
    retrieval = MagicMock()
    retrieval.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])
    return retrieval


@pytest.fixture
def runtime(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """共享的 LiuyaoAgentRuntime，携带完整依赖（llm / retrieval 在纯 cast 测试中不触发）"""
    return LiuyaoAgentRuntime(
        casting_service=mock_caster,
        llm_client=mock_llm,
        retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval,
    )


def test_cast_node_guard_blocks_unconfirmed(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """1. 验证 cast_node() 独立防御：即使绕过路由，也会因 can_cast 失败死死防住。"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试直接进入 cast")

    with pytest.raises(LiuyaoPolicyError, match="未就绪或未通过用户确认"):
        runtime.cast_node(state)

    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 0


def test_cast_node_allows_confirmed(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """2. 验证 cast_node() 对完全合法的已确认状态放行，并直接调用 Tool。"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("合法进入")

    q = rebuild_question(
        state["question"],
        clarified_question="被用户彻底确认的问题",
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
    )
    state["question"] = q
    state["confirmed"] = True

    result = runtime.cast_node(state)
    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 1
    assert result["hexagram"].casting_method == CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN
    assert result["hexagram"].primary_hexagram.name == "乾"


async def test_workflow_confirmation_interrupt_and_resume_confirm(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """3. 验证 Graph 中断契约及确认后调用起卦服务。"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)

    def mock_clarify(state):
        q = rebuild_question(
            state["question"],
            clarified_question="未来一周是否该跳槽？",
            reality_constraints=["薪资要求"],
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
        )
        return {"question": q, "pending_user_input": None}

    runtime.clarify_node = mock_clarify
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "test_confirm"}}
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("跳槽？")

    # [首次执行] 触发流程，将被 interrupt() 挂起
    await graph.ainvoke(state, config)
    current_state = await graph.aget_state(config)

    assert current_state.next == ("confirmation",)

    interrupt_payload = current_state.tasks[0].interrupts[0].value
    assert interrupt_payload["type"] == "liuyao_confirmation"
    assert interrupt_payload["question"] == "未来一周是否该跳槽？"
    assert interrupt_payload["reality_constraints"] == ["薪资要求"]
    assert "confirm" in interrupt_payload["allowed_actions"]

    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 0

    # [外部恢复]
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "confirm"})
    final_state = await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 1
    assert final_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    assert final_state["hexagram"].primary_hexagram.name == "乾"


async def test_workflow_resume_modify_preserves_user_input_and_routes(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """4. 验证 MODIFY 保留用户修改内容，并在 Clarify 被消费后产生新候选问题重新挂起等待确认。"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)

    def mock_clarify(state):
        if state.get("pending_user_input"):
            runtime.test_captured_input = state["pending_user_input"]
            q = rebuild_question(
                state["question"],
                clarified_question="测事业？",
                status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
            )
            # LLM 消费完毕，清空
            return {"question": q, "pending_user_input": None}

        q = rebuild_question(
            state["question"],
            clarified_question="初始候选问题",
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
        )
        return {"question": q}

    runtime.clarify_node = mock_clarify
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "test_modify"}}

    await graph.ainvoke(create_initial_liuyao_state("测感情"), config)

    # [外部恢复] MODIFY 并带修改意见
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "modify", "modification": "别测感情了，改测事业吧。"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    current_state = await graph.aget_state(config)

    # 验证新问题必须重新挂起确认（不可直接起卦）
    assert current_state.next == ("confirmation",)
    assert current_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION
    assert current_state.values["question"].clarified_question == "测事业？"

    # 验证 pending_user_input 在 clarify 节点确实被消费/清空了
    assert current_state.values["pending_user_input"] is None

    # 验证修改后工具仍未调用
    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 0
    assert getattr(runtime, "test_captured_input", None) == "别测感情了，改测事业吧。"


async def test_workflow_resume_cancel_routes_to_end(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """5. 验证 CANCEL 切断流向 END。"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)

    def mock_clarify(state):
        q = rebuild_question(
            state["question"],
            clarified_question="初始候选问题",
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
        )
        return {"question": q}

    runtime.clarify_node = mock_clarify
    graph = runtime.build_graph()
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": "test_cancel"}}

    await graph.ainvoke(create_initial_liuyao_state("算卦"), config)

    # [外部恢复]
    resume_cmd = Command(resume={"action": "cancel"})
    await graph.ainvoke(resume_cmd, config)

    current_state = await graph.aget_state(config)
    assert len(current_state.next) == 0
    assert current_state.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED
    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 0


def test_cast_node_dispatches_manual_cast(mock_caster, mock_llm, mock_retrieval):
    """6. 验证 Task 2 的 Manual Six Lines 正确分发"""
    runtime = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting_service=mock_caster, llm_client=mock_llm, retrieval_builder=mock_retrieval)
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("测试手工起卦")

    state["question"] = rebuild_question(
        state["question"],
        clarified_question="已确认的问题",
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
    )
    state["confirmed"] = True
    state["casting_method"] = CastingMethod.MANUAL_SIX_LINES
    state["manual_lines"] = (
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
        LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
    )

    runtime.cast_node(state)

    assert mock_caster.cast_manual.call_count == 1
    assert mock_caster.cast_random.call_count == 0

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import httpx
import pytest
from langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel
from langchain_core.messages import AIMessage, HumanMessage, SystemMessage
from openai import APIStatusError
from pydantic import BaseModel

import guanchen_v2.llm.client
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.llm.exceptions import (
    LLMEmptyResponseError,
    LLMProviderError,
    LLMRetryExhaustedError,
    LLMStructuredOutputError,
)


class DummySchema(BaseModel):
    field: str


@pytest.fixture
def fake_model():
    return Mock(spec=BaseChatModel)


@pytest.fixture
def llm_client(fake_model):
    return LLMClient(
        model=fake_model,
        max_retries=2,
        retry_wait_exponential_jitter=False,
    )


def test_text_happy_path(llm_client, fake_model):
    fake_model.invoke.return_value = AIMessage(content="ok")

    result = llm_client.text("hello")

    assert result == "ok"
    assert fake_model.invoke.call_count == 1


def test_non_retryable_provider_failure(llm_client, fake_model):
    mock_request = httpx.Request(
        "POST",
        "https://api.deepseek.com/chat",
    )

    fake_model.invoke.side_effect = APIStatusError(
        message="Bad Request",
        response=httpx.Response(
            400,
            request=mock_request,
        ),
        body=None,
    )

    with pytest.raises(LLMProviderError):
        llm_client.text("hello")

    assert fake_model.invoke.call_count == 1


def test_empty_structured_output_exhausts_retries(
    llm_client,
    fake_model,
):
    structured_mock = Mock()
    structured_mock.invoke.return_value = None

    fake_model.with_structured_output.return_value = structured_mock

    with pytest.raises(LLMEmptyResponseError):
        llm_client.structured(
            "hello",
            DummySchema,
        )

    assert structured_mock.invoke.call_count == 3


def test_structured_parse_failure_exhausts_retries(
    llm_client,
    fake_model,
):
    structured_mock = Mock()
    structured_mock.invoke.return_value = {
        "field": "wrong type",
    }

    fake_model.with_structured_output.return_value = structured_mock

    with pytest.raises(LLMStructuredOutputError):
        llm_client.structured(
            "hello",
            DummySchema,
        )

    assert structured_mock.invoke.call_count == 3


def test_transient_provider_failure_exhausts_retries(
    llm_client,
    fake_model,
):
    mock_request = httpx.Request(
        "POST",
        "https://api.deepseek.com/chat",
    )

    fake_model.invoke.side_effect = APIStatusError(
        message="Bad Gateway",
        response=httpx.Response(
            502,
            request=mock_request,
        ),
        body=None,
    )

    with pytest.raises(LLMRetryExhaustedError):
        llm_client.text("hello")

    assert fake_model.invoke.call_count == 3

FILE: tests\llm\test_provider.py

from unittest.mock import Mock

import guanchen_v2.llm.provider
from guanchen_v2.llm.provider import build_chat_model


def test_build_chat_model_contract(monkeypatch):
    """
    验证基础模型工厂是否严格遵循 Stage 1 的构造契约。
    重点防御底层重试泄露 (max_retries=0) 和 thinking mode 禁用。
    """
    mock_chat_deepseek = Mock()
    monkeypatch.setattr(guanchen_v2.llm.provider, "ChatDeepSeek", mock_chat_deepseek)

    # 使用 Mock 对象彻底隔离 Pydantic Settings 对 .env 文件的磁盘读取
    mock_settings = Mock()
    mock_settings.deepseek_model = "deepseek-test"
    # llm_api_key 是 SecretStr，因此我们要 mock 它的 get_secret_value 方法
    mock_settings.deepseek_api_key.get_secret_value.return_value = "sk-fake-key"
    mock_settings.llm_temperature = 0.8
    mock_settings.llm_timeout_seconds = 45.0

    # 调用工厂
    build_chat_model(mock_settings)

    mock_chat_deepseek.assert_called_once()
    call_kwargs = mock_chat_deepseek.call_args.kwargs

    # 验证常规透传
    assert call_kwargs["model"] == "deepseek-test"
    assert call_kwargs["api_key"] == "sk-fake-key"
    assert call_kwargs["temperature"] == 0.8
    assert call_kwargs["timeout"] == 45.0

    # 验证底线契约
    assert call_kwargs["max_retries"] == 0
    assert call_kwargs["extra_body"].get("thinking", {}).get("type") == "disabled"


================================================================================
FILE: tests\memory\test_birth_profile_repository.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 8 必测 1：BirthProfile 持久化集成测试（写入 / 读取 / 更正）。

验收点：
- 应用重启后 BirthProfile 仍可恢复（repository 写入 -> 全新 repository 实例读取）；
- 后写的明确更正覆盖当前有效事实，并保留 updated_at（用户更正语义）。
"""
import pytest

from guanchen_v2.memory.repositories import BirthProfileRepository
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_birth_profile_write_read_correct(postgres_session_factory):
    """写入 -> 读取（模拟重启）-> 更正 -> 再次读取，且 updated_at 随更正推进。"""
    repo = BirthProfileRepository(postgres_session_factory)

    user_id = "user-birth-1"

    # 1. 用户明确提供出生资料
    original = BirthProfile(
        year=1990,
        month=5,
        day=20,
        hour=14,
        minute=30,
        gender=Gender.MALE,
        birth_place_text="北京",
    )
    await repo.upsert(user_id, original)

    # 2. “应用重启”：完全新的 repository 实例读取（同一数据库）
    repo_after_restart = BirthProfileRepository(postgres_session_factory)
    loaded = await repo_after_restart.get(user_id)

    assert loaded is not None
    assert loaded.year == 1990
    assert loaded.month == 5
    assert loaded.day == 20
    assert loaded.hour == 14
    assert loaded.minute == 30
    assert loaded.gender == Gender.MALE
    assert loaded.birth_place_text == "北京"

    # 3. 用户更正出生时间（后写覆盖）
    corrected = BirthProfile(
        year=1990,
        month=5,
        day=20,
        hour=16,
        minute=0,
        gender=Gender.MALE,
        birth_place_text="北京",
    )
    await repo.upsert(user_id, corrected)

    loaded_corrected = await repo.get(user_id)
    assert loaded_corrected is not None
    assert loaded_corrected.hour == 16
    assert loaded_corrected.minute == 0

    # 4. 更正后 updated_at 必须推进（保留 updated_at 语义，行级维护）
    from sqlalchemy import select

    from guanchen_v2.memory.models import BirthProfileRow

    async def fetch_row():
        async with postgres_session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(BirthProfileRow).where(BirthProfileRow.user_id == user_id)
            )
            return result.scalar_one()

    row_after_first = await fetch_row()
    await repo.upsert(user_id, corrected)
    row_after_correction = await fetch_row()

    assert row_after_first.updated_at is not None
    assert row_after_correction.updated_at is not None
    assert row_after_correction.updated_at >= row_after_first.updated_at


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_birth_profile_single_current_fact(postgres_session_factory):
    """单用户只保留一条当前有效事实（唯一约束 + upsert 覆盖）。"""
    repo = BirthProfileRepository(postgres_session_factory)
    user_id = "user-birth-2"

    first = BirthProfile(
        year=1988, month=1, day=1, hour=8, minute=0, gender=Gender.FEMALE
    )
    second = BirthProfile(
        year=1988, month=1, day=1, hour=10, minute=15, gender=Gender.FEMALE
    )

    await repo.upsert(user_id, first)
    await repo.upsert(user_id, second)

    loaded = await repo.get(user_id)
    assert loaded is not None
    assert (loaded.hour, loaded.minute) == (10, 15)

    from sqlalchemy import func, select

    from guanchen_v2.memory.models import BirthProfileRow

    async with postgres_session_factory() as session:
        count = (await session.execute(select(func.count()).select_from(BirthProfileRow).where(BirthProfileRow.user_id == user_id))).scalar_one()
    assert count == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_birth_profile_missing_returns_none(postgres_session_factory):
    """从未提供过出生资料的用户返回 None，而不是异常。"""
    repo = BirthProfileRepository(postgres_session_factory)
    assert await repo.get("user-never-existed") is None


================================================================================
FILE: tests\memory\test_checkpoint_recovery.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 8 必测 2：LangGraph HITL 跨进程恢复（PostgreSQL checkpointer）。

验收点：
- 六爻流程 interrupt 后模拟进程重建，再用同 thread_id resume；
- LangGraph interrupted workflow 可跨进程 resume（官方 AsyncPostgresSaver）。
"""
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from langgraph.checkpoint.postgres.aio import AsyncPostgresSaver
from langgraph.types import Command
from psycopg_pool import AsyncConnectionPool

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.casting import HexagramCastingService
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.policy import ClarificationAssessment
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.retrieval import LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import create_initial_liuyao_state
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.workflow import LiuyaoAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.memory.checkpointer import to_psycopg_conninfo
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    CastingMethod,
    CoinToss,
    DecisionQuestionStatus,
    DecisionReflection,
    HexagramIdentity,
    HexagramLine,
    HexagramResult,
    LineValue,
    YinYang,
)
from tests.conftest import TEST_DATABASE_URL

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def build_mock_services():
    casting = MagicMock(spec=HexagramCastingService)

    lines = tuple(
        HexagramLine(
            position=i,
            value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
            polarity=YinYang.YANG,
            moving=False,
            heavenly_stem="甲",
            earthly_branch="子",
            element="水",
            six_relative="父母",
        )
        for i in range(1, 7)
    )
    mock_hex = HexagramResult(
        casting_method=CastingMethod.SYSTEM_THREE_COIN,
        lines=lines,
        primary_hexagram=HexagramIdentity(name="乾", upper_trigram="乾", lower_trigram="乾"),
        changed_hexagram=None,
        moving_line_positions=[],
        coin_tosses=[
            CoinToss(
                position=i,
                # 3+2+2=7 => 少阳（Young Yang），与 line_value 严格一致
                coins=("head", "tail", "tail"),
                line_value=LineValue.YOUNG_YANG,
            )
            for i in range(1, 7)
        ],
    )
    casting.cast_random.return_value = mock_hex
    casting.cast_manual.return_value = mock_hex

    llm = MagicMock(spec=LLMClient)

    async def mock_generate_structured(*args, **kwargs):
        schema = kwargs.get("schema")
        if schema == ClarificationAssessment:
            return ClarificationAssessment(
                needs_clarification=False,
                clarified_question="确认要离职吗？",
                reality_constraints=[],
            )
        if schema == DecisionReflection:
            return DecisionReflection(
                situation_summary="情况",
                traditional_reflection="推演",
                reality_check="现实",
                decision_options=["选项"],
                uncertainty_note="免责",
            )
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown schema: {schema}")

    llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock(side_effect=mock_generate_structured)

    retrieval = MagicMock(spec=LiuyaoRetrievalBuilder)
    retrieval.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])

    return casting, llm, retrieval


async def open_saver() -> tuple[AsyncPostgresSaver, AsyncConnectionPool]:
    pool = AsyncConnectionPool(
        conninfo=to_psycopg_conninfo(TEST_DATABASE_URL),
        kwargs={"autocommit": True},
        open=False,
    )
    await pool.open()
    saver = AsyncPostgresSaver(pool)
    await saver.setup()
    return saver, pool


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_liuyao_hitl_cross_process_resume(db_env):
    """进程 A 挂起 -> 进程彻底重建 -> 进程 B 用同 thread_id resume 完成全流程。"""
    casting, llm, retrieval = build_mock_services()
    thread_id = "liuyao_cross_process_resume_1"
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}

    # ====== 进程 A：首次执行，停在 confirmation interrupt ======
    saver_a, pool_a = await open_saver()
    runtime_a = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(
        casting_service=casting,
        llm_client=llm,
        retrieval_builder=retrieval,
        checkpointer=saver_a,
    )
    graph_a = runtime_a.build_graph()

    initial = create_initial_liuyao_state("我该不该辞职")
    await graph_a.ainvoke(initial, config)

    state_a = await graph_a.aget_state(config)
    assert state_a.next == ("confirmation",)
    assert state_a.values["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 0  # 确认前禁止起卦

    # 进程 A 关闭（模拟服务彻底重启）
    await pool_a.close()

    # ====== 进程 B：全新 runtime + 全新 saver，同一数据库 / 同一 thread_id ======
    saver_b, pool_b = await open_saver()
    runtime_b = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(
        casting_service=casting,
        llm_client=llm,
        retrieval_builder=retrieval,
        checkpointer=saver_b,
    )
    graph_b = runtime_b.build_graph()

    final_state = await graph_b.ainvoke(
        Command(resume={"action": "confirm"}),
        config,
    )

    # 验证：基于同一个 thread 恢复了图状态并跑完全部节点
    assert final_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    assert final_state["hexagram"] is not None
    assert final_state["final_response"] is not None
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 1

    # 进程 B 的 saver 能看到同一个 thread（未新建线程）
    threads = [c async for c in saver_b.alist({"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}})]
    assert any(t.config["configurable"]["thread_id"] == thread_id for t in threads)

    await pool_b.close()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_liuyao_cancel_after_restart(db_env):
    """进程重建后拒绝确认（cancel）也能正确走到 END。"""
    casting, llm, retrieval = build_mock_services()
    thread_id = "liuyao_cross_process_cancel_1"
    config = {"configurable": {"thread_id": thread_id}}

    saver_a, pool_a = await open_saver()
    runtime_a = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval, checkpointer=saver_a)
    graph_a = runtime_a.build_graph()
    await graph_a.ainvoke(create_initial_liuyao_state("要不要投资"), config)
    await pool_a.close()

    saver_b, pool_b = await open_saver()
    runtime_b = LiuyaoAgentRuntime(casting, llm, retrieval, checkpointer=saver_b)
    graph_b = runtime_b.build_graph()

    final_state = await graph_b.ainvoke(Command(resume={"action": "cancel"}), config)

    assert final_state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CANCELLED
    assert casting.cast_random.call_count == 0  # 取消后绝不触发起卦
    await pool_b.close()

FILE: tests\memory\test_domain_record_stores.py

"""Stage 8 域记录持久化：DreamRecord / DecisionRecord 跨进程可恢复。"""
from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.dream.retrieval import DreamRetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.dream.runtime import DreamAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient
from guanchen_v2.memory.stores import (
    PostgresDecisionRecordStore,
    PostgresDreamRecordStore,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import DreamContextStatus, DreamRecord
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import (
    DecisionQuestion,
    DecisionQuestionStatus,
    DecisionRecord,
)

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def make_decision_record(thread_id: str = "liuyao_thread_x") -> DecisionRecord:
    return DecisionRecord(
        question=DecisionQuestion(
            original_question="要不要换工作",
            clarified_question="今年要不要换工作",
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
        ),
        thread_id=thread_id,
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
        final_response="参考推演",
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dream_record_round_trip_across_restart(postgres_session_factory):
    """DreamRecord 写入 -> 新 store 实例（模拟重启）读取，内容完整。"""
    user_id = "user-dream-1"
    store_a = PostgresDreamRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory)

    record = DreamRecord()
    record.raw_descriptions.append("梦见在旧教学楼里迷路")
    record.elements.objects.append("旧教学楼")
    record.elements.emotions.append("焦虑")

    await store_a.save(record)

    # 模拟应用重启：全新 store 实例 + 全新 repository
    store_b = PostgresDreamRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory)
    recovered = await store_b.load_latest()

    assert recovered is not None
    assert recovered.dream_id == record.dream_id
    assert recovered.raw_descriptions == record.raw_descriptions
    assert recovered.elements.objects == ["旧教学楼"]
    assert recovered.elements.emotions == ["焦虑"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_decision_record_round_trip_across_restart(postgres_session_factory):
    """DecisionRecord 写入 -> 新 store 实例读取（含 thread_id 引用）。"""
    user_id = "user-decision-1"
    store_a = PostgresDecisionRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory)

    record = make_decision_record(thread_id="liuyao_thread_recovery")
    await store_a.save(record)

    store_b = PostgresDecisionRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory)
    recovered = await store_b.load_latest()

    assert recovered is not None
    assert recovered.record_id == record.record_id
    assert recovered.thread_id == "liuyao_thread_recovery"
    assert recovered.question.clarified_question == "今年要不要换工作"
    assert recovered.status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dream_runtime_restores_record_after_restart(postgres_session_factory):
    """解梦运行时重启后自动恢复当前 DreamRecord 并继续多轮会话（硬门禁）。"""

    class FakeContextService:
        """每轮仅追加描述并进入澄清态（避免触发解释链路，专注验证记忆恢复）。"""

        async def apply_turn(self, user_text, current_record=None):
            record = current_record if current_record is not None else DreamRecord()
            record.raw_descriptions.append(user_text)
            return MagicMock(
                status=DreamContextStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION,
                record=record,
                patch=None,
                clarification_question="请补充更多梦境细节",
                started_new_dream=False,
            )

    context_service = FakeContextService()
    retrieval = MagicMock(spec=DreamRetrievalService)
    retrieval.retrieve.return_value = []
    llm = MagicMock(spec=LLMClient)

    user_id = "user-dream-runtime-1"
    store = PostgresDreamRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory)

    # 进程 A：第一轮
    runtime_a = DreamAgentRuntime(
        context_service=context_service,
        retrieval_service=retrieval,
        llm_client=llm,
        record_store=store,
    )
    result_a = await runtime_a.handle_turn("梦见一条大蛇")
    assert result_a.record.raw_descriptions == ["梦见一条大蛇"]

    # 进程 B：完全新的 runtime（同一 store 绑定同一 user）
    runtime_b = DreamAgentRuntime(
        context_service=context_service,
        retrieval_service=retrieval,
        llm_client=llm,
        record_store=PostgresDreamRecordStore(user_id, postgres_session_factory),
    )
    result_b = await runtime_b.handle_turn("蛇变成了龙")

    # 恢复后继续：raw_descriptions 包含上一轮的梦境描述
    assert result_b.record.raw_descriptions == ["梦见一条大蛇", "蛇变成了龙"]


================================================================================
FILE: tests\memory\test_inmemory_store.py
================================================================================

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import ConversationState
from guanchen_v2.memory.exceptions import (
    MemoryAlreadyExistsError,
    MemoryNotFoundError,
)
from guanchen_v2.memory.implementations.inmemory import InMemoryMemoryStore


@pytest.fixture
def store() -> InMemoryMemoryStore:
    return InMemoryMemoryStore()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_1_save_and_load(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 1: 验证 Session 保存与加载，且必须切断 Object Identity"""
    original_state = ConversationState(session_id="session-1")
    await store.save(original_state)
    
    loaded_state = await store.load("session-1")
    
    assert loaded_state is not None
    assert loaded_state.session_id == "session-1"
    # 【核心验证】: 不能是同一个内存对象（模拟 DB 序列化边界）
    assert loaded_state is not original_state 
    # 数据等价
    assert loaded_state.model_dump() == original_state.model_dump()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_2_duplicate_save(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 2: 验证重复 Save 必须失败，保护现有数据"""
    state = ConversationState(session_id="session-2")
    await store.save(state)
    
    with pytest.raises(MemoryAlreadyExistsError):
        await store.save(state)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_3_update_existing(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 3: 验证存在的 Session 可以被正常 Update"""
    state = ConversationState(session_id="session-3", current_query="旧问题")
    await store.save(state)
    
    state.current_query = "新问题"
    await store.update(state)
    
    loaded_state = await store.load("session-3")
    assert loaded_state is not None
    assert loaded_state.current_query == "新问题"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_4_update_missing(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 4: 验证 Update 不存在的 Session 必须抛错"""
    non_exist_state = ConversationState(session_id="missing-4")
    
    with pytest.raises(MemoryNotFoundError):
        await store.update(non_exist_state)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_5_delete_idempotent(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 5: 验证删除功能，并确保其具备幂等性 (Idempotent)"""
    state = ConversationState(session_id="session-5")
    await store.save(state)
    
    # 第一次删除
    await store.delete("session-5")
    assert await store.load("session-5") is None
    
    # 第二次删除不存在的 ID，必须静默成功，不报错
    await store.delete("session-5") 
    await store.delete("never-existed")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_6_session_isolation(store: InMemoryMemoryStore):
    """Test 6: 验证不同的 Session ID 之间绝对隔离"""
    state_a = ConversationState(session_id="A", current_query="Query A")
    state_b = ConversationState(session_id="B", current_query="Query B")
    
    await store.save(state_a)
    await store.save(state_b)
    
    loaded_a = await store.load("A")
    loaded_b = await store.load("B")
    
    assert loaded_a is not None and loaded_b is not None
    assert loaded_a.current_query == "Query A"
    assert loaded_b.current_query == "Query B"



================================================================================
FILE: tests\memory\test_memory_selector.py
================================================================================

"""Stage 8 必测 3：Memory Selector 领域隔离测试。

验收点：
- DreamRecord 不出现在 Bazi Agent 的 memory context；
- Memory Selector 不把全历史无脑塞给模型；
- 每个领域只能看到自己命名空间的数据。
"""
import pytest

from guanchen_v2.memory.selector import MemorySelector
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender
from guanchen_v2.models.dream import DreamRecord
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def make_dream_record() -> DreamRecord:
    record = DreamRecord()
    record.raw_descriptions.append("梦见一条大蛇在追我")
    record.elements.people.append("陌生人")
    record.elements.objects.append("大蛇")
    return record


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dream_record_never_leaks_into_bazi_context(postgres_session_factory):
    """核心隔离断言：梦境数据绝不进入八字 memory context。"""
    selector = MemorySelector(postgres_session_factory)
    user_id = "user-isolation-1"

    # 1. 同一个用户同时拥有梦境记录与出生资料
    dream = make_dream_record()
    from guanchen_v2.memory.repositories import DreamRecordRepository

    await DreamRecordRepository(postgres_session_factory).save(user_id, dream)

    profile = BirthProfile(
        year=1995, month=3, day=10, hour=9, minute=0, gender=Gender.FEMALE
    )
    await selector.persist_birth_profile(user_id, profile)

    # 2. 梦境记忆确实存在（持久层可恢复）
    loaded_dream = await selector.load_dream_memory(user_id)
    assert loaded_dream is not None
    assert any("蛇" in obj for obj in loaded_dream.elements.objects)

    # 3. Bazi 的 memory context 只包含出生资料，绝无梦境内容
    bazi_context = await selector.select_for_intent(IntentType.BAZI, user_id)
    assert RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE in bazi_context.available_data
    assert len(bazi_context.available_data) == 1
    assert RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT not in bazi_context.available_data
    assert "dream" not in bazi_context.available_data


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_selector_does_not_inject_history_for_other_intents(postgres_session_factory):
    """Knowledge / 普通请求不注入任何用户历史记忆。"""
    selector = MemorySelector(postgres_session_factory)
    user_id = "user-isolation-2"

    profile = BirthProfile(
        year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE
    )
    await selector.persist_birth_profile(user_id, profile)

    knowledge_ctx = await selector.select_for_intent(IntentType.KNOWLEDGE, user_id)
    assert knowledge_ctx.available_data == {}

    dream_ctx = await selector.select_for_intent(IntentType.DREAM, user_id)
    # 当前轮梦境内容来自用户本轮输入；历史 DreamRecord 由运行时 record_store 维护
    assert dream_ctx.available_data == {}

    liuyao_ctx = await selector.select_for_intent(IntentType.LIUYAO, user_id)
    assert liuyao_ctx.available_data == {}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_bazi_memory_prefill_enables_ready_without_reask(postgres_session_factory):
    """八字长期记忆预填：已有出生资料时，Requirement Check 直接 READY（重启后无需再问）。"""
    from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import RoutingResult
    from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementStatus, check_requirements

    selector = MemorySelector(postgres_session_factory)
    user_id = "user-isolation-3"

    profile = BirthProfile(
        year=1990, month=6, day=15, hour=20, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE
    )
    await selector.persist_birth_profile(user_id, profile)

    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
        reason="八字测试",
    )

    context = await selector.select_for_intent(IntentType.BAZI, user_id)
    result = check_requirements(routing, context)

    assert result.status == RequirementStatus.READY
    assert result.missing_data == ()

FILE: tests\memory\test_postgres_memory_store.py

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.memory.implementations.postgres import (
    PostgresMemoryStore,
)

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_postgres_memory_save_load(
    postgres_session_factory,
    sample_state,
):

    store = PostgresMemoryStore(
        postgres_session_factory
    )

    await store.save(sample_state)

    loaded = await store.load(
        sample_state.session_id
    )

    assert loaded is not None

    assert (
        loaded.session_id
        ==
        sample_state.session_id
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_postgres_memory_save_existing_raises(
    postgres_session_factory,
    sample_state,
):
    """save 重复 session 必须拒绝覆盖。"""
    from guanchen_v2.memory.exceptions import MemoryAlreadyExistsError

    store = PostgresMemoryStore(postgres_session_factory)
    await store.save(sample_state)

    with pytest.raises(MemoryAlreadyExistsError):
        await store.save(sample_state)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_postgres_memory_update_and_delete(
    postgres_session_factory,
    sample_state,
):
    """update / delete 生命周期：不存在时 update 必须报错，delete 幂等。"""
    from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import MessageState
    from guanchen_v2.memory.exceptions import MemoryNotFoundError

    store = PostgresMemoryStore(postgres_session_factory)

    with pytest.raises(MemoryNotFoundError):
        await store.update(sample_state)

    await store.save(sample_state)

    sample_state.messages.append(MessageState(role="user", content="你好"))
    await store.update(sample_state)

    loaded = await store.load(sample_state.session_id)
    assert loaded is not None
    assert len(loaded.messages) == 1

    await store.delete(sample_state.session_id)
    assert await store.load(sample_state.session_id) is None

    # delete 幂等
    await store.delete(sample_state.session_id)

from datetime import datetime

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import (
    BaziCalculationMethod,
    BaziChart,
    BirthProfile,
    DaYunPeriod,
    DaYunResult,
    EarthlyBranch,
    FiveElement,
    GanZhi,
    Gender,
    HeavenlyStem,
    HiddenStem,
    LuckDirection,
    LuckStartOffset,
    Pillar,
    TenGod,
)


def test_valid_birth_profile():
    profile = BirthProfile(
        year=2024,
        month=2,
        day=29,
        hour=14,
        minute=30,
        gender=Gender.MALE,
        birth_place_text="  成都  ",
    )
    assert profile.year == 2024
    assert profile.gender == Gender.MALE
    assert profile.birth_place_text == "成都"  # 验证清洗


def test_birth_profile_empty_place():
    profile = BirthProfile(year=2024, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE, birth_place_text="   ")
    assert profile.birth_place_text is None  # 空白字符串转为 None


def test_impossible_calendar_date():
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="非法的日期.*组合"):
        BirthProfile(year=2023, month=2, day=29, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.FEMALE)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="非法的日期.*组合"):
        BirthProfile(year=2025, month=2, day=30, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)


def test_illegal_calculation_method():
    # 验证严格禁用的排盘规则会被拒绝
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        BaziCalculationMethod(
            library_version="1.0",
            late_zi_sect=1,
            yun_sect=1,
            true_solar_time=True,  # Stage 2 锁定禁止
        )


def test_empty_lists_rejected():
    gz = GanZhi(stem=HeavenlyStem.JIA, branch=EarthlyBranch.ZI)

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Pillar(gan_zhi=gz, stem_ten_god=None, hidden_stems=[])  # 必须包含藏干

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        DaYunResult(
            direction=LuckDirection.FORWARD,
            start_offset=LuckStartOffset(years=0, months=0, days=0, hours=0),
            start_at=datetime(2030, 1, 1, 0, 0),
            periods=[],  # 必须包含大运步
        )


def test_derived_fields_and_invariants():
    # 验证甲子干支正确派生出五行为：水、木
    gz = GanZhi(stem=HeavenlyStem.JIA, branch=EarthlyBranch.ZI)
    assert gz.gan_zhi == "甲子"

    hidden = HiddenStem(stem=HeavenlyStem.GUI, ten_god=TenGod.ZHENG_YIN)
    assert hidden.element == FiveElement.WATER

    pillar = Pillar(gan_zhi=gz, stem_ten_god=None, hidden_stems=[hidden])
    assert pillar.stem_element == FiveElement.WOOD
    assert pillar.branch_element == FiveElement.WATER

    dummy_method = BaziCalculationMethod(library_version="1.0", late_zi_sect=2, yun_sect=1)
    dummy_dayun = DaYunResult(
        direction=LuckDirection.FORWARD,
        start_offset=LuckStartOffset(years=0, months=0, days=0, hours=0),
        start_at=datetime(2030, 1, 1, 0, 0),
        periods=[DaYunPeriod(index=1, start_year=2025, start_age=1, gan_zhi=gz)],
    )

    chart = BaziChart(
        year_pillar=pillar,
        month_pillar=pillar,
        day_pillar=pillar,
        hour_pillar=pillar,
        da_yun=dummy_dayun,
        calculation_method=dummy_method,
    )
    # 日主自动从日柱天干甲读取
    assert chart.day_master == HeavenlyStem.JIA

FILE: tests\models\test_liuyao_models.py

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.liuyao.state import create_initial_liuyao_state
from guanchen_v2.models.liuyao import DecisionQuestion, DecisionQuestionStatus


def test_decision_question_draft_allows_empty_clarified():
    """DRAFT 和 NEEDS_CLARIFICATION 状态下允许 clarified_question 为空"""
    dq = DecisionQuestion(original_question="我要不要辞职？")
    assert dq.status == DecisionQuestionStatus.DRAFT
    assert dq.clarified_question is None

    dq.status = DecisionQuestionStatus.NEEDS_CLARIFICATION
    assert dq.clarified_question is None


def test_decision_question_pending_confirmation_requires_clarified():
    """防线测试：如果标记为待确认，决不能拿着一个空问题去问用户"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="必须存在 clarified_question"):
        DecisionQuestion(
            original_question="我要不要辞职？",
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.PENDING_CONFIRMATION,
        )


def test_decision_question_confirmed_requires_clarified():
    """防线测试：如果标记为已确认，问题实体必须实质存在"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="必须存在 clarified_question"):
        DecisionQuestion(
            original_question="我要不要辞职？",
            status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
        )


def test_decision_question_valid_confirmation():
    """合法的完整确认状态应被 Pydantic 顺利放行"""
    dq = DecisionQuestion(
        original_question="我要辞职吗？近期房贷压力大。",
        clarified_question="是否应该在未来三个月内离职？",
        reality_constraints=["近期有房贷压力，不能断供"],
        status=DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED,
    )
    assert dq.status == DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED
    assert len(dq.reality_constraints) == 1


def test_create_initial_liuyao_state():
    """验证工作流入口必须产出纯净的、未被污染的初始基线状态"""
    state = create_initial_liuyao_state("工作太累了，算一卦")

    assert state["question"].original_question == "工作太累了，算一卦"
    assert state["question"].status == DecisionQuestionStatus.DRAFT
    assert state["question"].clarified_question is None

    assert state["clarification"] is None
    assert state["confirmed"] is False
    assert state["hexagram"] is None
    assert state["citations"] == []
    assert state["final_response"] is None


def test_cannot_assign_confirmed_without_clarified():
    """防线测试：严格拦截在实例化之后，被违规赋值为 CONFIRMED 状态"""
    dq = DecisionQuestion(original_question="我要不要辞职？")

    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="必须存在 clarified_question",
    ):
        # 如果 validate_assignment=False，这行代码将无视验证规则强制修改成功
        dq.status = DecisionQuestionStatus.CONFIRMED

FILE: tests\router\test_adapters.py

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from langgraph.types import Command

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime import BaziAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.agents.knowledge.agent import KnowledgeAgent
from guanchen_v2.dream.runtime import DreamAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.liuyao.workflow import LiuyaoAgentRuntime
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import BirthProfile, Gender
from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.bazi import BaziAgentAdapter
from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.dream import DreamAgentAdapter
from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.knowledge import KnowledgeAgentAdapter
from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.liuyao import LiuyaoAgentAdapter
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_bazi_adapter_happy_path_and_invariants():
    """验证 Bazi Adapter 准确提取参数并转换为 ReadyForBazi；缺失时 fail-fast"""
    mock_runtime = AsyncMock(spec=BaziAgentRuntime)
    adapter = BaziAgentAdapter(mock_runtime)

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="缺少已声明可用"):
        await adapter.invoke("查事业", RequirementContext())

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="数据类型不合法"):
        context_wrong_type = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "Not A Profile Object"})
        await adapter.invoke("查事业", context_wrong_type)

    profile = BirthProfile(year=2000, month=1, day=1, hour=12, minute=0, gender=Gender.MALE)
    context_valid = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: profile})

    await adapter.invoke("查事业", context_valid)

    mock_runtime.run.assert_awaited_once()
    ready_input = mock_runtime.run.await_args.args[0]
    assert ready_input.question == "查事业"
    assert ready_input.profile == profile


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dream_adapter_happy_path_and_invariants():
    """验证 Dream Adapter 正确校验和传递纯文本内容；缺失或类型错误时 fail-fast"""
    mock_runtime = AsyncMock(spec=DreamAgentRuntime)
    adapter = DreamAgentAdapter(mock_runtime)

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="缺少已声明可用"):
        await adapter.invoke("解梦", RequirementContext())

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="必须为非空字符串"):
        context_wrong = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: {"not": "a string"}})
        await adapter.invoke("解梦", context_wrong)

    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "梦见一条大蛇"})
    await adapter.invoke("我要解梦", context)

    mock_runtime.handle_turn.assert_awaited_once_with("梦见一条大蛇")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_knowledge_adapter_bypasses_context_entirely():
    """验证 Knowledge Adapter 完全忽略 Context 数据"""
    mock_agent = AsyncMock(spec=KnowledgeAgent)
    adapter = KnowledgeAgentAdapter(mock_agent)

    dirty_context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "八字机密数据"})

    await adapter.invoke("什么是正印", dirty_context)
    mock_agent.answer.assert_awaited_once_with("什么是正印")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_liuyao_adapter_preserves_resume_handle():
    """验证 Liuyao Adapter 初次唤醒能透出含 thread_id 的句柄，且外部可通过同句柄接力 resume"""
    mock_runtime = MagicMock(spec=LiuyaoAgentRuntime)
    mock_graph = AsyncMock()

    # 定义两次 graph ainvoke 的返回：第一次遇到 interrupt，第二次恢复执行完返回 done
    mock_graph.ainvoke.side_effect = [
        {"__interrupt__": ["pending"]},
        {"final_response": "done"},
    ]
    mock_runtime.build_graph.return_value = mock_graph

    adapter = LiuyaoAgentAdapter(mock_runtime)

    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION: "我该辞职吗"})

    # 第一次 Invoke 停在打断点，暴露出句柄
    first_result = await adapter.invoke("帮我起一卦", context)

    assert mock_graph.ainvoke.call_count == 1
    assert first_result.thread_id.startswith("liuyao_thread_")

    # 第二次直接基于刚才截获的 thread_id 和外部构造的 payload 进行 resume 接力
    resumed_result = await adapter.resume(first_result.thread_id, {"action": "confirm"})

    assert mock_graph.ainvoke.call_count == 2

    # 精确拦截并断言：第二次 ainvoke 时传入了准确无误的 Command 和同个 Thread ID Config
    resume_command = mock_graph.ainvoke.await_args_list[1].args[0]
    resume_config = mock_graph.ainvoke.await_args_list[1].args[1]

    assert isinstance(resume_command, Command)
    assert resume_command.resume == {"action": "confirm"}
    assert resume_config["configurable"]["thread_id"] == first_result.thread_id

    # 断言确实拿到了完成后的最终状态
    assert resumed_result == {"final_response": "done"}
