FILE: tests\router\test_contracts.py
================================================================================

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import (
    IntentType,
    RequiredDataType,
    RoutingResult,
)


def test_valid_single_domain_bazi():
    """1. 单领域 BAZI -> BIRTH_PROFILE 合法"""
    result = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        confidence=0.9,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
        reason="用户明确要求排八字",
    )
    assert result.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert result.secondary_intents == ()


def test_invalid_requirement_for_knowledge():
    """2. KNOWLEDGE + BIRTH_PROFILE 被拒绝。大模型不能凭空给 Knowledge 塞入出生要求"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="必须严格匹配意图所要求的数据集合"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.KNOWLEDGE,
            confidence=0.8,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),  # 非法多余
            reason="用户问什么是十神",
        )


def test_valid_dual_domain():
    """3. 显式 contract BAZI + DREAM -> BirthProfile + DreamContent 合法"""
    result = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
        confidence=0.85,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT),
        reason="用户希望结合八字来解梦",
    )
    assert result.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert result.secondary_intents == (IntentType.DREAM,)
    assert set(result.required_data) == {RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT}


def test_invalid_secondary_counts_and_overlap():
    """4. secondary 超过 1 个或与 primary 重复被拒绝"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Tuple should have at most 1 item"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM, IntentType.LIUYAO),
            confidence=0.8,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT, RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION),
            reason="太多领域了",
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="primary_intent 不能出现在 secondary_intents 中"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            secondary_intents=(IntentType.BAZI,),
            confidence=0.9,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
            reason="重复领域",
        )


def test_invalid_isolation_mixing():
    """5. CHITCHAT / OUT_OF_SCOPE 与专业 Intent 混搭被拒绝"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="secondary_intents 不能包含 chitchat"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            secondary_intents=(IntentType.CHITCHAT,),
            confidence=0.9,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
            reason="不能在八字时闲聊",
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="当 primary_intent 为 out_of_scope 时，secondary_intents 必须为空"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.OUT_OF_SCOPE,
            secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
            confidence=0.9,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,),
            reason="出界了不能再挂个解梦",
        )


def test_confidence_out_of_bounds():
    """6. confidence 越界被拒绝"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Input should be less than or equal to 1"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.KNOWLEDGE,
            confidence=1.1,
            reason="过于自信",
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Input should be greater than or equal to 0"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.CHITCHAT,
            confidence=-0.5,
            reason="不够自信",
        )


def test_duplicate_required_data_rejected():
    """7. required_data 存在重复项被拒绝"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="不允许包含重复项"):
        RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            confidence=0.8,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE),
            reason="重复提供出生要求",
        )


================================================================================
FILE: tests\router\test_core.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.core import (
    HighPrecisionRuleRouter,
    RouterDecision,
    TwoLayerIntentRouter,
)


@pytest.fixture
def mock_llm():
    llm = AsyncMock()
    # 默认兜底 mock，确保走 LLM 时的基础返回类型
    llm.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(primary_intent=IntentType.OUT_OF_SCOPE, confidence=0.8, reason="Mock fallback")
    return llm


@pytest.fixture
def router(mock_llm):
    return TwoLayerIntentRouter(
        rule_router=HighPrecisionRuleRouter(),
        llm_client=mock_llm,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_bazi_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 帮我看看我的八字事业 -> BAZI, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("帮我看看我的八字事业")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE in res.required_data
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_dream_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 昨晚梦到一条蛇，帮我解释一下 -> DREAM, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("昨晚梦到一条蛇，帮我解释一下")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.DREAM
    assert RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT in res.required_data
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_liuyao_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 帮我起一卦看看要不要离职 -> LIUYAO, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("帮我起一卦看看要不要离职")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.LIUYAO
    assert RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION in res.required_data
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_knowledge_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 正官是什么意思？ -> KNOWLEDGE, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("正官是什么意思？")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.KNOWLEDGE
    assert res.required_data == ()  # Knowledge 绝不能请求其他资料
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_chitchat_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 你好 -> CHITCHAT, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("你好")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.CHITCHAT
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_out_of_scope_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 帮我写一段Python代码 -> OOS, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("帮我写一段Python代码")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.OUT_OF_SCOPE
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_llm_fallback_enrichment(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 规则未命中，进入 LLM Fallback 消歧 -> LLM call=1"""
    # 模拟大模型判定为 LIUYAO
    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(
        primary_intent=IntentType.LIUYAO, confidence=0.85, reason="用户围绕具体工作选择请求传统占卜式决策参考"
    )

    # 采用真正需要 LLM 语义消歧，且业务意图高度贴合六爻的表达
    res = await router.route("最近有两个工作机会拿不定主意，想用传统占卜方式参考一下")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.LIUYAO
    assert RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION in res.required_data
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_dual_domain_rule_hit(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 显式结合规则 -> Dual Domain, LLM call=0"""
    res = await router.route("结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起看看")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert IntentType.DREAM in res.secondary_intents
    # 双重 requirement 自动化拼装
    assert set(res.required_data) == {RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT}
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_mention_does_not_trigger_dual(router, mock_llm):
    """Case: 我梦见有人给我算八字 -> LLM Fallback (DREAM), LLM call=1"""
    # 规则无法命中，交给大模型
    # 模拟大模型遵从 Prompt 契约，仅判定为 DREAM，而没有附加 BAZI
    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(primary_intent=IntentType.DREAM, confidence=0.88, reason="提及算命，但本身是一个梦境")

    res = await router.route("昨天睡觉的时候，梦里有个道士帮我排八字，这是啥意思？")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.DREAM
    assert res.secondary_intents == ()  # 大模型只输出了 DREAM

    # Python 代码自动基于 Intent 计算 Requirement，安全可信，DREAM 绝对不含 BirthProfile
    assert res.required_data == (RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,)
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_domain_comparison_is_knowledge(router, mock_llm):
    """验证包含领域词的概念对比请求会被优先分发给 Knowledge"""
    res = await router.route("看看八字和六爻有什么区别？")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.KNOWLEDGE
    assert res.secondary_intents == ()
    assert res.required_data == ()
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_router_personal_dream_meaning_not_knowledge(router, mock_llm):
    """验证带有明确个人暗示特征的解梦不会被 Knowledge 抢走，并能命中增强后的 Dream 规则"""
    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(
        primary_intent=IntentType.DREAM,
        confidence=0.9,
        reason="用户请求解释自己的具体梦境",
    )

    res = await router.route("我昨晚梦到蛇是什么意思？")

    assert res.primary_intent == IntentType.DREAM
    assert res.secondary_intents == ()
    # 命中扩充后的 DREAM 规则，不需要耗费一次 LLM 调用
    assert mock_llm.generate_structured.call_count == 0


def test_router_decision_rejects_non_combinable_secondary():
    """验证 RouterDecision schema 自身能够死守非法组合"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="secondary_intents 只能包含专业领域"):
        RouterDecision(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            secondary_intents=(IntentType.CHITCHAT,),
            confidence=0.9,
            reason="非法组合",
        )


================================================================================
FILE: tests\router\test_dispatch.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType, RoutingResult
from guanchen_v2.router.dispatch import SingleAgentDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementCheckResult, RequirementContext, RequirementStatus


@pytest.fixture
def mock_adapters():
    return {
        IntentType.BAZI: AsyncMock(),
        IntentType.DREAM: AsyncMock(),
        IntentType.KNOWLEDGE: AsyncMock(),
        IntentType.LIUYAO: AsyncMock(),
    }


@pytest.fixture
def dispatcher(mock_adapters):
    return SingleAgentDispatcher(mock_adapters)


def test_dispatcher_rejects_non_professional_registry():
    """Case 1: 注册表禁止注册闲聊与 OOS"""
    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="不能包含非专业 Agent"):
        SingleAgentDispatcher({IntentType.CHITCHAT: AsyncMock()})


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_ready_calls_target(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """Case 2: 准备妥当 -> 精准调用 1 次目标，其他不唤起"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="测试")
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(intent=IntentType.BAZI, status=RequirementStatus.READY, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,))
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "Dummy"})

    await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "测八字", context)

    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.call_count == 0
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.KNOWLEDGE].invoke.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_needs_input_bypasses_agents(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """Case 3: 缺少资料 (NEEDS_INPUT) -> 所有 Agent 调次数均为 0"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.DREAM, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,), reason="测试")
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(
        intent=IntentType.DREAM,
        status=RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,),
        missing_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,),
    )

    res = await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "解梦", RequirementContext())

    assert res is req_result  # 返回 orchestration 信息以供前端处理追问
    for adapter in mock_adapters.values():
        assert adapter.invoke.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_chitchat_bypass(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """Case 4: BYPASS (如寒暄) -> 所有 Agent 调用次数均为 0"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.CHITCHAT, confidence=1.0, reason="hi")
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(intent=IntentType.CHITCHAT, status=RequirementStatus.BYPASS)

    res = await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "你好", RequirementContext())

    assert res is req_result
    for adapter in mock_adapters.values():
        assert adapter.invoke.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_mismatch_intent_fails(dispatcher):
    """Case 5: 防御脏数据：如果路由结果与核对结果 Intent 发生漂移，立即阻断"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="测试")
    # 模拟外部意外传入了不匹配的 DREAM 的 CheckResult
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(intent=IntentType.DREAM, status=RequirementStatus.READY, required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,))

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="与 RequirementCheckResult intent 不一致"):
        await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "问题", RequirementContext())


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_dual_domain_fails(dispatcher):
    """Case 6: 双领域试图挤名单领域 Dispatcher -> 立刻拒绝"""
    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT),
        reason="测试",
    )
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(intent=IntentType.BAZI, status=RequirementStatus.READY, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,))

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="不接受双领域"):
        await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "问题", RequirementContext())


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_mismatch_context_fails(dispatcher):
    """验证防伪防线：当 RequirementCheckResult 合法，但它与传来的 Context 的重新计算不符合时报错"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="测试")
    # 这个 Requirement 是合法的，表明准备就绪
    req_result = RequirementCheckResult(intent=IntentType.BAZI, status=RequirementStatus.READY, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,))
    # 但实际投递的上下文是空的！
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={})

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="RequirementCheckResult 与当前 RoutingResult / Context 不一致"):
        await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req_result, "八字", context)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_knowledge_dispatch_never_requires_bazi(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """端到端拦截测试 1：知识查询就算上下文里什么都没有，也只会触发 Knowledge Adapter 而不会牵连八字检查"""
    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.KNOWLEDGE,
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(),
        reason="知识问答",
    )
    # 这里也可以故意在 Context 里丢一个假出生数据看看会不会出错，但根据契约，不要求就不会检查
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "干扰数据"})

    # 手动算一遍期待的 Requirement
    from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import check_requirements

    req = check_requirements(routing, context)
    assert req.status == RequirementStatus.READY

    await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req, "什么是正官", context)

    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.KNOWLEDGE].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dream_dispatch_never_triggers_others(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """端到端拦截测试 2：解梦请求哪怕满足条件了，也绝不能连累别的 Agent"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.DREAM, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,), reason="测试梦")
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "昨晚梦见蛇"})

    from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import check_requirements

    req = check_requirements(routing, context)
    assert req.status == RequirementStatus.READY

    await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, req, "解梦蛇", context)

    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 0
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.LIUYAO].invoke.call_count == 0
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.KNOWLEDGE].invoke.call_count == 0


================================================================================
FILE: tests\router\test_dual_dispatch.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType, RoutingResult
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_dispatch import AgentExecutionResult, DualDomainDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext


@pytest.fixture
def mock_adapters():
    def create_adapter(return_val: str):
        adapter = AsyncMock()
        adapter.invoke.return_value = return_val
        return adapter

    return {
        IntentType.BAZI: create_adapter("bazi_result"),
        IntentType.DREAM: create_adapter("dream_result"),
        IntentType.LIUYAO: create_adapter("liuyao_result"),
    }


@pytest.fixture
def dispatcher(mock_adapters):
    return DualDomainDispatcher(mock_adapters)


def test_dual_dispatcher_rejects_non_professional_registry():
    """验证注册表合法性：禁止闲聊进入双领域配置"""
    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="不能包含非专业 Agent"):
        DualDomainDispatcher({IntentType.CHITCHAT: AsyncMock()})


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dual_dispatcher_rejects_single_domain(dispatcher):
    """验证路由契约：坚决拒绝单领域请求挤进双领域 Dispatcher"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="单领域")

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="仅接受双领域请求"):
        await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, "测试", RequirementContext())


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dual_dispatcher_rejects_missing_adapters(mock_adapters):
    """验证防御机制：如果要求的 Agent 没注册，必须 Fail-fast"""
    # 故意剔除 DREAM adapter
    partial_registry = {IntentType.BAZI: mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI]}
    bad_dispatcher = DualDomainDispatcher(partial_registry)

    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT),
        reason="双领域",
    )

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="缺少处理 dream 的 AgentAdapter"):
        await bad_dispatcher.dispatch(routing, "测试", RequirementContext())


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dual_dispatcher_executes_sequentially(dispatcher, mock_adapters):
    """验证 Happy Path：串行调用，结果被正确包装进 AgentExecutionResult"""
    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT),
        reason="结合八字和梦境",
    )
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "profile", RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "content"})

    results = await dispatcher.dispatch(routing, "联合分析", context)

    # 验证底层被精确调用
    mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.assert_awaited_once_with("联合分析", context)
    mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.assert_awaited_once_with("联合分析", context)
    # 不相干的 Agent 绝对不会被唤起
    mock_adapters[IntentType.LIUYAO].invoke.assert_not_called()

    # 验证返回契约
    assert len(results) == 2

    assert isinstance(results[0], AgentExecutionResult)
    assert results[0].intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert results[0].output == "bazi_result"

    assert isinstance(results[1], AgentExecutionResult)
    assert results[1].intent == IntentType.DREAM
    assert results[1].output == "dream_result"


================================================================================
FILE: tests\router\test_requirements.py
================================================================================

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType, RoutingResult
from guanchen_v2.router.errors import RouterDispatchError
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import (
    RequirementCheckResult,
    RequirementContext,
    RequirementStatus,
    check_requirements,
)


def test_check_requirements_bazi_ready():
    """Case 1: BAZI + BirthProfile available -> READY"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="测试")
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "DummyProfile"})

    result = check_requirements(routing, context)

    assert result.status == RequirementStatus.READY
    assert result.missing_data == ()


def test_check_requirements_bazi_needs_input():
    """Case 2: BAZI missing BirthProfile -> NEEDS_INPUT"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,), reason="测试")
    context = RequirementContext()  # 空上下文

    result = check_requirements(routing, context)

    assert result.status == RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT
    assert result.missing_data == (RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,)


def test_check_requirements_knowledge_ready():
    """Case 3: KNOWLEDGE + empty context -> READY (因为知识域不要上下文)"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.KNOWLEDGE, confidence=1.0, required_data=(), reason="测试")
    context = RequirementContext()

    result = check_requirements(routing, context)

    assert result.status == RequirementStatus.READY
    assert result.missing_data == ()


def test_check_requirements_bypass():
    """Case 4: CHITCHAT / OOS -> BYPASS"""
    routing1 = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.CHITCHAT, confidence=1.0, reason="hi")
    result1 = check_requirements(routing1, RequirementContext())
    assert result1.status == RequirementStatus.BYPASS

    routing2 = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.OUT_OF_SCOPE, confidence=1.0, reason="code")
    result2 = check_requirements(routing2, RequirementContext())
    assert result2.status == RequirementStatus.BYPASS


def test_check_requirements_rejects_dual_domain():
    """Case 5: 双领域直接 Fail-fast 拒绝进入单领域 Checker"""
    routing = RoutingResult(
        primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
        secondary_intents=(IntentType.DREAM,),
        confidence=1.0,
        required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE, RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT),
        reason="测试",
    )

    with pytest.raises(RouterDispatchError, match="不接受双领域"):
        check_requirements(routing, RequirementContext())


def test_requirement_result_rejects_missing_data_on_ready():
    """验证非法状态构造：READY 不能带有 missing_data"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="READY 状态不能包含 missing_data"):
        RequirementCheckResult(
            intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            status=RequirementStatus.READY,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
            missing_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),  # 非法！
        )


def test_requirement_result_rejects_empty_missing_on_needs_input():
    """验证非法状态构造：NEEDS_INPUT 不能没有 missing_data"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="NEEDS_INPUT 必须包含 missing_data"):
        RequirementCheckResult(
            intent=IntentType.DREAM,
            status=RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,),
            missing_data=(),  # 非法！
        )


def test_requirement_result_rejects_bypass_for_professional_intent():
    """验证非法状态构造：专业能力不能乱挂 BYPASS"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="专业 Intent 不能使用 BYPASS 状态"):
        RequirementCheckResult(
            intent=IntentType.LIUYAO, status=RequirementStatus.BYPASS, required_data=(RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION,), missing_data=()
        )


def test_liuyao_question_seed_is_enough_to_start():
    """验证：六爻意图只要有最基本的用户问题输入 (Seed) 就可以过 READY 进入它的 workflow，不需要提前确认好"""
    routing = RoutingResult(primary_intent=IntentType.LIUYAO, confidence=1.0, required_data=(RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION,), reason="测试")
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DECISION_QUESTION: "我今年能脱单吗"})

    result = check_requirements(routing, context)
    assert result.status == RequirementStatus.READY
    assert result.missing_data == ()


def test_requirement_context_rejects_none_value():
    """验证非法状态构造：available_data 中声称拥有的数据不能是虚假的 None"""
    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="不能为 None",
    ):
        RequirementContext(
            available_data={
                RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: None,
            }
        )


================================================================================
FILE: tests\router\test_stage7_acceptance.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.liuyao import LiuyaoInvocationResult
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.core import HighPrecisionRuleRouter, RouterDecision, TwoLayerIntentRouter
from guanchen_v2.router.dispatch import SingleAgentDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_dispatch import DualDomainDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_requirements import check_dual_requirements
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext, RequirementStatus
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesis import SynthesisInput
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesizer import AgentSynthesizer


@pytest.fixture
def mock_adapters():
    def create_adapter(return_val):
        adapter = AsyncMock()
        adapter.invoke.return_value = return_val
        return adapter

    return {
        IntentType.BAZI: create_adapter({"bazi": "done"}),
        IntentType.DREAM: create_adapter({"dream": "done"}),
        IntentType.LIUYAO: create_adapter(LiuyaoInvocationResult(thread_id="test_tid", output="paused")),
        IntentType.KNOWLEDGE: create_adapter("knowledge done"),
    }


@pytest.fixture
def mock_llm():
    return AsyncMock()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_stage7_case1_explicit_dual_domain_happy_path(mock_adapters, mock_llm):
    """Test 1: 真实双领域，成功流转直到合成器"""
    router = TwoLayerIntentRouter(HighPrecisionRuleRouter(), mock_llm)
    dual_dispatch = DualDomainDispatcher(mock_adapters)
    synthesizer = AgentSynthesizer(mock_llm)

    # 1. 路由
    query = "结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起看看"
    routing_result = await router.route(query)
    assert routing_result.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert routing_result.secondary_intents == (IntentType.DREAM,)

    # 2. 校验
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "profile", RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "content"})
    req_result = check_dual_requirements(routing_result, context)
    assert req_result.status == RequirementStatus.READY

    # 3. 分发
    exec_results = await dual_dispatch.dispatch(routing_result, query, context)
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.call_count == 1

    # 4. 汇总
    synthesis_input = SynthesisInput(query=query, results=exec_results)
    await synthesizer.synthesize(synthesis_input)

    # Synthesizer 发起了 LLM 调用合并结论
    mock_llm.generate_structured.assert_awaited_once()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_stage7_case2_missing_data_rejects_dispatch(mock_adapters, mock_llm):
    """Test 2: 资料缺失导致中断，绝不盲目派发 Agent"""
    router = TwoLayerIntentRouter(HighPrecisionRuleRouter(), mock_llm)

    routing_result = await router.route("结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起看看")

    # Context 中故意漏掉 DreamContent
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "profile"})
    req_result = check_dual_requirements(routing_result, context)

    # 精准阻断
    assert req_result.status == RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT
    assert req_result.missing_data == (RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT,)

    # 确保没有任何 Agent 被实际调用
    for adapter in mock_adapters.values():
        adapter.invoke.assert_not_called()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_stage7_case3_pseudo_dual_domain_isolated(mock_adapters, mock_llm):
    """Test 3: 防止伪双领域越权（提及算命，但属于单梦境诉求）"""
    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = RouterDecision(primary_intent=IntentType.DREAM, confidence=0.9, reason="仅仅提及八字")
    router = TwoLayerIntentRouter(HighPrecisionRuleRouter(), mock_llm)
    single_dispatch = SingleAgentDispatcher(mock_adapters)

    query = "昨天睡觉的时候，梦里有个道士帮我排八字，这是啥意思？"
    routing_result = await router.route(query)

    # 坚决不产生双领域
    assert routing_result.primary_intent == IntentType.DREAM
    assert routing_result.secondary_intents == ()

    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "梦见道士排八字"})

    # 因为是单领域，所以可以直接使用 Single Dispatch
    from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import check_requirements

    req = check_requirements(routing_result, context)
    await single_dispatch.dispatch(routing_result, req, query, context)

    # 确保没有发生蔓延，Bazi Adapter 完全不知情
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_stage7_case4_liuyao_synthesizer_preserves_thread_id(mock_adapters, mock_llm):
    """Test 4: Liuyao 返回结构必须能无损送达，且严格防止 thread_id 泄露给大模型"""
    synthesizer = AgentSynthesizer(mock_llm)

    exec_results = (
        mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke("...", None),
        mock_adapters[IntentType.LIUYAO].invoke("...", None),
    )
    resolved = await __import__("asyncio").gather(*exec_results)

    from guanchen_v2.router.dual_dispatch import AgentExecutionResult

    synthesis_input = SynthesisInput(
        query="结合八字看看要不要换工作",
        results=(
            AgentExecutionResult(intent=IntentType.BAZI, output=resolved[0]),
            AgentExecutionResult(intent=IntentType.LIUYAO, output=resolved[1]),
        ),
    )

    await synthesizer.synthesize(synthesis_input)

    synthesizer_call_args = mock_llm.generate_structured.await_args.kwargs
    prompt_sent_to_llm = synthesizer_call_args["user_prompt"]

    # 核心断言：大模型看到的必须是纯业务载荷，绝对不包含内部状态句柄 (test_tid)
    assert "[Liuyao Result]" in prompt_sent_to_llm
    assert "test_tid" not in prompt_sent_to_llm  # 防止内存越权泄漏
    assert "paused" in prompt_sent_to_llm


================================================================================
FILE: tests\router\test_stage7_router_acceptance.py

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.router.adapters.liuyao import LiuyaoInvocationResult
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType
from guanchen_v2.router.core import HighPrecisionRuleRouter, RouterDecision, TwoLayerIntentRouter
from guanchen_v2.router.dispatch import SingleAgentDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_dispatch import AgentExecutionResult, DualDomainDispatcher
from guanchen_v2.router.dual_requirements import check_dual_requirements
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementContext, RequirementStatus
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesis import SynthesisInput
from guanchen_v2.router.synthesizer import AgentSynthesizer


@pytest.fixture
def mock_adapters():
    def create_adapter(return_val):
        adapter = AsyncMock()
        adapter.invoke.return_value = return_val
        return adapter

    return {
        IntentType.BAZI: create_adapter({"bazi": "done"}),
        IntentType.DREAM: create_adapter({"dream": "done"}),
        IntentType.LIUYAO: create_adapter(LiuyaoInvocationResult(thread_id="test_tid", output="paused")),
        IntentType.KNOWLEDGE: create_adapter("knowledge done"),
    }


@pytest.fixture
def mock_llm():
    return AsyncMock()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_stage7_case1_explicit_dual_domain_happy_path(mock_adapters, mock_llm):
    """Test 1: 真实双领域，成功流转直到合成器"""
    router = TwoLayerIntentRouter(HighPrecisionRuleRouter(), mock_llm)
    dual_dispatch = DualDomainDispatcher(mock_adapters)
    synthesizer = AgentSynthesizer(mock_llm)

    # 1. 路由
    query = "结合我的八字和昨晚的梦一起看吧"
    routing_result = await router.route(query)
    assert routing_result.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert routing_result.secondary_intents == (IntentType.DREAM,)

    # 2. 校验
    context = RequirementContext(available_data={RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE: "profile", RequiredDataType.DREAM_CONTENT: "content"})

    # 3. 分发

    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.BAZI].invoke.call_count == 1
    assert mock_adapters[IntentType.DREAM].invoke.call_count == 1

    # 4. 汇总
    synthesis_input = SynthesisInput(query=query, results=exec_results)
    await synthesizer.synthesize(synthesis_input)

    # Synthesizer 发起的 LLM 调用合并结论
    mock_llm.generate_structured.assert_awaited_once()

================================================================================
FILE: tests\agents\bazi\test_requirements.py
================================================================================

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,
    InvalidBaziInput,
    MissingField,
    NeedUserInput,
    ReadyForBazi,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.requirements import check_bazi_requirements
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import Gender


def test_complete_input_ready_preserves_question():
    """Case 1: 资料齐全，不仅得到 BirthProfile，原问题也一字不落地保留并清洗。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="  测事业  ", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, ReadyForBazi)
    assert result.question == "测事业"
    assert result.profile.year == 1998


def test_missing_time_needs_input_preserves_question():
    """Case 2: 缺时辰时分，触发 NeedUserInput，同时记住用户想问什么。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="运势如何？", year=1998, month=7, day=21, gender=Gender.MALE)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.original_question == "运势如何？"
    assert result.missing_fields == [MissingField.HOUR, MissingField.MINUTE]


def test_missing_gender_needs_input():
    """Case 3: 缺少性别，触发 NeedUserInput。绝对不调用 LLM 去猜。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="测财运", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.missing_fields == [MissingField.GENDER]


def test_multiple_missing_fields_stable_order():
    """Case 4: 大面积缺失资料，确保系统返回的 missing_fields 顺序是稳定的。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="我适合做什么？", year=2000)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.missing_fields == [
        MissingField.MONTH,
        MissingField.DAY,
        MissingField.HOUR,
        MissingField.MINUTE,
        MissingField.GENDER,
    ]


def test_invalid_calendar_date_preserves_question():
    """Case 5: 非法的跨字段日期组合，拦截器立刻阻断并报 Invalid。已知错误优先于缺失信息。"""
    inp = BaziAgentInput(question="算一卦", year=2023, month=2, day=29)
    result = check_bazi_requirements(inp)

    assert isinstance(result, InvalidBaziInput)
    assert result.original_question == "算一卦"
    assert "非法的日期组合" in result.reason


def test_empty_question_rejected():
    """Case 6: 最基础的输入 Schema 校验，拦截空白问题。"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="question 不能为空或纯空格"):
        BaziAgentInput(question="   ", year=1998, month=1, day=1)


def test_known_single_field_invalid_rejected():
    """Case 7: 单字段自身的硬伤（如 year=0）直接在模型层被拒收，不会流入 NeedUserInput。"""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        BaziAgentInput(question="测事业", year=0, month=1, day=1)


================================================================================
FILE: tests\agents\bazi\test_runtime.py
================================================================================

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import pytest
from langchain_core.messages import ToolMessage

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.exceptions import (
    BaziAgentCitationError,
    BaziAgentInvariantError,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziInterpretation,
    ReadyForBazi,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime import (
    BaziAgentRuntime,
)
from guanchen_v2.bazi.adapter import (
    LunarPythonBaziAdapter,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalResult,
    SourceType,
)
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import (
    BirthProfile,
    Gender,
)
from guanchen_v2.tools.bazi_retrieval import (
    BaziKnowledgeArtifact,
)


@pytest.fixture
def ready_input():
    return ReadyForBazi(
        question="事业",
        profile=BirthProfile(
            year=1998,
            month=7,
            day=21,
            hour=14,
            minute=30,
            gender=Gender.MALE,
        ),
    )


def _patch_runtime_dependencies(
    monkeypatch,
    mock_agent,
) -> None:
    import guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime,
        "create_agent",
        lambda **kwargs: mock_agent,
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime,
        "build_chat_model",
        Mock(),
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime,
        "build_calculate_bazi_tool",
        Mock(),
    )

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.runtime,
        "build_retrieve_bazi_knowledge_tool",
        Mock(),
    )


def _create_chart(
    ready_input: ReadyForBazi,
):
    adapter = LunarPythonBaziAdapter()
    return adapter.calculate(ready_input.profile)


def _create_retrieval_result(
    chunk_id: str = "c1",
) -> RetrievalResult:
    return RetrievalResult(
        rank=1,
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id="d1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="T",
        source_type=SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE,
        section="S",
        text="T",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=1,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=1,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_success_with_calculation_and_retrieval(
    ready_input,
    monkeypatch,
):
    real_chart = _create_chart(ready_input)

    bazi_tool_message = ToolMessage(
        content="{}",
        name="calculate_bazi",
        tool_call_id="1",
        artifact=real_chart,
    )

    retrieval_result = _create_retrieval_result("c1")

    retrieval_tool_message = ToolMessage(
        content="txt",
        name="retrieve_bazi_knowledge",
        tool_call_id="2",
        artifact=BaziKnowledgeArtifact(results=[retrieval_result]),
    )

    mock_agent = Mock()

    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [
                bazi_tool_message,
                retrieval_tool_message,
            ],
            "structured_response": (
                BaziInterpretation(
                    answer="成功获取解释",
                    used_chunk_ids=["c1"],
                )
            ),
        }
    )

    _patch_runtime_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        mock_agent,
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(
        Mock(),
        Mock(),
    )

    result = await runtime.run(ready_input)

    assert result.answer == "成功获取解释"
    assert result.chart is real_chart

    assert len(result.citations) == 1

    assert result.citations[0].chunk_id == "c1"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_success_with_calculation_only(
    ready_input,
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    用户只询问确定性排盘事实时，
    Retrieval Tool 可以合法地完全不调用。
    """
    real_chart = _create_chart(ready_input)

    bazi_tool_message = ToolMessage(
        content="{}",
        name="calculate_bazi",
        tool_call_id="1",
        artifact=real_chart,
    )

    mock_agent = Mock()

    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [bazi_tool_message],
            "structured_response": (
                BaziInterpretation(
                    answer=("你的日主为当前排盘计算结果所示日主。"),
                    used_chunk_ids=[],
                )
            ),
        }
    )

    _patch_runtime_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        mock_agent,
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(
        Mock(),
        Mock(),
    )

    result = await runtime.run(ready_input)

    assert result.chart is real_chart

    assert result.answer == "你的日主为当前排盘计算结果所示日主。"

    assert result.citations == ()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_calculation_only_cannot_fabricate_citation(
    ready_input,
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    未调用 Retrieval 时，
    structured response 不能凭空声明知识证据。
    """
    real_chart = _create_chart(ready_input)

    bazi_tool_message = ToolMessage(
        content="{}",
        name="calculate_bazi",
        tool_call_id="1",
        artifact=real_chart,
    )

    mock_agent = Mock()

    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [bazi_tool_message],
            "structured_response": (
                BaziInterpretation(
                    answer="带有伪造出处的解释。",
                    used_chunk_ids=["fabricated-chunk"],
                )
            ),
        }
    )

    _patch_runtime_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        mock_agent,
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(
        Mock(),
        Mock(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        BaziAgentCitationError,
        match="未授权的证据编号",
    ):
        await runtime.run(ready_input)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_fails_on_zero_calculation_calls(
    ready_input,
    monkeypatch,
):
    mock_agent = Mock()

    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [],
            "structured_response": (
                BaziInterpretation(
                    answer="没有经过排盘的回答。",
                    used_chunk_ids=[],
                )
            ),
        }
    )

    _patch_runtime_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        mock_agent,
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(
        Mock(),
        Mock(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        BaziAgentInvariantError,
        match="必须只调用 1 次",
    ):
        await runtime.run(ready_input)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_runtime_fails_when_retrieval_has_no_valid_artifact(
    ready_input,
    monkeypatch,
):
    real_chart = _create_chart(ready_input)

    bazi_tool_message = ToolMessage(
        content="{}",
        name="calculate_bazi",
        tool_call_id="1",
        artifact=real_chart,
    )

    malformed_retrieval_message = ToolMessage(
        content="txt",
        name="retrieve_bazi_knowledge",
        tool_call_id="2",
        artifact=None,
    )

    mock_agent = Mock()

    mock_agent.ainvoke = AsyncMock(
        return_value={
            "messages": [
                bazi_tool_message,
                malformed_retrieval_message,
            ],
            "structured_response": (
                BaziInterpretation(
                    answer="解释。",
                    used_chunk_ids=[],
                )
            ),
        }
    )

    _patch_runtime_dependencies(
        monkeypatch,
        mock_agent,
    )

    runtime = BaziAgentRuntime(
        Mock(),
        Mock(),
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        BaziAgentInvariantError,
        match=("未返回合法的 BaziKnowledgeArtifact"),
    ):
        await runtime.run(ready_input)

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,
    BaziAgentResult,
    BaziBirthDataPatch,
    InvalidBaziInput,
    NeedUserInput,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.session import BaziTaskSession
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import Gender

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


@pytest.fixture
def mock_runtime():
    runtime = Mock()
    runtime.run = AsyncMock(return_value=Mock(spec=BaziAgentResult))
    return runtime


async def test_one_turn_completion(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21), mock_runtime)
    assert isinstance(await session.evaluate(), NeedUserInput)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 0

    res = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert isinstance(res, BaziAgentResult)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1


async def test_multi_turn_completion_and_question_preservation(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="感情怎样", year=1998), mock_runtime)
    res2 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(month=7, day=21))
    assert isinstance(res2, NeedUserInput)

    res3 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert isinstance(res3, BaziAgentResult)

    ready = mock_runtime.run.call_args.args[0]
    assert ready.question == "感情怎样"
    assert ready.profile.year == 1998


async def test_field_override_correction(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=13), mock_runtime)
    await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_args.args[0].profile.hour == 14


async def test_invalid_patch_protects_state(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7), mock_runtime)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(month=17))
    assert session._current_input.month == 7


async def test_invalid_date_combination_recovery(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="算卦", year=2023, month=2), mock_runtime)
    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(day=29)), InvalidBaziInput)
    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(year=2024)), NeedUserInput)


async def test_completed_session_does_not_run_runtime_again(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21), mock_runtime)
    result1 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))

    result2 = await session.evaluate()
    assert result2 is result1
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1

    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="已完成"):
        await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=15))

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.models import (
    BaziAgentInput,
    BaziAgentResult,
    BaziBirthDataPatch,
    MissingField,
    NeedUserInput,
)
from guanchen_v2.agents.bazi.session import BaziTaskSession
from guanchen_v2.models.bazi import Gender

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


@pytest.fixture
def mock_runtime():
    runtime = Mock()
    runtime.run = AsyncMock(return_value=Mock(spec=BaziAgentResult))
    return runtime


async def test_A01_complete_data_ready_for_bazi(mock_runtime):
    initial = BaziAgentInput(question="分析事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE)
    session = BaziTaskSession(initial, mock_runtime)
    result = await session.evaluate()
    assert isinstance(result, BaziAgentResult)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1


async def test_A02_missing_time(mock_runtime):
    initial = BaziAgentInput(question="看看事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21, gender=Gender.MALE)
    session = BaziTaskSession(initial, mock_runtime)
    result = await session.evaluate()
    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)
    assert result.missing_fields == [MissingField.HOUR, MissingField.MINUTE]


async def test_A03_missing_gender(mock_runtime):
    initial = BaziAgentInput(question="事业", year=1998, month=7, day=21, hour=14, minute=30)
    session = BaziTaskSession(initial, mock_runtime)
    result = await session.evaluate()
    assert isinstance(result, NeedUserInput)


async def test_A04_multi_turn_repeated_supplement(mock_runtime):
    session = BaziTaskSession(BaziAgentInput(question="感情", year=1998), mock_runtime)
    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(month=7, day=21)), NeedUserInput)
    assert isinstance(await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(hour=14)), NeedUserInput)
    r4 = await session.supplement(BaziBirthDataPatch(minute=30, gender=Gender.MALE))
    assert isinstance(r4, BaziAgentResult)
    assert mock_runtime.run.call_count == 1

FILE: tests\agents\knowledge\test_agent_orchestration.py

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.agents.knowledge.agent import KnowledgeAgent
from guanchen_v2.agents.knowledge.models import KnowledgeAnswerDraft
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import (
    KnowledgeAgentError,
    KnowledgeRetrievalError,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import (
    KnowledgeDomain,
    RetrievalFilter,
    RetrievalResult,
    SourceType,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.dense import DenseRetriever
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.service import RetrievalService
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.retrieval.sparse import SparseRetriever
from guanchen_v2.llm.client import LLMClient


def _create_result(
    chunk_id: str,
    rank: int,
) -> RetrievalResult:
    return RetrievalResult(
        rank=rank,
        chunk_id=chunk_id,
        document_id="doc1",
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="T",
        source_type=SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE,
        section="S",
        text="TXT",
        rrf_score=0.9,
        rrf_rank=rank,
        reranker_score=None,
        dense_rank=1,
        sparse_rank=2,
    )


def _create_llm_mock() -> MagicMock:
    mock_llm = MagicMock(spec_set=LLMClient)
    mock_llm.generate_structured = AsyncMock()
    return mock_llm


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retrieval_service_propagates_filters():
    mock_dense = MagicMock(spec=DenseRetriever)
    mock_sparse = MagicMock(spec=SparseRetriever)

    mock_dense.search = AsyncMock(return_value=[])
    mock_sparse.search = AsyncMock(return_value=[])

    mock_rrf = MagicMock()
    mock_reranker = MagicMock()
    mock_assembler = MagicMock()

    mock_rrf.fuse.return_value = []

    service = RetrievalService(
        mock_dense,
        mock_sparse,
        mock_rrf,
        mock_reranker,
        mock_assembler,
        candidate_limit=5,
        result_limit=2,
    )

    filters = RetrievalFilter(
        domains=frozenset(
            {
                KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
            }
        )
    )

    await service.retrieve(
        "Query",
        filters=filters,
    )

    mock_dense.search.assert_called_once_with(
        "Query",
        limit=5,
        filters=filters,
    )

    mock_sparse.search.assert_called_once_with(
        "Query",
        limit=5,
        filters=filters,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retrieval_service_limits_safeguards():
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeRetrievalError):
        RetrievalService(
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            MagicMock(),
            candidate_limit=2,
            result_limit=5,
        )

    service = RetrievalService(
        MagicMock(),
        MagicMock(),
        MagicMock(),
        MagicMock(),
        MagicMock(),
        5,
        2,
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        KnowledgeRetrievalError,
        match="不能为空",
    ):
        await service.retrieve("   ")


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_empty_evidence_bypasses_llm_and_single_call():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)
    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(return_value=[])

    mock_llm = _create_llm_mock()

    agent = KnowledgeAgent(
        mock_service,
        mock_llm,
    )

    response = await agent.answer("什么是八字？")

    assert "无法据此作答" in response.answer
    assert response.evidence_sufficient is False
    assert response.citations == ()

    mock_service.retrieve.assert_awaited_once_with(
        query="什么是八字？",
        filters=None,
    )

    mock_llm.generate_structured.assert_not_awaited()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_uses_canonical_structured_llm_contract():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)
    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(
        return_value=[
            _create_result(
                "合法_1",
                1,
            )
        ]
    )

    mock_llm = _create_llm_mock()
    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
        answer="基于证据作答。",
        evidence_sufficient=True,
        used_chunk_ids=["合法_1"],
    )

    agent = KnowledgeAgent(
        mock_service,
        mock_llm,
    )

    await agent.answer("查询")

    mock_llm.generate_structured.assert_awaited_once()

    kwargs = mock_llm.generate_structured.await_args.kwargs

    assert set(kwargs) == {
        "system_prompt",
        "user_prompt",
        "schema",
    }

    assert kwargs["schema"] is KnowledgeAnswerDraft

    assert "GuanChen Knowledge Agent" in kwargs["system_prompt"]

    assert "=== EVIDENCE START ===" in kwargs["user_prompt"]

    assert "问题：查询" in kwargs["user_prompt"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_zero_citations_discards_llm_answer():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)

    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(
        return_value=[
            _create_result(
                "合法",
                1,
            )
        ]
    )

    mock_llm = _create_llm_mock()

    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
        answer="没有引用证据的回答。",
        evidence_sufficient=False,
        used_chunk_ids=[],
    )

    response = await KnowledgeAgent(
        mock_service,
        mock_llm,
    ).answer("查")

    assert response.answer == "当前知识库中没有检索到足够依据，无法据此作答。"

    assert response.evidence_sufficient is False
    assert response.citations == ()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_insufficient_evidence_enforces_stamp():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)

    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(
        return_value=[
            _create_result(
                "合法_1",
                1,
            )
        ]
    )

    mock_llm = _create_llm_mock()

    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
        answer=("这里包含部分证据支持的内容。"),
        evidence_sufficient=False,
        used_chunk_ids=["合法_1"],
    )

    response = await KnowledgeAgent(
        mock_service,
        mock_llm,
    ).answer("查")

    assert response.answer.startswith("现有检索证据不足以完整回答该问题。\n\n")

    assert "这里包含部分证据支持的内容。" in (response.answer)

    assert len(response.citations) == 1
    assert response.citations[0].chunk_id == "合法_1"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_agent_llm_fabrication_fails_fast():
    mock_service = MagicMock(spec=RetrievalService)

    mock_service.retrieve = AsyncMock(
        return_value=[
            _create_result(
                "合法_1",
                1,
            )
        ]
    )

    mock_llm = _create_llm_mock()

    mock_llm.generate_structured.return_value = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
        answer="存在伪造证据编号。",
        evidence_sufficient=True,
        used_chunk_ids=[
            "合法_1",
            "虚构的_999",
        ],
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        KnowledgeAgentError,
        match="未授权的证据编号",
    ):
        await KnowledgeAgent(
            mock_service,
            mock_llm,
        ).answer("查询")


def test_knowledge_answer_draft_pydantic_invariants():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValidationError,
        match="必须至少提供一个实际引用的 chunk_id",
    ):
        KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
            answer="回答",
            evidence_sufficient=True,
            used_chunk_ids=[],
        )

    draft = KnowledgeAnswerDraft(
        answer="证据不足。",
        evidence_sufficient=False,
        used_chunk_ids=[],
    )

    assert draft.evidence_sufficient is False


================================================================================
FILE: tests\core\session\test_session.py

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from guanchen_v2.core.session.enums import SessionStatus
from guanchen_v2.core.session.models import (
    AgentStateContainer,
    ConversationState,
    MessageState,
    PendingInterrupt,
    SessionMetadata,
)
from guanchen_v2.router.contracts import IntentType, RequiredDataType, RoutingResult
from guanchen_v2.router.requirements import RequirementCheckResult, RequirementStatus


def test_1_create_session():
    state = ConversationState(session_id="session-xxx")
    assert state.session_id == "session-xxx"
    assert state.status == SessionStatus.ACTIVE
    assert state.metadata.created_at.tzinfo == timezone.utc

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        ConversationState(session_id=None)

def test_2_serialization_round_trip():
    state = ConversationState(
        session_id="session-123",
        status=SessionStatus.WAITING_INPUT,
        messages=[
            MessageState(
                role="user", 
                content="帮我分析八字", 
                timestamp=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 10, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            )
        ],
        current_query="帮我分析八字",
        metadata=SessionMetadata(
            trace_id="trace-001",
            extra={"runtime_flag": True}
        )
    )

    json_data = state.model_dump_json()
    restored_state = ConversationState.model_validate_json(json_data)

    assert restored_state.session_id == "session-123"
    assert restored_state.status == SessionStatus.WAITING_INPUT
    assert len(restored_state.messages) == 1
    assert restored_state.messages[0].timestamp.tzinfo == timezone.utc
    assert restored_state.metadata.extra["runtime_flag"] is True

def test_3_agent_namespace_isolation():
    """
    Test 3: 验证结构级的 Namespace 隔离。
    ADR 记录：Application层不再审查 Agent 内部字典的键值对。
    """
    state = ConversationState(
        session_id="test",
        agent_states=AgentStateContainer(
            dream={"record": {"id": 1}},
            liuyao={"step": "cast"}
        )
    )
    assert state.agent_states.dream["record"]["id"] == 1
    
    # 验证 Pydantic ConfigDict 拦截野生高层级命名空间
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        ConversationState(
            session_id="test",
            agent_states={"unknown_agent": {}}
        )

def test_4_reject_runtime_object():
    class ChatModel:
        pass

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="禁止包含 Runtime Object"):
        ConversationState(
            session_id="test",
            agent_states=AgentStateContainer(
                bazi={"client": ChatModel()}
            )
        )

def test_5_pending_interrupt_recovery():
    interrupt = PendingInterrupt(
        agent="liuyao",
        interrupt_type="confirmation_required",
        payload={"question": "是否确认起卦"},
        created_at=datetime(2026, 1, 1, 12, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )
    state = ConversationState(session_id="test", pending_interrupt=interrupt)
    
    json_str = state.model_dump_json()
    restored = ConversationState.model_validate_json(json_str)
    
    assert restored.pending_interrupt.interrupt_type == "confirmation_required"
    assert restored.pending_interrupt.payload["question"] == "是否确认起卦"
    assert restored.pending_interrupt.created_at.tzinfo == timezone.utc

def test_routing_result_round_trip():
    """Test 8: RoutingResult / RequirementResult serialization"""
    state = ConversationState(
        session_id="test-routing",
        routing_result=RoutingResult(
            primary_intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            secondary_intents=(),
            confidence=0.95,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
            reason="显式请求八字排盘"
        ),
        requirement_result=RequirementCheckResult(
            intent=IntentType.BAZI,
            status=RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT,
            required_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,),
            missing_data=(RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,)
        )
    )

    json_str = state.model_dump_json()
    restored = ConversationState.model_validate_json(json_str)

    assert restored.routing_result is not None
    assert restored.routing_result.primary_intent == IntentType.BAZI
    assert restored.routing_result.required_data == (RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,)

    assert restored.requirement_result is not None
    assert restored.requirement_result.status == RequirementStatus.NEEDS_INPUT
    assert restored.requirement_result.missing_data == (RequiredDataType.BIRTH_PROFILE,)



================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\ingestion\test_chunker.py
================================================================================

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.chunker import KnowledgeDocumentChunker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.errors import KnowledgeChunkingError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDocument, KnowledgeDomain, SourceType


def _create_doc(title: str, text: str) -> KnowledgeDocument:
    return KnowledgeDocument.from_parser(
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title=title,
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="测试章节",
        text=text,
    )


def test_short_document_remains_one_chunk():
    """C1: 长度未越界的段落，必须 Bypass 保持绝对完整"""
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", "100个字符的正文。")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)
    assert len(chunks) == 1
    assert chunks[0].text == "100个字符的正文。"
    # Metadata 全继承
    assert chunks[0].document_id == doc.document_id
    assert chunks[0].source_title == doc.source_title


def test_long_document_splits_with_metadata_inheritance():
    """C2: 长文档切分，且 Metadata 血缘完整继承"""
    text = "A" * 900
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)
    assert len(chunks) > 1
    for chunk in chunks:
        assert chunk.document_id == doc.document_id
        assert chunk.domain == doc.domain
        assert chunk.section == doc.section


def test_no_cross_document_contamination():
    """C3: 不同 Document 的切片绝不产生交叉感染"""
    doc_a = _create_doc("《书》", "A" * 900)
    doc_b = _create_doc("《测试》", "B" * 900)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    chunks = chunker.chunk_documents([doc_a, doc_b])
    a_chunks = [c for c in chunks if c.source_title == "《书》"]
    b_chunks = [c for c in chunks if c.source_title == "《测试》"]

    for c in a_chunks:
        assert "A" in c.text and "B" not in c.text
    for c in b_chunks:
        assert "B" in c.text and "A" not in c.text


def test_paragraph_aware_preference():
    """C4: 切片偏好验证，必须优先在段落处（双换行）下刀"""
    # 构造两个段落，单段均不到 100，总长超过 100
    text = "第一大段。" + ("A" * 60) + "\n\n第二大段。" + ("B" * 60)
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=100, chunk_overlap=10)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    # 因为在 \n\n 处切了，所以第一个块里绝对不该混入第二大段的内容
    assert "第二大段" not in chunks[0].text
    assert chunks[0].text.startswith("第一大段")


def test_deterministic_chunk_ids():
    """C5: 多次重试，生成的 ID 序列必须是恒定的指纹"""
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", "A" * 900)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    chunks_1 = chunker.chunk_document(doc)
    chunks_2 = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    ids_1 = [c.chunk_id for c in chunks_1]
    ids_2 = [c.chunk_id for c in chunks_2]
    assert ids_1 == ids_2


def test_whitespace_preservation_in_chunk_text():
    """C6: Chunk 储存的内容绝不被 Canonicalization 破坏段落和多余空格"""
    text = "第一段。   \n\n  第二段。"
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunk = chunker.chunk_document(doc)[0]

    assert "\n\n" in chunk.text
    assert "第一段。   \n\n  第二段。" == chunk.text


def test_ditiansui_multi_anchor_grouping():
    """C7: 滴天髓多锚点截断组装。不允许混杂！"""
    text = "【经文】A\n【原注】A解\n【经文】B\n【任氏曰】B解"
    doc = _create_doc("《滴天髓阐微》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    # 长度充裕，应当划分为两块，每块守着自己的锚
    assert len(chunks) == 2
    assert "【经文】A" in chunks[0].text and "【经文】B" not in chunks[0].text
    assert "【经文】B" in chunks[1].text and "【经文】A" not in chunks[1].text


def test_ditiansui_anchor_retained_after_fallback():
    """C8: 极其核心 - 当注解超长时，切断的残肢必须自带经文头部 context"""
    text = "【经文】何知其人富\n【任氏曰】" + ("释义" * 50)
    doc = _create_doc("《滴天髓阐微》", text)
    # 强行切断
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=50, chunk_overlap=5)
    chunks = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    assert len(chunks) > 1
    # 每一个被迫拆分的碎块，开头都必须带回原经文
    for chunk in chunks:
        assert chunk.text.startswith("【经文】何知其人富")
        assert len(chunk.text) > len("【经文】何知其人富")  # 不会产生 verse-only 孤立块


def test_empty_result_impossible():
    """C9: 输入有内容，绝对不能返回空"""
    doc = _create_doc("《书》", "哪怕只有一个字")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    assert len(chunker.chunk_document(doc)) > 0


def test_invalid_config():
    """C10: 防止程序员乱填切分参数"""
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="必须大于 0"):
        KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=-1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="不能为负数"):
        KnowledgeDocumentChunker(chunk_overlap=-1)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="严格小于"):
        KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=100, chunk_overlap=100)


def test_ditiansui_anchor_without_commentary_fails():
    """C11: 发现孤立经文锚点时 Fail Fast，决不静默产生 verse-only chunk"""
    # 只有经文锚点，没有任何后续说明正文
    text = "【经文】此句之后没有任何注解"
    doc = _create_doc("《滴天髓阐微》", text)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)

    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeChunkingError, match="缺失关联释义"):
        chunker.chunk_document(doc)


def test_repeated_split_content_is_stably_deduplicated():
    text = "全新的颠覆性文本二号，旧版全部作废！" * 15

    doc = _create_doc(
        "《书》",
        text,
    )

    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(
        max_chars=50,
        chunk_overlap=5,
    )

    chunks_first = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    chunks_second = chunker.chunk_document(doc)

    first_ids = [chunk.chunk_id for chunk in chunks_first]

    second_ids = [chunk.chunk_id for chunk in chunks_second]

    assert len(chunks_first) > 1

    assert len(first_ids) == len(set(first_ids))

    assert first_ids == second_ids


================================================================================
FILE: tests\knowledge\ingestion\test_ingestion_service.py

from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import select

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.embeddings import BgeEmbeddingAdapter
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.errors import KnowledgeTransactionError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.chunker import KnowledgeDocumentChunker
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.errors import KnowledgeChunkingError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.service import KnowledgeIngestionService
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDocument, KnowledgeDomain, SourceType
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.database import (
    async_session,
    dispose_db,
    get_engine,
    init_db,
)
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.persistence.models import (
    Base,
    KnowledgeChunkRow,
    KnowledgeDocumentRow,
)

pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
async def setup_database():
    await init_db()

    try:
        yield
    finally:
        try:
            async with get_engine().begin() as conn:
                await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.drop_all)
        finally:
            await dispose_db()


def _create_mock_adapter():
    adapter = MagicMock(spec=BgeEmbeddingAdapter)

    adapter.model_name = BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME

    adapter.embed_documents.side_effect = lambda chunks: [[0.5] * 512 for _ in chunks]

    return adapter


def _create_test_doc(text: str) -> KnowledgeDocument:
    return KnowledgeDocument.from_parser(
        domain=KnowledgeDomain.BAZI,
        source_title="《渊海子平》",
        source_type=SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC,
        section="卷一 / 测试",
        text=text,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_empty_chunks_fails_fast():
    """S0 异常防线：不能悄无声息地跳过未产生 Chunk 的残破文档。"""
    chunker = MagicMock()
    chunker.chunk_document.return_value = []
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    doc = _create_test_doc("将被 Mock 掉的文本")
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeChunkingError, match="未产生任何 Chunk"):
        await service.ingest_document(doc)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_success_happy_path():
    """S1 首次完美摄入验证。确认 Document 和衍生 Chunk 被无损保存。"""
    doc = _create_test_doc("一段非常普通的初版测试文字，用于基础查验。")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    await service.ingest_document(doc)

    async with async_session() as session:
        res_doc = await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow).where(KnowledgeDocumentRow.document_id == doc.document_id))
        assert res_doc.scalar_one().text == "一段非常普通的初版测试文字，用于基础查验。"

        res_chunks = await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow).where(KnowledgeChunkRow.document_id == doc.document_id))
        saved_chunks = res_chunks.scalars().all()
        assert len(saved_chunks) == 1
        assert len(saved_chunks[0].embedding) == 512
        assert saved_chunks[0].embedding_model == BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_identical_repeat_no_extra_rows():
    """S2 完全重复输入测试。必须维持稳定的一行记录和原样 Chunk，严禁行数膨胀！"""
    doc = _create_test_doc("不会变化的千古真理。")
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    await service.ingest_document(doc)
    await service.ingest_document(doc)

    async with async_session() as session:
        docs = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow))).scalars().all()
        assert len(docs) == 1
        chunks = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        assert len(chunks) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_text_update_replaces_chunks():
    """S3 高级幂等：旧历史必须全被物理除名，绝不留痕。"""
    # 故意设积极短的切割长度，迫使长文本产生大量的碎片 (Chunk)
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=50, chunk_overlap=5)
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, _create_mock_adapter())

    # 模拟初版入库产生了多个切片
    doc_v1 = _create_test_doc("旧版文本一。" * 20)
    await service.ingest_document(doc_v1)

    async with async_session() as session:
        chunks_v1 = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        old_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in chunks_v1}
        assert len(old_chunk_ids) > 1

    # 同一个 document_id，但文案全部更新
    doc_v2 = _create_test_doc("全新的颠覆性文本二号，旧版全部作废！" * 15)
    await service.ingest_document(doc_v2)

    async with async_session() as session:
        docs = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow))).scalars().all()
        assert len(docs) == 1
        assert "全新的颠覆性文本二号" in docs[0].text

        chunks_v2 = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        new_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in chunks_v2}

        # 绝大部分旧 ID 必须全部被物理删除，绝不能出现新老交织的混合态
        assert len(new_chunk_ids) > 1
        assert not old_chunk_ids.intersection(new_chunk_ids)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_idempotent_ingestion_rollback_on_partial_failure():
    """S4 终极战役：基于 PostgreSQL 底层报错触发的物理级大回滚。"""
    chunker = KnowledgeDocumentChunker(max_chars=800, chunk_overlap=80)
    adapter = MagicMock(spec=BgeEmbeddingAdapter)
    adapter.model_name = BgeEmbeddingAdapter.MODEL_NAME

    # 设局：只要碰到含有特定词汇的 Chunk，就向数据库返回 PostgreSQL pgvector 无法忍受的 768 维瞎编向量！
    def fake_embed(chunks):
        if "造反" in chunks[0].text:
            return [[0.5] * 768 for _ in chunks]
        return [[0.5] * 512 for _ in chunks]

    adapter.embed_documents.side_effect = fake_embed
    service = KnowledgeIngestionService(chunker, adapter)

    # Phase A：初版入库
    doc_v1 = _create_test_doc("绝不容篡改的正确初版。")
    await service.ingest_document(doc_v1)

    async with async_session() as session:
        old_chunks = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow))).scalars().all()
        old_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in old_chunks}

    # Phase B：模拟灾难现场
    # 此时：1. UPSERT 已完成; 2. DELETE 旧 Chunk 已完成; 3. INSERT 768维新 Chunk 惨遭 PostgreSQL 拒绝！
    doc_v2 = _create_test_doc("这版文本很正常，但我要在数据写入环节造反。")
    with pytest.raises(KnowledgeTransactionError, match="知识文档幂等替换失败"):
        await service.ingest_document(doc_v2)

    # Phase C：探查战后现场（验证数据库原子性保障）
    async with async_session() as session:
        # Document 回退如初
        saved_doc = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeDocumentRow).where(KnowledgeDocumentRow.document_id == doc_v1.document_id))).scalar_one()
        assert saved_doc.text == "绝不容篡改的正确初版。"

        # 那些在事务中曾被 DELETE 的 Chunk 又满血复活了！
        saved_chunks = (await session.execute(select(KnowledgeChunkRow).where(KnowledgeChunkRow.document_id == doc_v1.document_id))).scalars().all()
        saved_chunk_ids = {c.chunk_id for c in saved_chunks}
        assert saved_chunk_ids == old_chunk_ids

import pytest

from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.errors import KnowledgeSourceLoadError
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.ingestion.loader import KnowledgeSourceLoader
from guanchen_v2.knowledge.models import KnowledgeDomain, SourceType


@pytest.fixture
def temp_raw_root(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / "raw_verified"
    root.mkdir()
    return root


def test_loader_happy_path(temp_raw_root):
    bazi_dir = temp_raw_root / "bazi"
    bazi_dir.mkdir()

    sample = bazi_dir / "sample.md"

    original_text = "# 《测试经典》\n\n## 第一章\n正文  \t \n\n"

    sample.write_text(
        original_text,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(temp_raw_root)
    sources = loader.load_all()

    assert len(sources) == 1

    source = sources[0]

    assert source.domain == KnowledgeDomain.BAZI
    assert source.source_title == "《测试经典》"
    assert source.source_type == SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC
    assert source.source_path == "bazi/sample.md"
    assert source.text == original_text


def test_dream_domain_is_supported(temp_raw_root):
    dream_dir = temp_raw_root / "dream"
    dream_dir.mkdir()

    sample = dream_dir / "zhougong_jiemeng.md"

    sample.write_text(
        "# 《周公解梦》\n\n## 天地日月星辰\n日月初出家道昌。",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(temp_raw_root)

    sources = loader.load_all()

    assert len(sources) == 1

    source = sources[0]

    assert source.domain == KnowledgeDomain.DREAM
    assert source.source_title == "《周公解梦》"
    assert source.source_type == SourceType.PUBLIC_DOMAIN_CLASSIC
    assert source.source_path == "dream/zhougong_jiemeng.md"


def test_project_note_resolution(temp_raw_root):
    bazi_dir = temp_raw_root / "bazi"
    bazi_dir.mkdir()

    sample = bazi_dir / "guanchen_bazi_terms.md"

    sample.write_text(
        "# 术语",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(temp_raw_root)
    source = loader.load_all()[0]

    assert source.source_type == SourceType.PROJECT_NOTE


def test_source_path_cross_platform_contract(temp_raw_root):
    bazi_dir = temp_raw_root / "bazi"
    bazi_dir.mkdir()

    sample = bazi_dir / "test.md"

    sample.write_text(
        "# 标题",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(temp_raw_root)
    source = loader.load_file(sample)

    assert "\\" not in source.source_path
    assert "/" in source.source_path

def test_invalid_utf8_translation(temp_raw_root):
    bazi_dir = temp_raw_root / "bazi"
    bazi_dir.mkdir()

    sample = bazi_dir / "gbk.md"

    sample.write_bytes("这是一个古籍测试".encode("gbk"))

    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(temp_raw_root)

        exc_info.value.__cause__,
        UnicodeDecodeError,
    )


def test_only_md_files_discovered(temp_raw_root):
    bazi_dir = temp_raw_root / "bazi"
    bazi_dir.mkdir()

    (bazi_dir / "a.md").write_text(
        "# A",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    (bazi_dir / "b.txt").write_text(
        "# B",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    (bazi_dir / "c.pdf").write_text(
        "PDF",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(temp_raw_root)

    sources = loader.load_all()

    assert len(sources) == 1
    assert sources[0].source_path == "bazi/a.md"

        match="发现未知领域目录",
    ):
        loader.load_all()


def test_path_outside_root_fails(
    temp_raw_root,
    tmp_path,
):
    loader = KnowledgeSourceLoader(temp_raw_root)

    external_dir = tmp_path / "external_bazi"
    external_dir.mkdir()

    external_file = external_dir / "external.md"

    external_file.write_text(
        "# 《外部文件》",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        KnowledgeSourceLoadError,
        match="位于允许的语料目录之外",
    ) as exc_info:
        loader.load_file(external_file)

    assert isinstance(
        exc_info.value.__cause__,
        ValueError,
    )

FILE: tests\knowledge\ingestion\test_normalizer.py
